### BT23-064 — Bakemon

#### Card summary

Catalog record (`packages/shared/src/cards/data/cards.json`): Purple Digimon, Lv.4 Champion, play cost 4,
DP 4000, evo cost `Purple Lv.3: 2`, attributes `Virus`, types `Ghost` / `LIBERATOR`, rarity C.

Printed clauses:

1. `[On Play] By deleting 1 of your Digimon, delete 1 of your opponent's level 4 or lower Digimon.`
2. `[When Digivolving]` — same body as clause 1.
3. Inherited: `[On Deletion] Gain 1 memory.`

#### Rulings

`node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT23-064` returns **no knowledge-base entries**. There are no Q&A ids,
errata, or restrictions to cover for this card. The audit therefore leans on the comprehensive rules:

- §3-4-5-3 — cards in the breeding area can't be affected by effects. The IR target filters carry no
  `zone`, and `candidatePermanents` (`apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/targeting/permanents.ts:110-131`)
  defaults a zone-less permanent filter to `battleArea`, so breeding is already excluded. No IR change needed.

#### Clause -> test -> IR mapping

| Clause | IR | Test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Catalog identity + full coverage | `compiled.coverage`, `compiled.residual` | "matches every catalog field and complete compiled clause" |
| Clause 1 body | `OnPlay` -> `Delete` with `cost.kind: "deleteOwn"` | "plays for 4 and pays one own Digimon to delete an opposing level 4" |
| Clause 1 refusal | `optional: true`, `abortOnDecline: true` | "declining the By cost leaves both boards and memory untouched" |
| Clause 1 level gate | `levelComparison { op: lte, value: 4 }` | "does not pay the cost when the only opposing Digimon is level 5" |
| Clause 1 controller gate | `target.filter.controller: "opponent"` | "never deletes your own Digimon as the effect's target" (same test as the level gate: own Lv.3 survives) |
| Clause 2 body | `WhenDigivolving` -> same action | "digivolves from a purple Lv.3 for 2 and repeats the cost-then-delete" |
| Clause 2 illegal source | printed `evoCosts` | "refuses to digivolve from an illegal source" |
| Clause 1 self-cost | `cost.target.filter.controller: "mine"` includes the just-played Bakemon | "pays its own body when it is your only Digimon, and still deletes the opposing Digimon" |
| Clause 3 | inherited `OnDeletion` -> `GainMemory { amount: 1 }` | "gains 1 memory when a stack carrying it is deleted as the By cost" |
| Clause 3 scope | `isInherited: true` | "does not gain memory when Bakemon itself is the deleted top card" |

Every behavioural test drives the engine through public intents (`playCard`, `digivolve`) — no
`fireTiming` / `advance.fire` injection.

#### Commands and results

```
pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec vitest run src/cards/BT23/BT23-064.test.ts --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism
```

Result: 11 tests passed in 1 file, 6.0s (see `docs/audits/BT23-reaudit/logs/BT23-064-vitest.log`).

```
pnpm --filter @aegis/api typecheck
./node_modules/.bin/oxlint  apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-064.ts apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-064.test.ts
./node_modules/.bin/oxfmt --check apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-064.ts apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-064.test.ts
```

Typecheck: no errors in `BT23-064.ts` or `BT23-064.test.ts`. Pre-existing failures elsewhere
(`src/engine/effects/collect.ts:29`, `src/cards/BT23/BT23-065.test.ts` readonly board specs) belong to
other lanes. Lint clean; format clean after running `oxfmt` on the test file.

#### Defects found

1. **Injected-timing-only proof (fixed).** The previous suite fired `EffectTiming.OnPlay` and
   `EffectTiming.WhenDigivolving` through `fireTiming` / `advance().fire`, and drove the inherited
   clause through `advance().verb.deletePermanent`. None of it proved that a real play or a real
   digivolution reaches the effect, nor that the memory cost is paid. Replaced with public-intent tests.
2. **No cost/level negatives (fixed).** Nothing pinned the `level 4 or lower` gate, the opponent-only
   target scope, or the fact that the By cost is not paid when no legal target exists. Added.
3. **No illegal-source digivolution negative (fixed).** Added a red-source rejection.
4. **`// @ts-nocheck` masked type errors (fixed).** Removed from `BT23-064.ts`; the module typechecks
   without it, so any future bad `duration:` or action shape now surfaces.

Fixture note: the earlier suite stacked `BT23-064` under `BT23-065` Phantomon to prove the inherited
clause. Phantomon has its own `[On Deletion]` that replays a Ghost Digimon from the trash, so that
fixture silently re-played the Bakemon and masked the board endpoint. The stack now uses `BT2-075`
Myotismon (Purple Lv.5, no printed effects, legal Lv.4 Purple route).

No IR change was required: the compiled module already matches the printed text, and the zone-less
filters resolve to the battle area only.

#### Remaining gaps

- The inherited `[On Deletion]` is proven through a deletion caused by Bakemon's own By cost. A
  battle-deletion route would exercise the same producer and was not added.
- No test drives the effect from the opponent's seat, so the memory sign for a seat-1 controller is
  unproven. `GainMemory` reads `ctx.source.ownerSeat`
  (`apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/actions/resources.ts:44-64`), which looks correct by
  inspection only.

#### Worker score

| Column | Score | Reason |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Catalog/rules | 2 | Catalog fields asserted field by field; KB confirmed empty; breeding-zone rule checked against the interpreter. |
| IR trace | 2 | Every printed clause mapped to its IR node, including the `deleteOwn` cost shape and the inherited flag. |
| Behavioral proof | 2 | All clauses driven through public intents with memory, zone and instance-id endpoints, plus refusal and gate negatives. |
| Peer/stack proof | 1 | Digivolution source stack identity and an illegal-source negative are proven; no cross-card interaction beyond the inherited stack was exercised. |
| Delivery gates | 0 | Worker lane; the coordinator owns gates. |

**Total: 7/10.**

#### Accepted score

8/10 — catalog/rules 2/2; IR trace 2/2; behavioral proof 2/2; peer and stack proof 1/2; delivery gates 1/2. Reviewed; evidence; collection gates passed on the working tree (sync/check, typecheck, lint, format, regression 3542/3542); commit and PR outstanding

### BT23-065 — Phantomon

#### Card summary

Catalog record (`packages/shared/src/cards/data/cards.json`): Purple Digimon, Lv.5 Ultimate, Virus,
play cost 7, DP 7000, types `Ghost` / `LIBERATOR`, evolution cost `Purple Lv.4: 3`.

Printed clauses:

- **C1** `[Hand] [Main]` If you have [Violet Inboots], by placing 1 [Bakemon] from your trash as any of
  your [Ghostmon]'s bottom digivolution card, it digivolves into this card for a digivolution cost of 3,
  ignoring digivolution requirements.
- **C2** `[On Deletion]` You may play 1 level 4 or lower Digimon card with the [Ghost] trait from your
  trash without paying the cost.
- **C3** Inherited `[On Deletion]` — same text as C2.

#### Rulings covered

`node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT23-065` returns 2 Q&A.

| Q&A | Content | Covered by |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Q5334 | Combining C1 with BT21-065 Ghostmon's "reduce the digivolution cost by 1" makes the cost 2. | `pays 2 when the Ghostmon host reduces the cost (Q5334)` |
| Q5335 | C1 cannot be activated at the same time as a digivolving effect such as P-108 Wisdom Training. | `refuses activation while another digivolving effect is resolving (Q5335)` |

#### Clause -> test -> IR plan

| Clause | IR | Test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| C1 condition "If you have [Violet Inboots]" | `Main` effect `condition: youHave { zone: "battleArea", nameOrTrait: [{ tokens: ["Violet Inboots"], match: "nameExact" }] }` | positive path; refusal with no Tamer; refusal with the Tamer only in trash |
| C1 cost "placing 1 [Bakemon] from your trash ... bottom digivolution card" | `PlaceUnder` from `trash`, `position: "bottom"`, `bindHostAs: "ghostmonHost"` | stack order assertion `[Bakemon, Ghostmon, Phantomon]`; refusal with an empty trash |
| C1 host "any of your [Ghostmon]" | `PlaceUnder.underFilter` `controller: "mine"`, `nameExact` Ghostmon | refusal when the only board Digimon is a Bakemon; exact-name unit proof |
| C1 "digivolves into this card for a digivolution cost of 3" | `Digivolve` `fromSelectionRef: "ghostmonHost"`, `source: "triggerSource"`, `from: ["hand"]`, `costOverride: 3`, `payCost: true` | memory 5 -> 2 on a non-reducing Ghostmon (BT23-061) |
| C1 "ignoring digivolution requirements" | `ignoreRequirements: true` | Lv.3 Ghostmon -> Lv.5 Phantomon succeeds although the printed route needs a Lv.4 source |
| C1 + BT21-065 reduction (Q5334) | cost pipeline | memory 5 -> 3 on BT21-065 |
| C1 timing (Q5335) | engine gate `GameEngine.applyIntent` `mainActionWhileResolving` (`wrong-phase`) | refusal while another effect is mid-resolution |
| C2 | `OnDeletion` -> `PlayWithoutCost` from `trash`, `payCost: false`, `optional: true`, filter Digimon + level `lte 4` + trait `Ghost` | real combat deletion plays the Lv.4 Ghost for free; the Lv.5 Ghost stays in trash |
| C2 optional | `optional: true` | declined branch leaves the trash untouched |
| C3 | same actions with `isInherited: true` | inherited stack (an inert host over BT23-065) deleted in combat plays the Ghost |

#### Defects found and fixed

1. **Bracketed names used substring matching.** `match: "name"` is a substring test
   (`apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/matching/definition.ts:352`), while `nameExact` is
   equality (line 354). All three references on this card are printed in brackets
   ([Violet Inboots], [Bakemon], [Ghostmon]), so they are exact-name references. Changed all three to
   `nameExact`. No catalog card currently collides on these substrings, so the fix has no behavioral
   delta on today's catalog; the test proves it at the matcher level with synthetic near-name
   definitions ("Bakemon X", "DarkGhostmon", "Violet Inboots Ace"), which fail with `name` and pass
   with `nameExact`.
2. **Zone-less `youHave` also counted the breeding area.** `countMatching`
   (`apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/scaling.ts:22`) scans battle area *and* breeding when the
   filter has no `zone`. Added `zone: "battleArea"`. Violet Inboots is a Tamer and cannot legally sit in
   breeding, so this is a correctness-of-intent fix with no reachable behavioral delta.
3. **`// @ts-nocheck` removed** from the module; it typechecks without the suppression.
4. **Test used an injected timing API.** The old On Deletion test called the private
   `engine.fireTiming(EffectTiming.OnDestroyedAnyone, ...)` through an `as any` cast, which proves
   nothing about how the trigger is reached in play. Replaced with real combat deletions. The old
   `mainEffectKey` helper also reached through `(s.engine as any).cardSourceOf`; it now uses the public
   `observe(engine).cardSource(...)` seam.
5. **Test fixture was wrong about its own negative.** The old deletion test used BT23-066 Matadormon as
   the "too high level" card, but Matadormon is Lv.5 *Undead/CS* — it fails the trait filter as well, so
   it could not isolate the level bound. Replaced with BT20-072 Phantomon (Lv.5, Ghost), which isolates
   `levelComparison lte 4`.

```
pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec vitest run src/cards/BT23/BT23-065.test.ts --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism
-> 1 file, 13 tests, 13 passed, 0 failed (~0.08 s of test time)

pnpm --filter @aegis/api typecheck
-> no error in BT23-065.ts or BT23-065.test.ts
   (pre-existing, other-lane errors remain in src/engine/effects/collect.ts and
    src/engine/combat/controller.ts)

pnpm exec oxlint apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-065.ts apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-065.test.ts   -> exit 0
pnpm exec oxfmt --check <same files>                                                            -> correct format
git diff --check <same files>                                                                   -> clean
```

Log: `docs/audits/BT23-reaudit/logs/BT23-065-final.log`.

Regression sweep over the suites that name BT23-065:

```
vitest run src/cards/BT23/BT23-catalog-sync.test.ts src/engine/deckCardAuditBT23.test.ts \
  src/cards/BT23/BT23-066.test.ts src/cards/BT23/BT23-068.test.ts src/cards/BT23/BT23-071.test.ts
-> 325 tests, 323 passed, 2 failed
```

Both failures are outside this lane:

- `BT23-catalog-sync.test.ts > keeps every record synchronized with its authoritative module` —
  16 card ids mismatch, including BT23-065, because `packages/shared/src/effects/effects.json` still
  holds the pre-audit IR for every module this re-audit has edited. `effects.json` is coordinator-owned,
  so this lane did not touch it. **Coordinator action: re-sync `effects.json` from the modules.**
  Runtime behavior is unaffected — the module registers itself through `registerIrCard`.
- `BT23-068.test.ts > fires once per turn and resets on the next own turn` — another card's suite,
  unrelated to this change.

#### Test inventory

| Test | Proves |
| --- | --- |
| matches every catalog field and complete compiled clause | catalog identity; both printed texts (normalized for the catalog's non-breaking space); `coverage: "full"`, empty residual |
| places Bakemon under Ghostmon, digivolves into itself and pays the printed 3 | full C1 path on BT23-061 (no reduction): stack ends `[Bakemon, Ghostmon, Phantomon]` by instance id, memory 5 -> 2, hand and trash empty, no pending decision, no `actionRejected` event |
| pays 2 when the Ghostmon host reduces the cost (Q5334) | same path on BT21-065: memory 5 -> 3 |
| refuses the hand Main effect without Violet Inboots | `illegal-target`; memory, hand, trash and the host stack all unchanged |
| does not count a Violet Inboots that is only in the trash | the condition is a battle-area count, not a card-anywhere count |
| refuses the hand Main effect with no [Ghostmon] host on the board | the host name gate (board holds a Bakemon instead) |
| refuses the hand Main effect with no [Bakemon] in the trash | the cost gate (trash holds Dracmon instead) |
| refuses activation while another digivolving effect is resolving (Q5335) | `GameEngine.applyIntent`'s `mainActionWhileResolving` gate returns `wrong-phase` for a second activation while the first is parked on its decision |
| matches bracketed card names exactly, not by substring | `matchNameOrTrait` accepts "Bakemon X" / "DarkGhostmon" / "Violet Inboots Ace" under `name` and rejects them under `nameExact`; the compiled IR now uses `nameExact` for all three bracketed names |
| plays a level-4 Ghost from trash for free after a real combat deletion | C2 through a real attack into a 12000-DP suspended blocker: BT4-080 enters the battle area with an empty stack, memory unchanged at 3 against its printed play cost of 5, BT20-072 (Lv.5 Ghost) stays in trash, no pending decision |
| leaves the trash untouched when the optional On Deletion play is declined | the `autoDeclineOptional` branch: trash keeps both cards, battle area empty |
| runs the inherited On Deletion from under a host after a real combat deletion | C3 from beneath an inert host, so the inherited clause is the only trigger that can play the Ghost |
| compiles both On Deletion copies with the printed filter | exactly two `OnDeletion` effects, one `isInherited`, both with `PlayWithoutCost` from trash, `payCost: false`, `optional: true`, level `lte 4`, trait `Ghost` |

#### Remaining gaps

- The `nameExact` fix has no behavioral delta on today's catalog: no printed card name contains
  "Bakemon", "Ghostmon" or "Violet Inboots" as a proper substring. The proof is therefore at the
  matcher level with synthetic definitions, not through a board fixture. It becomes load-bearing the
  moment a card such as a hypothetical "BlackGhostmon" is printed.
- The `zone: "battleArea"` fix likewise has no reachable behavioral delta, because Violet Inboots is a
  Tamer and the breeding area only holds Digimon. It is recorded as intent, not as proven behavior.
- Q5335 is proven through the engine's generic "another effect is resolving" gate, using this card's own
  effect as the resolving effect. A fixture that drives P-108 [Wisdom Training] itself (delay zone ->
  Delay activation -> pending digivolve decision) would be the literal ruling; it was not built because
  the Delay-zone activation route needs its own setup work outside this card's scope.
- The C1 route is only exercised with the Ghostmon host already on the battle area (seeded). A full
  breeding -> promote -> digivolve-to-Ghostmon lead-in is not built.
- No retained red tests and no `it.skip`. No engine seam was found on this card.

#### Catalog discrepancy

None. The catalog text matches the official card text. The only quirk is a non-breaking space in
"[Ghost]\u00a0trait", which the catalog carries deliberately across the set; the test normalizes it
rather than proposing a catalog edit.

| IR trace | 2 | Every action, filter, flag and condition in the compiled IR is asserted, and three IR defects were found and fixed. |
| Behavioral proof | 2 | All three clauses driven through public intents (`activateEffect`, `attack`) with exact endpoints; five negatives; both optional branches; no injected timing API. |
| Peer / stack proof | 2 | BT21-065 reduction (Q5334) vs BT23-061 no-reduction, the BT23-087 Tamer gate, the inherited clause under a host, and a near-name matcher proof. |
| Delivery gates | 0 | Worker lane — the coordinator owns gates. |
| **Total** | **8/10** | |

### BT23-066 — Matadormon

#### Card

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Name | Matadormon |
| Colors | Purple |
| Level / Form | 5 / Ultimate |
| Play cost / DP | 7 / 7000 |
| Evo costs | Purple Lv.4 : 3 |
| Attribute / Types | Virus / Undead, CS |

Printed text:

```
[Digivolve] Lv.4 w/[CS] trait: Cost 3

＜Scapegoat＞
[On Play] [When Digivolving] Delete 1 of your opponent's level 4 or lower Digimon.
Then, if digivolving from the trash, you may play 1 play cost 3 or lower card with
the [Undead] or [CS] trait from your trash without paying the cost.
```

Inherited: `[All Turns] [Once Per Turn] When any of your other Digimon would leave the battle area, by deleting this Digimon, they don't leave.`

#### Printed clauses

1. **C1 — Alternate digivolution route.** Lv.4 with the [CS] trait for cost 3, on top of the printed Purple Lv.4 cost-3 route.
2. **C2 — ＜Scapegoat＞** on the card itself.
3. **C3 — [On Play] / [When Digivolving] delete.** Delete exactly 1 opposing Digimon of level 4 or lower.
4. **C4 — Conditional tail.** Only when this card digivolved *from the trash*: optionally play 1 card of play cost 3 or lower with the [Undead] or [CS] trait from your trash, free.
5. **C5 — Inherited leave-play replacement.** [All Turns] [Once Per Turn], when another of your Digimon would leave the battle area, delete this Digimon instead so they stay.

#### Rulings coverage

`node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT23-066` → **no knowledge-base entries**. the Knowledge base index section of this document line 72 agrees (`BT23-066 | 0 | None`). No Q&A ids to cover. Mechanics were checked against comprehensive rules §16-32 (＜Scapegoat＞), already covered by
`apps/api/src/engine/conformance/ch16c-deletion-and-advanced-keywords.test.ts`.

#### Plan (clause -> test -> IR)

| Clause | IR | Planned test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| C1 | `digivolutionRequirement: [{level:4, traits:["CS"], cost:3, isAlternate:true}]` | Public `digivolve` intent from an off-color Lv.4 [CS] base (BT23-050 Ankylomon, Black/Yellow) — assert `{ok:true}`, memory -3, stack identity. Printed route from a Purple Lv.4 non-CS base (BT23-064 Bakemon) also -3. Near-miss negative: BT23-052 Consulmon (Black Lv.4, no [CS]) -> `invalid-evolution`. |
| C2 | `Static` effect `keywords: [Scapegoat]` | Battle deletion of Matadormon while another own Digimon is on board — accept branch keeps Matadormon and trashes the sacrifice; decline branch deletes Matadormon. |
| C3 | `OnPlay`/`WhenDigivolving` `Delete` with `levelComparison lte 4`, `controller: opponent` | Real `playCard` intent (cost 7) and real `digivolve` intent, each with an opposing Lv.4 and an opposing Lv.5 on board — only the Lv.4 goes to trash. Zone boundary: an opponent breeding Digimon is not a legal target. |
| C4 | `PlayWithoutCost` with `condition {kind:"digivolvedFromZone", zone:"trash"}`, `from:["trash"]`, `playCostLte: 3`, trait tokens `["Undead","CS"]` | Negative: played from hand and digivolved from hand leave the eligible trash card in the trash. Positive: digivolve from trash through BT23-063 Sangloupmon's public [When Attacking] route (real `attack` intent), then the cost-3 [Undead]/[CS] card enters play free while a cost-4 [CS] card and a non-[Undead]/[CS] card stay in the trash. |
| C5 | `AllTurns` `isInherited`, `frequency: OncePerTurn`, `Replacement wouldLeavePlay` + `Prevent leavePlay` with `deleteOwn` self cost | Build the stack with a real `digivolve` (BT3-089 Boltmon, Purple Lv.6, no printed effect, onto Matadormon), then let another own Digimon lose a battle: accept branch keeps the ally and trashes the carrier; decline branch deletes the ally and keeps the carrier. |

#### Clause -> test -> IR (as built)

| Clause | Test | IR proved |
| --- | --- | --- |
| C1 | `digivolves for 3 off an off-color level-4 [CS] base and for 3 off the printed Purple level-4 route` | `digivolutionRequirement[0]` (alternate, cost 3) and the catalog `evoCosts` route. Both endpoints pinned: memory 5 -> 2, top card `BT23-066`, stack holds exactly the base card instance. |
| C1 negative | `rejects an off-color level-4 base without the [CS] trait` | Near-miss base BT23-052 Consulmon (Black, Lv.4, `Saving` only) -> `{ok:false, reason:"invalid-evolution"}`, memory unchanged, base untouched. |
| C3 | `deletes exactly one opposing level 4 or lower Digimon on play and leaves the trash untouched` | `OnPlay` `Delete` `levelComparison lte 4`. Real `playCard` (cost 7, memory 8 -> 1). The Lv.4 card instance is in the opponent's trash; the Lv.5 permanent is the only survivor. |
| C3 zone boundary | `does not reach an opponent's breeding-area Digimon` | Filter has no `zone`, and `targeting/permanents.ts` defaults a zone-less filter to `battleArea` only. Opponent's breeding Lv.4 survives, opponent trash empty. |
| C3 + C4 negative | `deletes on digivolving from the hand but still skips the trash tail` | `WhenDigivolving` `Delete` fires; `condition {digivolvedFromZone: trash}` blocks the tail — the eligible cost-3 [Undead]/[CS] card is still the only card in the trash. |
| C4 positive | `plays a free cost-3 [Undead]/[CS] card when it digivolves out of the trash` | `PlayWithoutCost` `from:["trash"]`, `payCost:false`, `playCostLte:3`, trait tokens `["Undead","CS"]`. Public route: real `attack` intent -> BT23-063 Sangloupmon's [When Attacking] trash digivolution. Endpoints: top card is Matadormon, stack is exactly the Sangloupmon instance, memory 5 -> 2 (route cost 3, tail free), Lv.4 opponent deleted, Lv.5 opponent alive, BT23-062 on the battle area, BT23-063 (cost 4 [CS]) and BT23-064 (cost 4, wrong traits) still in the trash. |
| C4 boundary | `plays nothing when the trash holds no eligible card after a trash digivolution` | Delete still resolves; no play, no pending decision, battle area stays at one permanent. |
| C2 | `survives a lost battle by sacrificing another of your Digimon, and dies when the sacrifice is declined` | `Static` `keywords: [Scapegoat]`, resolved through `combat/controller.ts` §16-32. Accept branch: Matadormon is the only permanent left and the sacrifice card is the only card in the trash. Refuse branch: the reverse. |
| C5 | `as a digivolution card, deletes its carrier so another of your Digimon does not leave play` | `AllTurns` `isInherited` `Replacement wouldLeavePlay` + `Prevent leavePlay` with the `deleteOwn` self cost. The stack is built by a real `digivolve` (BT3-089 Boltmon, Purple Lv.6, no printed effect, memory 5 -> 3, stack identity asserted), then the 3000 DP ally loses a battle: the ally stays, and both carrier cards go to the trash. |
| C5 refusal | `lets the ally leave play when the carrier's sacrifice is declined` | `optional: true, abortOnDecline: true`. Ally card in the trash, carrier permanent alive. |
| all | `compiles ＜Scapegoat＞, both delete triggers, the trash-gated tail and the inherited replacement` | IR shape backing every behavioral test above. |

#### Defects found and fixed

1. **`// @ts-nocheck` removed from `BT23-066.ts`.** The module typechecks without it, so IR type errors (for example a bad `duration:` string) will now surface instead of being silenced. No other change to the module — the compiled IR was already correct on every clause tested.
2. **The old test suite proved almost nothing behaviorally.** Three of its four "behavioral" tests drove injected timing (`engine.fireTiming`, `advance().fire`, `advance().verb.deletePermanent`) instead of public intents, and the rest asserted IR shape. All are replaced by public-intent tests: `playCard`, `digivolve`, `attack`.
3. **The old evolution test's negative was vacuous.** It used BT1-025 WarGreymon (Red, Lv.6) — rejected for level and colour before the [CS] trait is ever consulted. Replaced with BT23-052 Consulmon (Black, Lv.4, no [CS]), which isolates the trait gate.
4. **The old evolution test asserted `{ok:true}` with no cost.** Both routes now assert the exact memory delta (3) and the resulting stack identity.

No engine defect found. Nothing retained red; there are no `it.fails` / `it.skip` entries.

#### Notes for the coordinator

- **No catalog discrepancy.** `cards.json` matches the official text for BT23-066.
- **＜Scapegoat＞ refuses through an empty card selection, not a yes/no prompt.** `combat/controller.ts:1404` calls `selectOptionalInstance`, so `autoSelectCards` always accepts the sacrifice and `autoDeclineOptional` never sees a decision. The refusal test answers that one `selectCards` decision by hand. Worth adding an `autoRefuseOptionalSelection` flag to the harness if other Scapegoat/Fortitude cards need the same branch — harness edits are out of this lane.
- **Pre-existing typecheck failures unrelated to this card:** `src/engine/effects/collect.ts:29` (`Cannot find name 'Seat'`) and `src/cards/BT23/BT23-065.test.ts:65,102,129` (readonly board specs). Neither is in this lane's allowed edits. Note this is a different set from the `combat/controller.ts:617` failure named in the worker brief.

#### Remaining gaps

- **C5's [Once Per Turn] is not behaviorally observable.** The clause's own cost deletes the source Digimon, so a second use in the same turn is impossible by construction and there is nothing to reset on the next own turn. The `frequency: "OncePerTurn"` field is asserted structurally instead. Reporting this rather than faking a turn-loop test that proves nothing.
- **No Q&A coverage exists to check.** `tools/kb/query.mjs card BT23-066` returns no entries and `KB-INDEX.md` records 0.
- **The tail's non-Digimon reach is untested.** The printed wording is "1 play cost 3 or lower *card*", so an [Undead]/[CS] Tamer or Option in the trash should also qualify; the IR carries no `kind` filter, which is correct, but BT23 has no cost-3-or-lower [Undead]/[CS] non-Digimon card to prove it with.

### BT23-067 — LadyDevimon

#### Card

Purple Lv.5 Fallen Angel/CS Digimon. Play cost 7, DP 6000.
Evolution costs: Purple Lv.4 cost 3, Yellow Lv.4 cost 3.

Printed clauses:

1. `[Digivolve] Lv.4 w/[CS] trait: Cost 3` — alternate route.
2. "When this card would be played from the hand, if you have [Angewomon] or [Mirei Mikagura], reduce the play cost by 3."
3. `＜Blocker＞`.
4. `[On Play] [When Digivolving]` Delete 1 of your opponent's level 4 or lower Digimon.
5. Inherited: `＜Scapegoat＞`.

Catalog text matches the official card list. No catalog discrepancy.

#### Q&A covered

`node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT23-067` returns no knowledge-base entries, which matches
the Knowledge base index section of this document (0 Q&A ids). Rules used instead:

| Rule | Use |
| --- | --- |
| comprehensive §2-3-1-2 | A bracket-only card reference matches the printed name exactly. |
| comprehensive §3-4-5-3 / §3-4-5-8 | Cards in a breeding area can neither be affected nor referenced by effects that do not name the breeding area. |
| comprehensive §16-32 | `＜Scapegoat＞`: by deleting 1 of your other Digimon when this Digimon would be deleted other than by your own effects, the deletion is prevented. |
| comprehensive §16-1 | `＜Blocker＞`. |

#### Plan: clause -> test -> IR

| Clause | IR | Test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Alternate `[Digivolve] Lv.4 w/[CS]: Cost 3` | `digivolutionRequirement: [{ level: 4, traits: ["CS"], cost: 3, isAlternate: true }]` | alternate-route digivolve from a red [CS] Lv.4 plus an illegal-source negative |
| Printed purple/yellow Lv.4 routes | `evoCosts` in the catalog | two printed-route digivolve tests |
| Play-cost reduction | `Static` -> nested `Replacement wouldBePlayed` / `reduceCost 3` gated by `youHave` | reduced-cost tests for [Angewomon] and [Mirei Mikagura], full-cost test, and four negative controls |
| `＜Blocker＞` | `Static` with `keywords: [Blocker]`, not inherited | real block in combat; not granted to a carrier |
| `[On Play] [When Digivolving]` delete | `OnPlay` / `WhenDigivolving` -> `Delete` with `controller: "opponent"`, `kind: ["Digimon"]`, `levelComparison lte 4`, `count: 1` | public play and public digivolve, plus level-5 and own-Digimon negatives |
| Inherited `＜Scapegoat＞` | `Static`, `isInherited: true`, `keywords: [Scapegoat]` | carrier saved by sacrificing another Digimon; deleted with no other Digimon |

#### Defects found and fixed

Both are in `apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-067.ts`, both local to the card module.

1. **Substring name matching.** The reduction gate used `match: "name"`, which
   `apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/matching/definition.ts` implements as substring
   matching. The printed reference is bracket-only, so §2-3-1-2 requires the exact printed name.
   With the old IR, "Angewomon (X Antibody)" (BT9-040) wrongly enabled the -3.
   Fix: `match: "nameExact"`. Proved red-then-green by reverting the token: the near-name control
   then ended on memory 6 instead of 3.

2. **Breeding area counted as "you have".** The `youHave` filter carried no zone.
   `countMatching` (`apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/scaling.ts:13`) includes the
   breeding-area permanent when no zone is given, which §3-4-5-8 forbids.
   Fix: `zone: "battleArea"`.
   No behavioral test is attached: neither named card can legally sit in a breeding area
   ([Angewomon] is Lv.5, [Mirei Mikagura] is a Tamer), so no legal fixture can reach the wrong
   branch. The fix is proved at the IR level and by the unchanged battle-area positives.

`// @ts-nocheck` was removed from the module; it typechecks without it.

The `Delete` target needs no zone: permanent targeting already defaults to the battle area
(`apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/targeting/permanents.ts:110-119`), and the "level 5
opponent Digimon only" and "own level 4 Digimon" negatives pin that behavior.

#### Behavioral endpoints proved

- Reduction with own [Angewomon] (BT23-031) in the battle area: memory 10 -> 6 (cost 4),
  LadyDevimon's instance on the battle area, no pending decision.
- Reduction with own [Mirei Mikagura] (BT22-089) Tamer: memory 10 -> 6.
- No qualifier: memory 10 -> 3 (full cost 7).
- Controls, all full cost (memory 3): "Angewomon (X Antibody)" (BT9-040) in the battle area, an
  [Angewomon] in hand, an [Angewomon] in the trash, and the opponent's [Angewomon].
- On Play delete: the opponent's Sangloupmon (BT23-063, Lv.4) instance moves to the opponent's
  trash; GranDracmon (BT23-068, Lv.6) stays; memory 10 -> 3; no pending decision.
- On Play with only a Lv.6 opponent Digimon: nothing leaves either board, no pending decision.
- On Play never touches your own Lv.4 Digimon: the own Sangloupmon survives while the opponent's
  Lv.6 is untouched.
- Evolution routes over the public `digivolve` intent, each also proving the When Digivolving
  delete: BT11-078 (purple Lv.4, no [CS]), BT1-055 (yellow Lv.4, no [CS]) and BT23-008 (red Lv.4
  with [CS], reachable only through the alternate route). Each ends on memory 5 -> 2, the source as
  the only digivolution card, one bonus draw in hand, the opponent's Lv.4 in the opponent's trash,
  the opponent's Lv.6 still on the board, and no pending decision. The alternate route costs the
  same 3 as the printed routes, so `useAlternateCost` cannot be distinguished by memory here; the
  red source proves the route is taken, since no printed route accepts it.
- Illegal source: BT1-046 (yellow Lv.3, no [CS]) is rejected with `invalid-evolution`; the host top
  card and memory are unchanged.
- `＜Blocker＞` in real combat: the opponent attacks the player, LadyDevimon blocks, the 5000-DP
  attacker is deleted, LadyDevimon survives suspended, and the defending player's security stack
  still holds its four cards.
- `＜Blocker＞` is not inherited: a carrier with BT23-067 in its digivolution cards has no Blocker.
- Inherited `＜Scapegoat＞` (§16-32): a suspended 4000-DP carrier with BT23-067 underneath is attacked
  by a 9000-DP Digimon and survives by deleting the other own Digimon, which is the only card in the
  trash. With no other own Digimon, the same battle sends the carrier and its LadyDevimon
  digivolution card to the trash. LadyDevimon itself has no Scapegoat.

#### Remaining gap (engine, not fixed here)

Same latent gap the BT23-031 audit recorded: the printed clause says "would be played **from the
hand**", but the reducer carries no origin-zone condition and
`GameEngine.fireBeforePayCost` does not pass `originZone` into
`wouldBePlayedSelfReducersFor`. The `WouldBePlayedSelfReducer` shape
(`apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/registration/reducers.ts:25`) has no zone field, so the
gate cannot be added inside the card module. No retained red is attached because the gap is
unreachable today: every catalog effect that plays a Digimon from another zone does so without
paying the cost. Recommended engine work: carry `originZone` into self-reducer evaluation and
compile an `originZone: "hand"` gate for "would be played from the hand" text.

### BT23-068 — GranDracmon

#### Card

Purple Lv.6 Mega Digimon, Virus, traits [Dark Animal] / [CS]. Play cost 13, DP 13000.
Printed digivolution cost: Purple Lv.5 for 5.

Printed clauses:

1. `[Digivolve] Lv.5 w/[Undead]/[CS] trait: Cost 4` — alternate route; either trait qualifies.
2. `[Start of Your Main Phase] [On Deletion]` 1 of your Digimon may digivolve into a level 6 or
   lower [Undead] or [Dark Animal] trait Digimon card in the trash without paying the cost.
3. `[When Digivolving]` You may play 1 level 4 or lower purple Digimon card from your trash
   without paying the cost.
4. `[All Turns] [Once Per Turn]` When any of your Digimon digivolve from the trash, delete all of
   your opponent's lowest level Digimon.

Catalog record matches the official text. No discrepancy to report.

#### Rulings covered

| Q&A | Text | Test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Q5336 | The [All Turns] effect also triggers when one of your Digimon digivolves into this card from the trash. | `triggers its own effect when a Digimon digivolves into it from the trash (Q5336)` |

#### Clause -> test -> IR

| Clause | Test | IR proved |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Catalog identity, clause set | `matches every catalog field and complete compiled clause` | catalog record, `coverage`, `residual`, trigger list |
| 1 printed route | `charges the printed Purple Lv.5 route 5 memory` | `evoCosts` |
| 1 alternate route | `charges 4 on the alternate [Undead]/[CS] route and keeps the base under the stack` | `digivolutionRequirement[0]` (`cost: 4`, `isAlternate`) |
| 1 either trait | `takes the alternate route from a Lv.5 with only the [CS] trait` | `traits: ["Undead", "CS"]` read as OR |
| 1 negative | `denies the cost-4 route to a Lv.5 purple base with neither [Undead] nor [CS]` | trait gate (Phantomon still pays 5) |
| 1 illegal source | `rejects an off-colour Lv.5 [Dark Animal] source outright` | colour/level gate |
| 2 accept | `digivolves a Digimon into the trash [Dark Animal] card for free and skips the off-trait card` | `StartOfYourMainPhase` -> `Digivolve` `from:["trash"]` `payCost:false`, `into.nameOrTrait` |
| 2 requirement gate | same test (`unreachable` Lv.5 [Undead] card stays in trash) | requirements are NOT waived by `payCost:false` |
| 2 refusal | `leaves the board untouched when the controller declines` | `optional: true` |
| 2 controller boundary | `does not fire on the opponent's main phase` | trigger is "your" main phase |
| 2 On Deletion | `triggers its own effect when a Digimon digivolves into it from the trash (Q5336)` | `OnDeletion` -> same `Digivolve` action |
| 3 accept | `plays a level 4 or lower purple Digimon from the trash and leaves the Lv.5 behind` | `WhenDigivolving` -> `PlayWithoutCost` `from:["trash"]`, `levelComparison lte 4`, `colors:["Purple"]` |
| 3 refusal | `leaves the trash alone when the controller declines` | `optional: true` |
| 4 delete lowest | Q5336 test and the start-of-main accept test | `SubTrigger` -> `Delete` `superlative:"lowestLevel"`, `count:"all"`, `controller:"opponent"` |
| 4 zone gate | `does not delete when the watched evolution comes from hand` | `fireCondition.digivolvedFromZone = "trash"` |

Endpoints asserted throughout: permanent ids of the surviving opponents, digivolution stack
identity (base instance under, new card on top), memory before and after, hand size for the
digivolution bonus draw, and `pendingDecision === undefined` after each flow settles.

#### Defects found and fixed

1. **`// @ts-nocheck` on the module.** Removed. `apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-068.ts` typechecks
   clean without it, so the pragma only hid future type errors — exactly the failure mode
   REVIEW-NOTES records for BT23-047's silent `duration` mapping. The module has no `duration:`
   field, so nothing was being mis-mapped.
2. **Structural-only coverage.** Four of the eight prior tests asserted the compiled IR shape
   instead of behaviour, and the [When Digivolving] test drove the engine through the injected
   `fireTiming` seam. All are now behavioural, driven by the `digivolve` intent or the real turn
   loop.
3. **Unpinned alternate cost.** The prior evolution test asserted only `{ ok: true }`, which
   proves nothing about cost 4 (the printed Purple Lv.5 route also returns `ok`). Both branches
   now pin memory: 5 without `useAlternateCost`, 4 with it, and 5 for a Lv.5 base that carries
   neither trait.
4. **No once-per-turn proof.** The `[Once Per Turn]` gate and its reset are now proved through
   the real turn loop, with the intermediate same-turn refusal asserted explicitly.

No card-module logic changes were needed. The IR was already correct on every clause tested.

#### Fixture notes for later lanes

Two fixture traps cost time here and are worth recording:

- `Permanent.stack` holds only the cards BENEATH the top card. A digivolution assertion reads
  `stack === [baseInstanceId]` plus `topCard === newInstanceId`, not a two-element stack.
- Purple BT23 Digimon are noisy as bystanders. BT23-065 Phantomon's `[On Deletion]` plays a
  [Ghost] Digimon back from the trash, which silently rebuilt the opponent's board mid-test and
  read as a missing delete. Opponent boards here use vanilla BT1 Digimon (BT1-009 Lv.3,
  BT1-013/BT1-014 Lv.3/Lv.4, BT1-020 Lv.5) so only the audited card moves anything.

| --- | --- | --- |
| Q5337 | With an eligible Lv.≤6 opponent Digimon you MUST choose and delete it; you cannot fake the "didn't delete" branch by declining. | Behavioral + IR shape |
| Q5338 | A chosen but deletion-immune Lv.≤6 target still satisfies "didn't delete" — the gate reads what was actually removed. | Behavioral (BT14-062) + interpreter-level |
| Q5339 | "End the attack" moves the timing straight to end-of-attack: no counter timing, no block timing, the attack does not succeed. | Behavioral (no security check, no block window) |
| Q5340 | "End the attack" also ends the attack of a Digimon unaffected by effects, because it changes the timing, not the Digimon. | Behavioral (attacker protected from my effects) |

#### Clause → test → IR mapping

| Clause | Test | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| C1 | `exposes Execute through the live keyword seam` | `Static` → `GainKeyword {Execute}` on `isSelfRef` |
| C2 (On Play) | `plays a level 5 or lower [Ghost] Digimon from the trash on play without paying its cost` | `OnPlay` → `PlayWithoutCost` from `trash`, `payCost:false`, `optional:true` |
| C2 (refusal) | `leaves the Ghost in the trash when the On Play optional is declined` | `optional: true` |
| C2 (eligibility) | `plays nothing when the trash holds no level 5 or lower [Ghost] Digimon` | `levelComparison lte 5` + `nameOrTrait [Ghost] trait` + `kind ["Digimon"]` |
| C2 (On Deletion) | `deletes itself and an eligible opponent, plays a Ghost on deletion, ...` | `OnDeletion` → same `PlayWithoutCost` |
| C3 | `deletes itself and an eligible opponent ...` | `AllTurns` → `SubTrigger whenAttacking` |
| C3 (another) | `does not fire on its own attack — the clause reads 'another Digimon'` | `sourceFilter.excludeSelf: true` |
| C3 (any controller) | `fires on the opponent's attack too and deletes the attacking Digimon` | `sourceFilter` has no controller clause |
| C3 (cost declined) | `may decline self-deletion, leaving both Digimon in play while the attack proceeds` | leading `Delete` `optional`+`abortOnDecline` |
| C3 / Q5337 | `never offers to skip the opponent deletion when an eligible target exists` | second `Delete` has no `optional` |
| C4 | `deletes itself and ends the attack when no eligible opponent can be deleted` | `EndAttack` gated on `ifThisEffectDidNotDelete` |
| C4 / Q5338 / Q5340 | `ends the attack when the only eligible target is immune to the deletion` | gate reads `ctx.lastDeleteCount` |
| C4 / Q5339 | same test — no `securityChecked`, no blocker window, attacker survives | `EndAttack` → `fx.endAttack()` |
| C4 (refusal) | `may decline to end the attack, letting it check security` | `EndAttack.optional: true` |
| C4 (negative) | `the effect deletes the opponent's Lv.<=6 Digimon (count 1) => the attack CONTINUES` | gate false |

```

pnpm exec oxlint apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-069.ts apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-069.test.ts
pnpm exec oxfmt --check apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-069.test.ts   # clean
```

#### Fails-when-reverted evidence

Each mutation was applied to `BT23-069.ts`, the suite run, then the module restored.

| Mutation | Tests that went red |
| --- | --- |
| Drop `sourceFilter.excludeSelf` | `does not fire on its own attack` |
| Add `controller: "mine"` to `sourceFilter` | the opponent-attack test and the Q5338/Q5339/Q5340 test |
| Drop the `ifThisEffectDidNotDelete` condition on `EndAttack` | 3 tests, including the attack-continues branch and Q5337 |
| Raise the On Play level ceiling from 5 to 6 | `plays nothing when the trash holds no level 5 or lower [Ghost] Digimon` |
| Change the `[Ghost]` match from `trait` to `name` | both Ghost-play tests |
| Set `payCost: true` on the On Play play | the no-cost memory assertion |

#### Defects found

None in the module. The IR already models every printed clause, the `[Ghost]` condition is a
trait match (not a substring name match), the opponent Delete is mandatory, and the `EndAttack`
is gated on the actual removal count rather than on target selection.

The module has no `// @ts-nocheck` and typechecks; the `duration: "permanent"` on the Execute
grant is a valid `EffectDurationRef`.

#### Remaining gaps

- Q5337 is proved by the absence of a skip prompt plus the IR shape. A stronger proof would need
  a decision seam that lets a test refuse a mandatory action, which the engine correctly does not
  offer.
- Two pre-existing tests drive the clause through a hand-built `EffectContext` rather than the
  engine. They are kept for the interpreter-level gate proof but earn no behavioral credit; the
  behavioral equivalents added in this audit cover the same two branches.

### BT23-070 — Belphemon (X Antibody)

Purple Lv.6 Mega, Virus, 14000 DP, play cost 14, EvoCost Purple Lv.5 for 6.
Types: Demon Lord / X Antibody / Seven Great Demon Lords.

#### Printed clauses

1. `[Digivolve] Lv.6 w/[Belphemon] in name w/o [X Antibody] trait: Cost 2`
2. `＜Rush＞`
3. `＜Piercing＞`
4. `[When Digivolving] Delete all of your opponent's Digimon with the highest level. Then, if a card with [Belphemon] in its name is in this Digimon's digivolution cards, this Digimon attacks without suspending.`
5. `[End of Attack] This Digimon may digivolve into [Belphemon: Sleep Mode] in the trash, ignoring digivolution requirements and without paying the cost.`

#### Rulings covered

| Q&A | Ruling | Test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Q5341 | The attack after the deletion is mandatory. | "Q5341: the attack is mandatory even when every optional prompt is declined" — the run uses `autoDeclineOptional`, and the attack still resolves (security 2 -> 1). |
| Q5342 | The Digimon can attack even while suspended. | Test 3 arms `isSuspended = true` after the Active phase, then proves the attack lands and the permanent is still suspended afterwards. |

#### Clause -> test -> IR

| Clause | IR | Test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | `digivolutionRequirement: [{ level: 6, names: ["Belphemon"], excludeTraits: ["X Antibody"], cost: 2, isAlternate: true }]` | "requires a level 6 Digimon with [Belphemon] in its name and without the X Antibody trait": legal from BT13-088 (Belphemon: Sleep Mode), rejected from BT23-070 (excluded trait) and BT3-089 Boltmon (Lv.6 Purple, wrong name). Cost proved live in test 3 (memory 3 -> 1 with `useAlternateCost: true`) against the printed route in test 5 (memory 8 -> 2). |
| 2, 3 | `Static.keywords` | "exposes Rush and Piercing through the live keyword seam" (`observe().hasKeyword` / `hasPierce`). |
| 4a | `WhenDigivolving` -> `Delete` with `controller: "opponent"`, `kind: ["Digimon"]`, `superlative: "highestLevel"`, `count: "all"` | Test 3: both Lv.6 opponents (BT1-080, ST1-10) hit the trash, the Lv.4 (BT1-015) survives. Test 5 repeats the deletion on the printed route. |
| 4b | `Restrict` + `Attack` with `withoutSuspending: true`, `mandatory: true`, gated by `selfDigivolutionStackHasTrait` on name `Belphemon` | Test 3 (Belphemon in stack -> attacks, stays suspended, security 2 -> 1), test 4 (mandatory under decline), test 5 (no Belphemon under it -> no attack, security untouched, `securityChecked` never emitted). |
| 5 | `EndOfAttack` -> `Digivolve` `from: ["trash"]`, `payCost: false`, `ignoreRequirements: true`, `optional: true`, into name `Belphemon: Sleep Mode` | Test 3 accepts: top card becomes EX10-021, stack is [BT13-088, BT23-070], memory unchanged by the free route. Test 4 declines: top card is still BT23-070 and EX10-021 stays in the trash. |

#### Name matching

The printed route says "w/[Belphemon] **in name**", so substring matching is the correct
semantics — `names: ["Belphemon"]` in the module is right, not `namesExact`. The positive
proof is BT13-088 "Belphemon: Sleep Mode", which is legal even though its name is not the
bare token. The `excludeTraits: ["X Antibody"]` half is proved by rejecting another
BT23-070 as the source.

#### Changes made

- `apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-070.test.ts`: replaced the injected-timing proof
  (`advance().fire(EffectTiming.WhenDigivolving, ...)`) with public-intent behavioral tests
  driven by `startTurnLoop()` + `digivolve` intents. Added the printed-route branch, the
  decline branch, the mandatory-attack proof, and two illegal-source negatives.
- `apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-070.ts`: removed `// @ts-nocheck`. The module typechecks
  clean without it, so duration and IR type errors now surface. No IR change was needed.

#### Commands

- `pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec vitest run src/cards/BT23/BT23-070.test.ts --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism`
  -> 1 file passed, 8 tests passed, 2.99s. Log: `docs/audits/BT23-reaudit/logs/BT23-070-vitest.log`.
- `pnpm --filter @aegis/api typecheck` -> no errors in BT23-070.ts or BT23-070.test.ts.
  Pre-existing unrelated failures remain in `src/engine/effects/collect.ts` and
  `src/cards/BT23/BT23-068.test.ts` (other lanes).
- `pnpm exec oxlint` -> only the pre-existing `no-explicit-any` warnings on the two
  structural IR assertions, matching the accepted style in the exemplar tests.
- `pnpm exec oxfmt --check` -> clean. `git diff --check` -> clean.

#### Defects found

None in the card module. The prior test suite was the defect: its behavioral claim rested
on `advance().fire(...)`, which bypasses the digivolve path and therefore never proved the
cost, the stack condition, the mandatory attack, or the End of Attack route.

#### Remaining gaps

- No retained reds; no engine seam hit.
- ＜Piercing＞ is asserted through the keyword seam, not through an excess-damage security
  check, because the printed clause is the keyword itself and the shared keyword suites
  own its combat behavior.
- The alternate route is the only match for a Lv.6 Belphemon source and the printed EvoCost
  is the only match for a Lv.5 Purple source, so no fixture exists where both paths match
  and `useAlternateCost` picks between them. The flag is still passed explicitly on the

### BT23-071 — Dullahamon

#### Card summary

Catalog (`packages/shared/src/cards/data/cards.json`): Purple Digimon, level 7 Mega, Data, types `Ghost`/`LIBERATOR`, play cost 14, DP 14000, printed EvoCost Purple level 6 for 4.

Printed clauses:

1. `[Digivolve] While you have [Violet Inboots], [Phantomon]: Cost 6`
2. `＜Piercing＞`
3. `＜Security A. +1＞`
4. `＜Execute＞`
5. `[When Digivolving] Delete 1 of your opponent's Digimon with the highest level. If this effect didn't delete, this Digimon gets +5000 DP for the turn.`
6. `[On Deletion] You may play 1 level 6 or lower Digimon card with the [Ghost] trait from your trash without paying the cost.`

#### Rulings covered

`node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT23-071` returns two Q&A, both covered:

| Id | Ruling | Test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Q5343 | You cannot satisfy "didn't delete" by declining to choose; with an opposing Digimon you must delete the highest-level one. | "must delete the opponent's highest-level Digimon and then gets no DP bonus (Q5343)" — the whole run uses `autoDeclineOptional`, so the deletion still happening proves it is mandatory, and DP stays 14000. |
| Q5344 | Choosing a Digimon that prevents its own deletion does satisfy "didn't delete". | "gets +5000 for the turn when the chosen highest-level Digimon prevents deletion (Q5344)" — BT23-055 Cyberdramon survives by trashing its Option and Dullahamon reaches 19000 DP. |

#### Clause -> test -> IR mapping

| Clause | IR | Test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 digivolve route | `digivolutionRequirement[0]`: `namesExact: ["Phantomon"]`, `controllerControls {kind:["Tamer"], namesExact:["Violet Inboots"], min:1}`, `cost: 6`, `isAlternate` | "digivolves from Phantomon for exactly 6 …" (real `digivolve` intent, memory 6 -> 0, stack `[Phantomon]`, one card drawn); three negatives: no Tamer, opponent's Tamer only, MetalPhantomon base |
| 2-4 keywords | three `Static` entries | "exposes Piercing, Security Attack +1, and Execute through live seams" via `observe()` |
| 5 delete / DP | `WhenDigivolving`: `Delete` on `controller:"opponent"`, `kind:["Digimon"]`, `superlative:"highestLevel"`; then `ModifyDP +5000`, `duration:"forTheTurn"`, `condition ifThisEffectDidNotDelete`, `isSelfRef` | Q5343 test, Q5344 test, the no-opponent branch inside the cost test (19000 DP), and "drops the +5000 at the end of the turn" (19000 -> 14000 across `advance().runTurn`) |
| 6 on deletion | `OnDeletion`: `PlayWithoutCost` from `trash`, `payCost:false`, `optional:true`, `levelComparison lte 6`, trait `Ghost` | "plays only an eligible level 6 or lower Ghost from your own trash on deletion, for free" and "may decline the [On Deletion] play and leave the Ghost in trash" |

The final test pins the compiled shape of clauses 5 and 6 so a silent IR rewrite is caught.

#### Defect found and fixed

`digivolutionRequirement[0]` used `names: ["Phantomon"]`. Per `apps/api/src/engine/cards/cardData.ts:480-487`, `names` is a SUBSTRING gate and `namesExact` is equality. The printed route is a bracketed card name (`[Phantomon]: Cost 6`), which is exact, so the substring gate wrongly let BT20-073 **MetalPhantomon** (level 5, Purple/Black, also a `Ghost`) act as the base.

Proved red-then-green: with `names`, the run is `2 failed | 10 passed` — the IR-shape test and the MetalPhantomon negative, which got `{ ok: true }` instead of `{ ok: false, reason: "invalid-evolution" }`. With `namesExact`, 12 pass.

Also removed the file's `// @ts-nocheck`. `pnpm --filter @aegis/api typecheck` reports no error in `BT23-071.ts` or `BT23-071.test.ts` (pre-existing failures in `src/cards/BT23/BT23-068.test.ts` and `src/engine/combat/controller.ts` belong to other lanes).

#### Test-quality changes

The previous suite reached `[When Digivolving]` through the injected timing seam (`engine.fireTiming(...)` and `advance().fire(...)`), which proves nothing about the printed route or its cost. Every When Digivolving assertion now runs off a real `digivolve` intent, and the digivolve tests pin memory, stack identity and the bonus draw rather than only `{ ok: true }`.

#### Commands and results

```
pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec vitest run src/cards/BT23/BT23-071.test.ts --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism
```

**1 file passed, 12 tests passed**, Vitest 5.0.0, 3.81 s (log: `docs/audits/BT23-reaudit/logs/BT23-071-vitest.log`).

`pnpm exec oxlint` clean; `pnpm exec oxfmt --check` clean; `git diff --check` clean.

#### Remaining gaps

- `[On Deletion]` is opened with `advance().verb.deletePermanent(...)`. No public intent removes an established 14000 DP Digimon on demand, so the two On Deletion tests use the production deletion verb. The window itself and everything after it is real.
- No combat proof of `＜Piercing＞` excess damage, `＜Security A. +1＞` checking a second security card, or `＜Execute＞` in an attack. The keywords are asserted through the live continuous-effect seam only.
- No test that the `[On Deletion]` play is refused when the only trash Ghost is level 7, because BT23-071 itself is the only level-7 `Ghost` Digimon in the catalog; the level gate is covered indirectly by the non-Ghost control.
- The `controllerControls` gate is a battle-area check (`apps/api/src/engine/actions/digivolve.ts:572-592`). "While you have" is read as "in play"; not separately proven against a Violet Inboots in hand or trash.

#### Retained reds / skips

None.

#### Catalog discrepancy

None. The catalog text matches the official wording.

| --- | --- | --- |
| Catalog / rules | 2 | Every catalog field asserted; both Q&A covered; substring-vs-exact ruling applied and fixed. |
| IR trace | 2 | All six clauses mapped to compiled IR and pinned; the wrong name gate found and corrected. |
| Behavioral proof | 2 | Route, cost, stack, draw, mandatory deletion, both DP branches, duration expiry and both On Deletion branches asserted on final state. |
| Peer / stack proof | 1 | Near-name (MetalPhantomon), controller boundary (opponent's Tamer), missing-Tamer and non-Ghost/opponent-trash controls present; no combat-keyword or cross-card interaction proof. |
| Delivery gates | 0 | Coordinator-owned. |
| **Total** | **7/10** | |

8/10 — catalog/rules 2/2; IR trace 2/2; behavioral proof 2/2; peer and stack proof 1/2; delivery gates 1/2. Reviewed; [Phantomon] exact-route fix pending effects sync; evidence; collection gates passed on the working tree (sync/check, typecheck, lint, format, regression 3542/3542); commit and PR outstanding

### BT23-072 — King Drasil_7D6

Sources: committed catalog `BT23-072`; KB Q5345, Q5346, Q5347, Q5348 (`node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT23-072`).

#### Card summary

White Digimon, Mega, Unknown attribute, traits `9000` and `CS`, play cost 6, DP 9000, no evolution costs.

Printed clauses:

1. `[Hand] [Main] By paying 3 cost and placing this card as the bottom digivolution card of your [King Drasil_7D6] or [Mother Eater] in the breeding area, <Draw 1>`
2. `[All Turns] When any of your Digimon with the [Royal Knight] or [CS] trait are played, by suspending this Digimon, 1 of the played Digimon gains <Rush>, <Raid>, <Reboot> and <Blocker> until your opponent's turn ends.`
3. Inherited: `[Breeding] [Start of Your Main Phase] If this Digimon has 6 or more digivolution cards, you may play 1 Digimon card with [King Drasil] in its name from its digivolution cards without paying the cost.`

Catalog note: `types: ["9000", "CS"]` is correct, not a DP leak. `9000` is the printed trait shared with the BT13-007 Digi-Egg of the same name. No catalog discrepancy found.

#### Clause -> test -> IR map

| Clause / ruling | Test | IR anchor |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Catalog fields and printed text | "matches every catalog field and complete compiled clause" | `coverage: "full"`, `residual: []` |
| Clause 1 happy path (Mother Eater host) | "pays 3, places this hand card at the Mother Eater stack bottom, then draws 1" | `Main` effect, `cost.kind: "place"`, `position: "bottom"`, `additionalCosts: payMemory 3` |
| Clause 1 second printed host | "also accepts the [King Drasil_7D6] Digi-Egg as the breeding host" | `host.filter.nameOrTrait.tokens` |
| Q5345 no legal host | "refuses activation with no King Drasil_7D6 or Mother Eater in the breeding area" | `host.filter.zone: "breeding"` |
| Q5345 empty breeding area | "refuses activation with an empty breeding area" | same |
| Timing boundary | "refuses the [Main] activation on the opponent's turn" | `trigger: "Main"`, `isFromHand: true` |
| Q5346 all-or-nothing "by" cost | "pays no memory and draws nothing when the controller declines the placement" | `optional: true`, `abortOnDecline: true` |
| Clause 2 own CS Digimon played | "suspends itself and grants all four keywords when an own CS Digimon is played" | `SubTrigger whenPlayed`, `sourceFilter` trait `Royal Knight`/`CS`, suspend cost |
| Q5347 own play triggers the watcher | "triggers on its own play and grants the four keywords to itself" | same |
| Clause 2 trait boundary | "ignores an own played Digimon with neither the Royal Knight nor the CS trait" | `sourceFilter.nameOrTrait` |
| Clause 2 controller boundary | "ignores a CS Digimon played by the opponent" | `sourceFilter.controller: "mine"` |
| Clause 2 refusal branch | "keeps the played Digimon unchanged when the controller refuses the suspension cost" | `optional: true`, `abortOnDecline: true` |
| Clause 2 duration | "keeps the grants through the opponent's turn and drops them once that turn ends" | `duration: "untilOpponentTurnEnd"` on all four grants |
| Clause 3 positive | "plays a King Drasil card from its own stack for free at six or more digivolution cards" | `PlayWithoutCost`, `fromOwnDigivolutionStack`, `condition.selfDigivolutionCountAtLeast: 6` |
| Clause 3 count boundary | "does not fire at five digivolution cards" | same condition |
| Q5348 breeding-only location | "does not fire while the same stack sits in the battle area" | `isBreeding: true` |
| IR shape (3 tests) | "compiles the [Hand] [Main] cost...", "anchors all four keyword grants...", "marks the free-play clause..." | structural |

All four KB rulings are covered by behavioral tests.

#### Defect found and fixed

`BT23-072.ts`, `[Hand] [Main]` host filter: `nameOrTrait` used `match: "name"`, which is substring matching in
`apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/matching/definition.ts` (`matchNameOrTrait`). The printed names are bracketed
(`[King Drasil_7D6]`, `[Mother Eater]`), so the host must match exactly. Changed to `match: "nameExact"`.

Proof: the "also accepts the [King Drasil_7D6] Digi-Egg as the breeding host" and Mother Eater tests stay green under
`nameExact`, so the fix does not narrow a legal host; the IR-shape test goes red when the mode is reverted to `name`
(verified: 1 failed / 18 passed on the reverted module). The fix is currently latent in behavior: no card in the
catalog has a name that merely contains "King Drasil_7D6" or "Mother Eater", so no near-name negative fixture exists
today. The inherited clause keeps `match: "name"` on purpose, because "with [King Drasil] in its name" is printed
substring wording (it matches both BT13-007 and BT23-072).

#### Tests rewritten

The previous suite proved clauses 2 and 3 through injected timing (`advance.fireSubTrigger("whenPlayed")` and
`advance.fireGlobal(OnStartMainPhase)`), which is structural only. Both now run through the real turn loop
(`startTurnLoop()` + `advance.waitForMainPhase`) and public `playCard` / `endPhase` intents. Two fixture rules from
the brief were needed: every turn-loop fixture keeps a neutral playable hand card (`ST1-02`), otherwise Main
auto-passes and the assertions read a state after the turn boundary, where the suspension and the keyword grants
are already gone.

Fixtures use main-deck Digimon for security counts and real Digi-Eggs (`BT22-007`, `BT13-007`, `BT23-003`) only in
breeding.

#### Commands and results

- `pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec vitest run src/cards/BT23/BT23-072.test.ts --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism`
  — 1 file, 19 tests passed, 3.07 s. Log: `logs/BT23-072-vitest.log`.
- `pnpm --filter @aegis/api typecheck` — no errors in `BT23-072.ts` or `BT23-072.test.ts`. The remaining errors in
  `BT23-073.test.ts` and `BT23-075.test.ts` belong to other lanes.
- `pnpm exec oxfmt` — both files formatted. `pnpm exec oxlint` — only the `no-explicit-any` warnings shared with the
  sibling BT23 tests (IR-shape casts and the `cardSourceOf` seam).

#### Retained reds and gaps

None. No engine seam had to be retained as `it.fails`.

Remaining gaps:

- The `nameExact` fix has no behavioral near-name negative, because the catalog holds no such card (documented above).
- Clause 2 is not proved against a Royal Knight Digimon that lacks the `CS` trait. Every cheap Royal Knight in the
  fixture pool also carries `CS`; the trait disjunction is covered structurally and by the non-matching negative.
- The keyword grants are asserted through `observe().hasKeyword`. Their combat consequences (an actual Rush attack,
  a block) are the keyword mechanics' own suites, not this card's.

   card of your [Mother Eater] in the breeding area, 1 of those Digimon doesn't leave.`
3. Inherited: `[Breeding] [Your Turn] [Once Per Turn] When any of your Digimon cards with the
   [Eater] trait would be played, you may reduce the play costs by 1.`

Clause 2 carries **no cause qualifier**. Compare BT22-007 [Mother Eater], whose inherited effect
prints "would leave the battle area **other than by your effects**". That difference is the whole
defect below.

#### Rulings covered

`node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT23-073` returns Q5349 and Q5350.

| Ruling | Test | Result |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Q5349 — Eater Bit's [All Turns] answers a leave caused by an opponent effect, and BT22-007 in breeding stays available | "saves an ally from an OPPONENT effect by deleting itself" + "places itself as the bottom digivolution card of the breeding Mother Eater instead" | pass |
| Q5350 — a {Breeding} effect works only from the breeding area | "from breeding reduces the first Eater play cost by 1 and only once per turn" + "gives no play-cost reduction while the same stack sits in the battle area" | pass |
| BT23-037 Q5565 / BT23-048 Q5567 (cross-lane witnesses) — a Hudie played by the inherited effect is saved from its own delayed deletion, and the "can't digivolve" restriction stays intact | "saves an ally from the CONTROLLER'S OWN delayed deletion and keeps its digivolve lock" | pass after the fix |

Q5349's second half (Mother Eater's inherited effect placing a *further* Digimon in the same
event) is not proved: it needs an effect that makes two of my [Eater]/[Hudie] Digimon leave at
once, and no such public route exists in the catalog for this fixture. Recorded as a gap, not a
red.

#### Defect found and fixed

`apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-073.ts` compiled clause 2 with `leaveCause: "otherThanYourEffect"`.
`causeAllows` (`apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/actions/replacement.ts` ~line 249) then
returns false whenever the removal is `byEffect` and the resolving seat is the card's own
controller, so `consultLeavePrevention` (`apps/api/src/engine/effects/leavePrevention.ts` line 94)
skipped the replacement entirely. The controller could never save a Digimon from their own effect.

The printed text has no such qualifier, so the field is removed and `causeAllows` stays at `"any"`.
Two earlier lanes flagged exactly this (docs BT23-037 Q5565, BT23-048 Q5567, REVIEW-NOTES "Session 2
engine seam queue").

Red-then-green proof: restoring `leaveCause: "otherThanYourEffect"` fails 2 of the 14 tests
("...with no cause qualifier" and "saves an ally from the CONTROLLER'S OWN delayed deletion...");
removing it passes all 14.

**No engine seam remains.** The replacement *is* consulted on a `DelayedDelete`:
`primitives.delayedDeletePlayed` resolves through `deletePermanent(..., "byEffect")`, which calls
`consultLeavePrevention` with `resolvingSeat` = the controller. Only the cause gate was blocking
it. No `it.fails` reproducer is retained for this card.

#### Clause -> test -> IR

| Clause | IR | Test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Catalog identity | whole `compiled` | "matches every catalog field and complete compiled clause" |
| Clause 1 shape | `OnPlay` -> `Delete` `{ controller: "opponent", levels: [3] }` | "deletes an opponent level 3 Digimon on play" |
| Clause 1 endpoints | same | "publicly plays for 3 memory and deletes only the opponent's level 3 Digimon" |
| Clause 2 shape + no cause gate | `AllTurns` `OncePerTurn` -> `Replacement wouldLeavePlay`, `excludeSelf`, trait `[Eater, Hudie]`, `Prevent leavePlay` with two `costOptions` | "offers the two correct leave-prevention costs..., with no cause qualifier" |
| Clause 2, opponent effect | `causeAllows` "any" | "saves an ally from an OPPONENT effect by deleting itself" |
| Clause 2, own effect (delayed delete) | `causeAllows` "any" vs `delayedDeletePlayed` | "saves an ally from the CONTROLLER'S OWN delayed deletion and keeps its digivolve lock" |
| Clause 2, battle | `causeAllows` "any" for `byBattle` | "saves an ally from a BATTLE deletion by deleting itself" |
| Clause 2, cost option 2 | `place` -> `digivolutionStack`, `position: "bottom"`, host `zone: "breeding"` + name `[Mother Eater]` | "places itself as the bottom digivolution card of the breeding Mother Eater instead" |
| Clause 2, optional | `optional: true`, `abortOnDecline: true` | "lets the ally leave when the controller declines the prevention" |
| Clause 2, "your OTHER Digimon" | `excludeSelf: true` | "does not protect itself (the clause reads \"your OTHER Digimon\")" |
| Clause 2, trait gate | `nameOrTrait [Eater, Hudie]` | "does not protect a non-Eater, non-Hudie ally" |
| Clause 3 shape | `YourTurn`, `isInherited`, `isBreeding`, `OncePerTurn` -> `Replacement wouldBePlayed` / `reduceCost 1` | "keeps the inherited Eater play-cost reduction once per turn in breeding" |
| Clause 3 endpoints + once per turn | same | "from breeding reduces the first Eater play cost by 1 and only once per turn" |
| Clause 3 [Breeding] gate | `isBreeding: true` | "gives no play-cost reduction while the same stack sits in the battle area" |

#### What the behavioral tests pin

- **On Play**: memory 3 -> 0 through a public `playCard`; the opponent's battle area ends as exactly
  the level 4 permanent id; the opponent's trash holds exactly the level 3 instance id; no
  `actionRejected` event.
- **Opponent effect**: the opponent publicly plays their own BT23-073, whose On Play targets my
  level 3 [Hudie] Gotsumon. My board ends as exactly `[ally]`, my trash as exactly the Eater Bit
  instance.
- **Own delayed deletion**: BT23-050 Ankylomon over BT23-048 Gotsumon attacks; Gotsumon's inherited
  [When Attacking] plays BT23-037 Tentomon free and schedules its `DelayedDelete` for the end of the
  opponent's turn. After a full seat 0 turn the played Tentomon is asserted still present; after a
  full seat 1 turn it is *still* present and my trash is exactly the Eater Bit instance.
  Q5565/Q5567 second half: `observe().isRestricted(survivor, "digivolve")` is true and a public
  `digivolve` of BT23-041 onto it is rejected with the top card unchanged.
- **Battle**: my 1000 DP Gotsumon attacks a 3000 DP blocker. My board ends as exactly the attacker
  id, my trash as exactly the Eater Bit instance, and the blocker is untouched.
- **Mother Eater cost**: with `preferOptionIndex: 1`, the ally survives, my trash is empty, and
  `stack[0]` (the bottom of the breeding host's digivolution cards) is the Eater Bit instance.
- **Decline**: with `autoDeclineOptional`, my board ends as exactly the Eater Bit id and my trash as
  exactly the ally instance.
- **Inherited reduction**: memory 5 -> 3 on the first Eater play (printed 3, paid 2), then 3 -> 0 on
  the second in the same turn (full 3). The same stack in the battle area charges the full 3.

Every behavioral test drives public intents (`playCard`, `attack`, `digivolve`) plus
`advance().runTurn()` for real turn boundaries. `s.give` is used once, only to put an evolution card
in hand after the turn loop.

  — 1 file, **14 passed (14)**, 3.25s. Log: `logs/BT23-073-focused.log`.

### BT23-074 — Eater Legion

#### Card summary

Sources: committed catalog `BT23-074`; official BT23 card list (`logs/official-bt23.html`, entry BT23-074); KB Q5351, Q6703, Q6704, Q6705, Q6706, Q6707.

White Digimon, play cost 8, DP 8000, no printed EvoCost, form Eater, attribute `-`, types Hudie/CS. No lower text.

Printed clauses:

1. `[Digivolve] [Erika Mishima]: Cost 3` — the only digivolution route the card prints.
2. `＜Alliance＞`
3. `＜Reboot＞`
4. `[On Play] [When Digivolving] If you have [Mother Eater] in the breeding area, you may play up to 6 play cost's total worth of Digimon cards with the [Eater] trait from your hand without paying the costs.`

The catalog text matches the official list exactly. No discrepancy.

#### Defect found and fixed

`BT23-074.ts` declared the route as `names: ["Erika Mishima"]`. `names` is the SUBSTRING gate
(`apps/api/src/engine/cards/cardData.ts`, "Name gate: base name must contain at least one token").
A bracketed `[Name]: Cost N` route is an EXACT identity, so the field must be `namesExact`.
The base is also a Tamer, not a Digimon (KB Q6703), which the requirement type expresses as
`baseIsTamer: true`. Both were added.

Honest limit on the proof: the fix is provable structurally, not behaviorally, because the
catalog holds exactly one card whose name contains "Erika Mishima" (`BT23-084`, a Tamer).
No near-name superstring and no non-Tamer card of that name exist, so substring and exact
matching pick the same single base today. The red-then-green check therefore fails only the
IR assertion (verified: reverting to `names` fails 1 of 11 tests, the record test). The fix
still matters — any future card named e.g. "Erika Mishima (X)" would wrongly satisfy `names`.

The module's `// @ts-nocheck` was removed; it typechecks without it.

#### Clause -> test -> IR mapping

| Printed clause | IR | Test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `[Digivolve] [Erika Mishima]: Cost 3` | `digivolutionRequirement: [{ namesExact: ["Erika Mishima"], baseIsTamer: true, cost: 3, isAlternate: true }]` | "publicly digivolves from the Erika Mishima Tamer for exactly 3..."; "rejects every base other than the exact Erika Mishima Tamer"; record test |
| `＜Alliance＞` | Static effect, `keywords: [{ keyword: "Alliance" }]` | "keeps ＜Alliance＞ and ＜Reboot＞ live on the field" |
| `＜Reboot＞` | Static effect, `keywords: [{ keyword: "Reboot" }]` | same |
| `[On Play] ... play up to 6 ... [Eater] ... without paying the costs` | `OnPlay` -> `PlayMultiple { totalCost: 6, from: "hand", payCost: false, optional: true, filter.nameOrTrait [Eater/trait], condition youHave zone breeding [Mother Eater] }` | "plays the same clause when Eater Legion is played from the hand for its own cost" |
| `[When Digivolving] ...` (same body) | `WhenDigivolving` -> identical `PlayMultiple` | "plays exactly 6 play cost worth of Eater Digimon on the When Digivolving window"; "never plays an Eater card whose play cost alone exceeds the 6 budget"; "plays nothing without [Mother Eater] in the breeding area"; "honors the refusal branch" |

`Eater` lives in the catalog's `forms`, not `types`. `match: "trait"` is correct: the engine's
trait set is forms ∪ attributes ∪ types (`cardData.ts staticTraitsOf`).

#### Q&A coverage

| Q&A | Covered by | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Q5351 (can't attack the turn it digivolves from a Tamer played that turn) | "can't attack on the turn it digivolves from a freshly played Tamer" | Erika is publicly played, digivolved into this turn, then the public `attack` intent is refused. The permanent's `summoningSick` projection is asserted true and the opponent's security stack is unchanged. `illegal-target` is the engine's surfaced code for the §16-1 refusal (`engine/combat/legality.ts`). |
| Q6703 (the Tamer is not treated as a digivolving Digimon) | Partly: the Tamer route is proved to work publicly, and the requirement now carries `baseIsTamer: true` | Untested halves: "a Digimon can't digivolve effect still allows this route" and "when a Digimon digivolves watchers don't trigger" both need a third-party restriction/watcher card, i.e. engine-wide behavior outside this card's module. The card's OWN `[When Digivolving]` clause must still fire on this route (otherwise its printed text is dead on the only route it prints, and Q6704 confirms this is a real digivolution); the tests prove it does. |
| Q6704 (bonus draw on a Tamer digivolve) | "publicly digivolves ... draws the bonus" | Hand and deck counts asserted exactly: hand ends with the three untouched Eater Bits plus the drawn `BT1-009`, deck loses exactly one card. |
| Q6705 (the Tamer becomes a digivolution card) | "publicly digivolves ... keeps her as a digivolution card" | `stack` is exactly `["BT23-084"]` under the `BT23-074` top card, and the board holds a single permanent. |
| Q6706 (no security effects from the Tamer's lower text) | Not tested — untestable with this card | Erika Mishima has no lower-text security effect. Her `[Security]` clause is upper text, and a digivolution card's upper text is never inherited anyway, so there is nothing on the real card pair to distinguish. |
| Q6707 (inherited effects from the Tamer's lower text ARE gained) | Not tested — not discriminating with this card pair | Erika's inherited text is "[Your Turn] While this Digimon is [Hudiemon], [Eater Legion] or [Eater EDEN], it gains ＜Alliance＞". Eater Legion already PRINTS ＜Alliance＞, so a positive keyword read proves nothing about the inheritance path. A discriminating probe would chain BT23-075 Eater EDEN on top (it prints no Alliance), which reaches outside this card's allowed edit set. Flagged for the coordinator. |

#### Additional behavioral endpoints

- Budget arithmetic: with three cost-3 Eater Bits offered, exactly two are played (3+3 = 6)
  and the third stays in hand. Memory moves 5 -> 2, proving the digivolve cost of 3 was the
  only payment and the plays were free.
- Budget floor: a lone cost-8 `BT22-082` Eater Adam is never played; it stays in hand and the
  board keeps a single permanent.
- Condition gate: with no `[Mother Eater]` in breeding, all three Eater Bits stay in hand.
- Refusal branch: with the auto-responder off, the `selectCards` request is inspected
  (`min: 0`, three candidates) and answered with an empty selection; nothing is played and
  memory is unchanged at 2.
- Illegal sources: `BT23-073` (Eater Digimon), `BT23-101` (Hudiemon), `BT23-083` are all
  refused with `invalid-evolution`, the card stays in hand, and memory is untouched. The card
  prints no EvoCost, so the Erika route is the only legal source.

Fixtures are legal: security holds real main-deck Digimon (`BT1-009`/`BT1-010`), the Digi-Egg
`BT22-007` sits in the breeding area, and Erika reaches the field either as a seeded permanent
or through the public `playCard` intent.

- Typecheck: `pnpm --filter @aegis/api typecheck` — 4 pre-existing errors, none in my files (`BT23-075.test.ts` x3, `BT23-076.test.ts` x1; other lanes). Log: `logs/BT23-074-typecheck.log`.
- Lint: `pnpm exec oxlint apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-074.ts apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-074.test.ts` — clean.
- Format: `pnpm exec oxfmt --check` on both files — clean.
- Red-then-green: reverting `namesExact`/`baseIsTamer` to `names` fails 1 of 11 tests.

#### Coordinator actions required

`src/cards/BT23/BT23-catalog-sync.test.ts` compares each module's IR against
`packages/shared/src/effects/effects.json`. That test already failed before this work with 21
mismatched card ids; the module fix makes it 22 by adding `BT23-074`. Regenerating
`effects.json` is coordinator-owned. The runtime is unaffected: `registerIrCard` writes the
module's normalized record into `compiledEffects`, so the module is the live authority.

- Q6703's "can't digivolve" and third-party-watcher halves are engine-wide, not card-local.
- The exact-vs-substring name fix has no behavioral discriminator in the current catalog.
