### BT23-075 — Eater EDEN

#### Card summary

White Digimon, play cost 10, DP 12000, form Eater, type CS, no printed level, no printed
evolution cost.

Printed clauses (official card list, `docs/audits/BT23-reaudit/logs/official-bt23.html`):

1. `[Digivolve] [Eater Legion]: Cost 3`
2. `[On Play] [When Digivolving] Return 1 of your opponent's play cost 6 or lower Digimon or
   Tamers to the bottom of the deck. For each of your [Mother Eater]'s digivolution cards in
   the breeding area, add 1 to this effect's play cost maximum.`
3. `[All Turns] When this Digimon would leave the battle area other than by your effects, you
   may play 1 [Eater] trait Digimon card from your hand without paying the cost.`
4. `[End of Opponent's Turn] [Once Per Turn] Delete 1 of your opponent's lowest play cost
   Digimon.`

##### Catalog discrepancy (coordinator action)

`packages/shared/src/cards/data/cards.json` renders clause 3 as "1 [Eater] Digimon card". The
official list says "1 [Eater] **trait** Digimon card". Cosmetic only — the compiled IR already
matches on trait, not name — but the catalog string should be corrected by the catalog owner.

#### Q&A coverage

| Q&A | Subject | Covered by |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Q5352 | Cannot chain BT22-007's inherited breeding placement with EDEN's own leave effect | retained red `it.fails` "does not stack the breeding [Mother Eater] placement…" |

Coordinator probe for the BT23-074 lane (Q6706/Q6707) is answered below.

#### Clause -> test -> IR mapping

| Clause | Behavioral test | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 (route) | "publicly digivolves from [Eater Legion] for 3, draws, and returns an opposing cost-6 Tamer"; "charges the printed 3 whether or not the intent asks for the alternate cost"; "refuses a near-name [Eater] source that is not [Eater Legion]" | `digivolutionRequirement: [{ namesExact: ["Eater Legion"], cost: 3, isAlternate: true }]` |
| 2 (return) | same digivolve test (When Digivolving); "cannot reach a play cost 8 Digimon with no [Mother Eater] in breeding" (On Play, ceiling floor) | `Return` to `deckBottom`, `playCostLte: 6` |
| 2 (ceiling) | "raises the ceiling by each digivolution card of a breeding [Mother Eater]" | `playCostCeiling { base 6, raise 1, per 1, unit digivolutionCardsOfFiltered, filter { mine, breeding, nameExact "Mother Eater" } }` |
| 3 (leave) | "plays a free [Eater] from hand when battle deletion takes it off the board"; "may decline the free [Eater] play and still leaves the battle area"; "does not trigger the free [Eater] play when your own effect removes it" | `Replacement` on `wouldLeavePlay`, `leaveCause: "otherThanYourEffect"`, `sourceFilter.isSelfRef`, nested optional `PlayWithoutCost` from hand |
| 4 (end of opponent's turn) | "deletes the opponent's lowest play cost Digimon once per opponent turn and resets next turn" | `EndOfOpponentsTurn` + `frequency: "OncePerTurn"`, `superlative: "lowestPlayCost"` |

Structural tests pin the exact IR shape for all four clauses so a future regeneration cannot
silently drop the ceiling, the leave cause or the once-per-turn gate.

#### Defects found and fixed (local, in `BT23-075.ts`)

1. **Substring digivolve route.** `digivolutionRequirement` used `names: ["Eater Legion"]`.
   `matchingDigivolutionRequirement` (`apps/api/src/engine/cards/cardData.ts` ~479) treats
   `names` as substring — the "[X] in name" wording. The printed `[Eater Legion]: Cost 3` is
   an exact card name. Changed to `namesExact`.
2. **Substring bracketed name in the ceiling filter.** Both ceiling filters used
   `match: "name"` for `[Mother Eater]`; `matchNameOrTrait` treats `"name"` as substring and
   `"nameExact"` as equality. Changed to `nameExact` on both effects.
3. **`// @ts-nocheck` removed.** The module typechecks without it, so future `duration`/field
   errors will surface.

Neither name fix changes behavior against the current catalog (no other card's name contains
"Eater Legion" or "Mother Eater"), so both are proved structurally, plus a near-name
digivolve negative using BT22-082 Eater Adam (same [Eater] trait, different name) which is
refused.

#### Retained red (engine seam, do not fix in this lane)

`it.fails("does not stack the breeding [Mother Eater] placement with its own leave effect
(Q5352)")`.

- Setup: seat 0 has a breeding Digimon with BT22-007 [Mother Eater] in its digivolution
  cards, Eater EDEN in the battle area and an [Eater] Digimon in hand. Seat 1 deletes EDEN by
  effect.
- Ruling (Q5352): you may **not** first place EDEN under the breeding Digimon with BT22-007's
  inherited replacement and then also activate EDEN's own [All Turns] replacement.
- Actual: both apply — EDEN is placed under the breeding host **and** the free [Eater] is
  played.
- Seam: `apps/api/src/engine/effects/leavePrevention.ts`, `consultLeaveReplacements`, the
  `repl.mode === "instead"` branch. It stops offering further replacements only when the
  leaving permanent was relocated (`const relocated = host.permanentById(leavingId) ===
  undefined`). EDEN's replacement plays a card from hand and leaves EDEN in place, so it does
  not consume the leave event and the second "instead" replacement runs as well.
- Expected fix: one "instead" replacement per leave event, chosen by the leaving permanent's
  controller, or an explicit exclusivity gate.

#### Coordinator probe: BT23-074 Q6706/Q6707

Discriminator used: Erika Mishima (BT23-084) is a Tamer whose **lower** text grants
＜Alliance＞ while the Digimon is [Hudiemon], [Eater Legion] or [Eater EDEN]. Eater EDEN
prints no ＜Alliance＞, so an EDEN stack built through Erika separates the inherited-effect
ruling from EDEN's own printed keywords.

- `gains inherited ＜Alliance＞ on Eater EDEN through an Erika Mishima source (Q6707)`:
  play Erika on the board, digivolve BT23-074 off the Tamer (`useAlternateCost: true`), then
  BT23-075 off Eater Legion. Final stack is `["BT23-084", "BT23-074"]` under EDEN and
  `observe().hasKeyword(perm, "Alliance")` is `true`.
- `has no ＜Alliance＞ on an Eater EDEN stack without Erika Mishima`: BT23-074 (which prints
  ＜Alliance＞ itself) as the base, digivolve EDEN onto it. Stack is `["BT23-074"]` and
  Alliance is `false` — the base's printed keyword correctly does not carry up.

**Result: the engine matches Q6707.** A Tamer digivolution card's inherited (lower) text does
reach the Digimon above it, and only that text does. Q6706 (security effects are *not*
gained) is satisfied by construction here: Erika's `[Security] Play this card without paying
the cost` never activates from the digivolution cards, but this probe does not assert it
directly — it is not observable without a security check on a card already on the board.

```
# 1 file passed, 15 passed | 1 expected fail (16), ~6.8s
pnpm --filter @aegis/api typecheck        # no BT23-075 errors (pre-existing failures elsewhere)

git diff --check                          # clean
```

#### Remaining gaps

- **Ceiling wording is ambiguous.** "For each of your [Mother Eater]'s digivolution cards in
  the breeding area" is modeled as *the digivolution cards under a breeding permanent named
  Mother Eater*. The competing reading is *Mother Eater cards that are digivolution cards in
  the breeding area* — the shape Q5352 describes. The possessive `'s` and BT22-007's own
  breeding effect ("place [Mother Eater]s as **this Digimon's** top digivolution cards", "if
  this Digimon has 10 or more digivolution cards") both support the modeled reading, but no
  ruling settles it. Coordinator may want a KB question.
- The Q5352 red uses `verb.enterEffectResolution` + `verb.deletePermanent` for the
  opponent-effect leave cause (the same seam BT22-007's own suite uses); no printed card in
  the fixture pool produces that cause on demand. The battle-deletion tests for clause 3 are
  fully public.
- The "your own effect" negative for clause 3 also uses the removal verb inside seat 0's
  effect resolution, for the same reason.
- Q6706 is not directly asserted (see probe section).

### BT23-076 — Sistermon Blanc

#### Card

- Catalog: `packages/shared/src/cards/data/cards.json` → `BT23-076`.
- White, Digimon, Lv.3 Rookie, play cost 3, DP 3000, Vaccine, traits Puppet / CS, no printed digivolution costs.
- Printed text:
  1. `[On Play] Add your top security card to the hand. Then, ＜Recovery +1 (Deck)＞`
  2. `[Your Turn] When this Digimon suspends, 1 of your other Digimon may digivolve into a Digimon card with [Huckmon] in its name or the [Royal Knight] or [CS] trait in the hand or trash with the digivolution cost reduced by 1.`
- No inherited effect.

#### Rulings

`node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT23-076` → no knowledge-base entries. the Knowledge base index section of this document line 82 also lists 0 Q&A ids. So no Q&A coverage obligation; mechanics were checked against the comprehensive rules instead:

- `[comprehensive §16-6]` ＜Recovery＞: place the stated number of cards from the stated area face down on top of the security stack.
- `[glossary] ＜Recovery +x (Deck)＞`: the cards come from the top of the deck.
- `[comprehensive §3-4-5-3]` breeding-area cards can't be affected by effects that do not name the breeding area, so "1 of your other Digimon" means battle area only.

#### Clause → test → IR plan

| # | Printed clause | IR | Test |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `[On Play]` add top security to hand | `SecurityManipulation op:"toHand" controller:"mine" amount:1 toTop:true` | play Blanc with the real `playCard` intent; assert the old top security instance id is in hand |
| 2 | `Then, ＜Recovery +1 (Deck)＞` | `SecurityManipulation op:"addTop" controller:"mine" source:"deck" amount:1` | same test: former deck top is now `security[0]`, face down, deck shrinks by 1, security count unchanged, order preserved below the new top |
| 3 | `[Your Turn] When this Digimon suspends` | `YourTurn` effect wrapping `SubTrigger event:"whenSuspended"` with `sourceFilter:{isSelfRef:true}` | Blanc attacks (public `attack` intent) → suspends → offer appears |
| 4 | only *this* Digimon suspending counts | `sourceFilter:{isSelfRef:true}` | another friendly Digimon attacks and suspends → nothing happens |
| 5 | `[Your Turn]` restriction | trigger `YourTurn` | opponent's turn: opponent plays P-131 Pteromon, whose `[On Play]` suspends Blanc → no digivolution offered |
| 6 | "1 of your **other** Digimon" | `target.filter.excludeSelf:true` + default battle-area-only zone | candidate set from the real `chooseTargets` decision excludes Blanc; a Digimon in the breeding area is also not offered |
| 7 | into a card with `[Huckmon]` in its name | `nameOrTrait tokens:["Huckmon"] match:"name"` | BaoHuckmon (BT13-013) from hand |
| 8 | into a `[CS]` trait card | `nameOrTrait tokens:["CS"] match:"trait"` | Meramon (BT22-010, CS) from hand |
| 9 | into a `[Royal Knight]` trait card | `nameOrTrait tokens:["Royal Knight"] match:"trait"` | Magnamon (BT23-054, Royal Knight) from hand |
| 10 | ineligible cards stay put | same filter | Greymon (BT1-015), a legal digivolution but no name/trait match, is never taken |
| 11 | "in the hand **or trash**" | `from:["hand","trash"]` | BaoHuckmon in trash digivolves |
| 12 | "with the digivolution cost reduced by 1" | `payCost:true, reduceCost:1` | memory delta is printed cost − 1 (2 → 1 for BaoHuckmon, 4 → 3 for Magnamon), and the same digivolution costs the full amount through the plain `digivolve` intent |
| 13 | "**may**" | `optional:true` | `autoDeclineOptional` run: no digivolution, memory unchanged, card stays in hand |
| 14 | digivolution requirements still apply | engine legality | a Digimon whose colour/level does not satisfy the target card is not offered |

Plus the catalog conformance test and the digivolution bonus draw (a real digivolution draws 1), asserted through hand and deck counts.

#### Tests written

`apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-076.test.ts`, 13 tests. All behavioral tests reach the watcher through the public `attack` intent — the only public way to suspend Blanc — plus one turn-loop test for the opponent's turn. No injected-timing (`advance().fire*`) test is used for a positive proof; the single Advance-surface call is `verb.unsuspend`, used as setup between two attacks.

| Test | Clauses |
|---|---|
| matches every catalog field and declares complete coverage | catalog |
| on play, adds the top security card to hand and then recovers 1 from the deck on top | 1, 2 |
| when Blanc suspends by attacking, another Digimon digivolves into a [Huckmon] card from hand for 1 less | 3, 6, 7, 12 |
| takes a [CS]-trait card from the trash on its printed route for 1 less | 8, 11, 12 |
| reduces the alternate [CS] route by 1 as well | 8, 12 |
| triggers again on a second suspension in the same turn | 3 (no once-per-turn cap) |
| takes a [Royal Knight]-trait card from hand for 1 less | 9, 12 |
| charges the full digivolution cost without Blanc's effect | 12 (control) |
| never offers a card that matches neither the name nor the two traits | 10 |
| declining the optional digivolution leaves the board and memory untouched | 13 |
| does not react when a different Digimon suspends | 4 |
| offers only other battle-area Digimon, never Blanc itself or the breeding Digimon | 6 |
| stays silent on the opponent's turn when an opponent effect suspends Blanc | 5 |

Endpoints asserted: exact instance ids in hand, security (with face-down orientation and stack order), deck, trash and on the digivolution stack; memory after every payment; `pendingDecision` empty; `assertNoLoudGap`.

#### Findings

No defect found in `BT23-076.ts`. Every printed clause resolves correctly.

Two things checked and cleared rather than fixed:

- **Breeding area.** The `Digivolve` target filter carries no `zone`, but `resolvePermanentTargets` (`apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/targeting/permanents.ts:110-124`) defaults a zone-less filter to the battle area, so the breeding Digimon is never offered. Proven behaviorally, not just by reading the code. The `zone`-less pitfall listed in the worker brief applies to `youHave` count filters, not to permanent targeting.
- **`[Huckmon]` in its name.** Printed as "with [Huckmon] in its name", which is substring wording, so `match: "name"` with substring semantics is right; BaoHuckmon is proven to qualify.

`// @ts-nocheck` removed from `BT23-076.ts`. The module typechecks without it.

##### Non-defect worth recording

A card that satisfies both its printed EvoCost and its own alternate `[Digivolve]` requirement makes the effect-driven digivolution ask a `chooseOption` ("Printed digivolution requirement (cost N)" / "Alternate digivolution requirement (cost M)") before it resolves — `apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/actions/digivolve.ts:440-452`. A test that does not answer `chooseOption` silently sees no digivolution at all. This cost me a diagnosis cycle on Meramon (BT22-010); it is correct behavior, not a bug, and Blanc's reduction is applied to whichever route the controller picks (both branches asserted).

```
  Test Files  1 passed (1)
       Tests  13 passed (13)
    Duration  5.74s
```
Log: `docs/audits/BT23-reaudit/logs/BT23-076-vitest.log`.

```

pnpm exec oxlint  apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-076.ts apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-076.test.ts   # clean
pnpm exec oxfmt --check apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-076.ts apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-076.test.ts   # clean
git diff --check   # clean
```

#### Remaining gaps

- No card in the catalog is a legal digivolution target for a White Lv.3 base with the `[Huckmon]` name or the `[Royal Knight]`/`[CS]` trait, so "other" cannot be proven by letting Blanc try to digivolve itself. It is proven instead from the real `chooseTargets` candidate list, which never contains Blanc's permanent id.
- The opponent-turn negative relies on P-131 Pteromon's `[On Play]` suspension. That is the only public opponent-controlled way found to suspend Blanc on the opponent's turn; no ruling is needed to interpret it.
- No Q&A entries exist for this card, so nothing is claimed about corner cases the rules team has not published.

### BT23-077 — Sistermon Ciel

#### Card summary

Catalog record (`packages/shared/src/cards/data/cards.json`):

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Name | Sistermon Ciel |
| Colors | White |
| Kinds | Digimon |
| Level / Play cost / DP | 4 / 4 / 4000 |
| Forms | Champion |
| Attributes | Data, Virus |
| Types | Puppet, CS |
| Rarity / Max in deck | R / 4 |
| Evolution costs | none — official "Digivolve Cost 1/2" are both `-` |

Printed clauses:

1. `＜Blocker＞`
2. `[On Play] Delete 1 of your opponent's Digimon with a play cost of 4 or less.`
3. `[All Turns] When this Digimon suspends, ＜De-Digivolve 1＞ 1 of your opponent's Digimon.`
4. `(Rule) Also has Name:[Sistermon Noir] and Trait: [Virus].`

Clause 4 is on the official entry
(`docs/audits/BT23-reaudit/logs/official-bt23.html`) but is **missing from the catalog
`effectText`**. Other printings of this card keep the rule line in the catalog text
(ST12-13: "*Also treat as if name is [Sistermon Noir] and traits include [Virus]").

#### Rulings covered

`node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT23-077` returns "no knowledge-base entries", and
the Knowledge base index section of this document line 83 lists 0 Q&A ids for this card. There is
no card-specific Q&A to cover.

Mechanics were checked against the rules behaviour the engine already encodes:

- An attack declaration suspends by the rules and opens the `whenSuspended` window
  (`apps/api/src/engine/combat/controller.ts:1152-1168`). Blocking suspends the blocker
  through the same combat path, so the clause fires there too.
- A block requires ＜Blocker＞ (§16-5) — `apps/api/src/engine/combat/legality.ts:374`.
- De-Digivolve may target a Digimon with no digivolution cards; it then does nothing
  (`apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/actions/digivolution.ts:16-48`).
- A "trait" is forms union attributes union types (`apps/api/src/engine/cards/cardData.ts:297-316`),
  so the catalog's `attributes: ["Data", "Virus"]` already answers a `[Virus]` trait
  condition. The whole Sistermon Ciel family (BT6-084, BT7-083, ST12-13) is encoded that
  way.

#### Clause -> test -> IR mapping

| Clause | IR | Test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Catalog identity + full IR shape | all three effects | "matches every catalog field and complete compiled clause" |
| `＜Blocker＞` | `Static`, `keywords: [{ keyword: "Blocker" }]` | "projects Blocker through the live continuous ledger" |
| `＜Blocker＞` behaviourally | same | "blocks a declared attack and de-digivolves during the opponent's turn" — attack on the player, `declareBlock`, security untouched, attacker deleted |
| On Play delete, cost <= 4 | `OnPlay` -> `Delete`, `filter: { controller: "opponent", kind: ["Digimon"], playCostLte: 4 }`, `count: 1` | "deletes exactly the play-cost-4 opposing Digimon when played publicly" — BT1-015 (cost 4) dies, BT1-034 (cost 5) lives, memory 6 -> 2 |
| On Play with no legal target | same | "leaves an opposing play-cost-5 Digimon alone and asks nothing" |
| All Turns suspend watcher, own turn | `AllTurns` -> `SubTrigger whenSuspended`, `sourceFilter: { isSelfRef: true }`, `DeDigivolve amount: 1` | "de-digivolves an opposing stack when it suspends to attack" |
| All Turns suspend watcher, opponent turn | same | "blocks a declared attack and de-digivolves during the opponent's turn" (`turnSeat = 1`) |
| Watcher anchored to this card, not the board bus | `sourceFilter.isSelfRef` | "ignores another friendly Digimon suspending" |
| Mandatory effect on a stackless target | `DeDigivolve` | "resolves with no pending decision when the only opposing Digimon has no digivolution cards" |
| (Rule) alternate name + trait | `Rule` -> two `GrantStatic` (`grant: "name"` / `grant: "trait"`) | "carries the official (Rule) alternate name and Virus trait grant" |
| (Rule) alternate name, live | same | "answers to [Sistermon Noir] on the live continuous ledger" |
| (Rule) `[Virus]` trait, behaviourally | same | "is seen by an opposing card's public [Virus] trait condition" — BT14-102 Angemon's When Attacking modal places Ciel at the bottom of its owner's security and leaves the Vaccine BT1-015 alone |

All behavioural tests drive public intents only (`playCard`, `attack`, `declareBlock`).
No `fireTiming` / `fireSubTrigger` injection remains in this file — the previous test
used both, so those cases carried no behavioural weight.

Fixtures use main-deck Digimon only. Security stacks hold BT1-012 Biyomon. The
de-digivolve fixture is BT1-015 Greymon over ST1-02 Biyomon, which is a legal public
route (Red Lv.3, cost 2).

```

  Tests  11 passed (11)
```

Log: `docs/audits/BT23-reaudit/logs/BT23-077-vitest.log`.

```

pnpm exec oxlint <module> <test>        -> clean
pnpm exec oxfmt --check <module> <test> -> all matched files use the correct format
git diff --check                        -> clean
```

#### Mutation checks

Each behavioural assertion was proved load-bearing by breaking the IR and re-running:

| Mutation | Tests that failed |
| --- | --- |
| `playCostLte: 4` -> `5` | catalog/IR shape, "leaves an opposing play-cost-5 Digimon alone" |
| `isSelfRef: true` -> `controllerDefault: true` | catalog/IR shape, "ignores another friendly Digimon suspending" |
| whole `AllTurns` effect deleted | catalog/IR shape, both de-digivolve tests |
| `Blocker` keyword renamed in the module | catalog/IR shape, "projects Blocker through the live continuous ledger" |
| whole `Rule` effect deleted | "carries the official (Rule) ... grant", "answers to [Sistermon Noir] on the live continuous ledger" |

Note on the last row: renaming the keyword in the module does NOT stop the block. The
combat gate falls back to the printed keyword in the catalog effect text
(`hasBlocker` -> `hasPrintedKeyword`, `apps/api/src/engine/combat/legality.ts:89-97`),
so the behavioural block test proves the rule, while the ledger test is what pins the
module's IR. This is engine behaviour, not a defect in this card.

#### Defects

##### Fixed (local)

- Removed `// @ts-nocheck` from `apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-077.ts`. The module
  typechecks without it, so `toDuration`-style silent type errors would now surface.
  `pnpm --filter @aegis/api typecheck` is clean with it removed.
- **Added the missing `(Rule)` effect** to `BT23-077.ts`: `trigger: "Rule"` with two
  `GrantStatic` actions (`grant: "name"`, `tokens: ["Sistermon Noir"]` and
  `grant: "trait"`, `tokens: ["Virus"]`). This is the exact shape BT6-084 Sistermon Ciel
  already uses in `effects.json`; BT23-077 had no `Rule` effect at all, so the alternate
  name did not exist on the board. Proved red (both new rule tests fail with the effect
  removed) then green.
- Replaced the injected-timing test file. The old tests called
  `engine.fireTiming(EffectTiming.OnPlay, ...)` and `advance().fireSubTrigger("whenSuspended", ...)`
  directly, so neither the On Play delete nor the suspend watcher was proved through a
  public path. Both now run through real play and real combat.

##### Catalog gap for the coordinator (not edited)

The catalog `effectText` for BT23-077 omits the official `(Rule) Also has
Name:[Sistermon Noir] and Trait: [Virus].` line. The behaviour is now carried in the
card module, so play is correct, but any consumer reading `effectText` (card viewer,
text search, re-generation of the IR) will not see the rule. **Coordinator action:**
append the rule line to the BT23-077 catalog entry, matching the ST12-13 convention.

The empty `evoCosts` is **not** a gap: the official entry shows "Digivolve Cost 1" and
"Digivolve Cost 2" both as `-`, so this card has no evolution route. Nothing to fix,
and no evolution test is possible.

#### Remaining gaps

- No evolution coverage: the card has no printed digivolve cost, so no route exists.
- No once-per-turn clause on this card, so no same-turn refusal / next-turn reset case
  applies.
- No duration on any effect, so no expiry-boundary case applies.
- The `[Virus]` trait proof is behavioural but not a mutant of this module: the trait is
  already true from the catalog `attributes`, so the module's `grant: "trait"` is
  redundant there (BT6-084 carries the same redundancy). The `grant: "name"` half is the
  part that only the module supplies.
- No card in the catalog references `[Sistermon Noir]` by name, so the alternate name has
  no card-driven public proof. It is proved on the live continuous ledger instead.
- The de-digivolve target choice in the multi-target fixture is steered with
  `preferInstanceIds`; the engine's own free choice among legal opposing Digimon is not
  asserted, because the printed effect leaves that choice to the controller.

### BT23-078 — Gorou Matayoshi

#### Card

Catalog record (`packages/shared/src/cards/data/cards.json`): `cardId` BT23-078, set BT23, Red
Tamer, play cost 3, DP 0, forms `-`, attributes `-`, types `[CS]`, rarity U, max 4 per deck.
`nameEn` is "Gorou Matayoshi", which matches the 2025-10-17 erratum ("Goro Matayoshi" ->
"Gorou Matayoshi"). No catalog discrepancy found.

Printed clauses:

1. `[Start of Your Main Phase] If your opponent has a Digimon, gain 1 memory.`
2. `[Your Turn] When your Digimon are played or digivolve, if any of them have [Avian], [Bird], [Beast], [Animal] or [Sovereign] in any of their traits (other than [Sea Animal]) or the [CS] trait, by returning this Tamer to the hand, 1 of your Digimon gets +3000 DP for the turn. Then, 1 of your Digimon may attack.`
3. `[Security] Play this card without paying the cost.`

The catalog text uses U+00A0 before "in any of their traits" and before "trait"; the catalog test
pins those exact code points.

#### Rulings covered

`node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT23-078`:

- Errata 2025-10-17 (name correction) — covered by the catalog test.
- Q5353: "Can I process the part of the effect after 'then' without meeting the 'by' condition?"
  Answer: no. If you do not return the Tamer, no Digimon may attack. Covered by the decline test.

Rules used from `data/kb/rules/comprehensive.md`:

- CR 2-3-2-3: "with the [XX] trait" references a trait that MATCHES the bracketed text.
- CR 2-3-2-4: "with [XX] in any of its traits" references any trait that INCLUDES the bracketed
  text. This is what the printed [Sea Animal] carve-out exists for.
- CR 3-4-5-8: effects that do not name the breeding area do not reach it.

#### Clause -> test -> IR

| Clause | IR | Test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Catalog + errata + clause count | n/a | "matches every catalog field, the 2025-10-17 name erratum and the full compiled clause set" |
| Trait gate + cost/attack shape | `SubTrigger.sourceFilter.or`, `ModifyDP`, `Attack` | "compiles the trait gate with CR 2-3-2-4 substring reach and an exact [CS] branch" |
| 1. Start of Your Main Phase | `StartOfYourMainPhase` -> `GainMemory{1, opponentHas Digimon}` | real turn loop (`advance().runTurn`): +1 with an opponent Digimon; no gain with only an opponent Tamer; no gain on the opponent's turn |
| 2. play trigger + cost + DP + attack | `SubTrigger{whenPlayed}` | play BT1-012 Biyomon ([Bird]) with `playCard`: Tamer in hand, ally 3000 -> 6000, ally suspended, opponent security 3 -> 2, memory 10 -> 7 |
| 2. Q5353 refusal | `abortOnDecline: true` on the cost-gated `ModifyDP` | decline branch: Tamer still in the battle area, ally still 3000, nothing suspended, security still 3 |
| 2. CR 2-3-2-4 substring reach | `match: "traitContains"` | BT1-014 Kokatorimon ([Giant Bird]) fires the clause |
| 2. [Sea Animal] carve-out | `excludeNameOrTrait [Sea Animal]` | BT1-033 Dolphmon ([Sea Animal]) does not fire it |
| 2. exact [CS] branch | `match: "trait"` on `[CS]` | BT23-023 Whamon ([Sea Animal]/[CS]) fires it; Whamon 9000 -> 12000 |
| 2. digivolve trigger | `SubTrigger{whenOneOfYoursDigivolves}` | public `digivolve` BT1-049 Labramon -> BT12-036 Mikemon: memory 10 -> 8, Mikemon 4000 -> 7000, Tamer in hand |
| 2. controller/turn boundary | `trigger: "YourTurn"` | the turn player plays a [Bird] Digimon while the opponent holds Gorou: Gorou stays in play, its ally stays at 3000, nothing suspends |
| 3. Security | `Security` -> `PlayWithoutCost{payCost:false}` | attack into the security stack holding it: it enters its owner's battle area, security 1 -> 0, memory unchanged, not trashed |

#### Defects found and fixed

All three fixes are local to `BT23-078.ts`, and each was proved red before green.

1. **Trait gate matched exactly instead of by substring.** The five tokens used
   `match: "trait"` (exact trait equality, `engine/effects/interpreter/matching/definition.ts`
   ~356). The printed wording is "in any of their traits", which CR 2-3-2-4 defines as
   substring containment; the printed "(other than [Sea Animal])" exists only because
   `[Sea Animal]` contains "Animal". Under the old IR, every compound trait — `[Giant Bird]`,
   `[Holy Beast]`, `[Dark Animal]` — silently failed the gate. Changed to
   `match: "traitContains"`. Red proof: reverting the token to `match: "trait"` fails
   "reads [Giant Bird] as [Bird] per CR 2-3-2-4" and the IR shape test. Second red proof:
   with `traitContains` in place but the `excludeNameOrTrait` line deleted, Dolphmon wrongly
   fires the clause, so the carve-out is now load-bearing rather than decorative.
   The `[CS]` branch stays `match: "trait"`, which is CR 2-3-2-3's "with the [XX] trait".

2. **"1 of your Digimon" reached the breeding area.** `ModifyDP` and `Attack` used a zone-less
   `{ controller: "mine", kind: ["Digimon"] }` filter, so the breeding-area Digimon was a legal
   pick for the DP boost and the forced attack. Added `zone: "battleArea"` to both (CR 3-4-5-8).

3. **`// @ts-nocheck` hid the IR types.** Removed. The module now typechecks, which required
   typing the `whenPlayed`/`whenOneOfYoursDigivolves` map callback as
   `(event: SubTriggerEvent): Action`. This is the guard that catches silent `duration` and
   filter-key errors (the BT23-047 class of bug).

The old test suite used `advance().fireSubTrigger(...)` for every behavioral claim, so it proved
only that the watcher body runs, not that a real play or digivolve arms it. All behavioral tests
now go through public intents (`playCard`, `digivolve`, `attack`) and the real turn loop.

#### Peer impact (not edited — outside this lane)

The same "in any of their traits (other than [Sea Animal])" wording is compiled with the exact

### BT23-079 — Eri Karan

#### Card summary

Catalog record (`packages/shared/src/cards/data/cards.json`): Blue Tamer, play cost 3, types
`App Driver` / `Appmon`, rarity R, max 4 per deck. The catalog text matches the official card
list. No discrepancy to report.

Printed clauses:

1. `[Start of Your Main Phase] If your opponent has a Digimon, gain 1 memory.`
2. `[Your Turn] When any of your Digimon get linked, by suspending this Tamer, those Digimon
   get +3000 DP until your opponent's turn ends.`
3. `Then, 1 of your Digimon may app fuse into a Digimon card in the hand.`
4. `[Security] Play this card without paying the cost.`

#### Rulings covered

`node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT23-079` returns one Q&A.

| Q&A | Ruling | Test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Q5354 | The part after "then" cannot be performed without paying the suspend cost. | `Q5354: declining the suspend cost aborts both the DP boost and the App Fuse` |

#### Clause to test to IR mapping

| Clause | IR | Test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Catalog identity and clause inventory | `compiled.effects` triggers `StartOfYourMainPhase`, `YourTurn`, `Security` | `matches every catalog field and complete compiled clause` |
| 1 | `GainMemory` amount 1 under `opponentHas` filter `kind:["Digimon"]` | `gains exactly 1 memory at the start of its own Main phase when the opponent has a Digimon`; `does not gain memory on the opponent's Main phase even with an opposing Digimon` |
| 2 | `YourTurn` -> `SubTrigger whenLinked` (`sourceFilter` controller `mine`) -> `ModifyDP` +3000, `duration: untilOpponentTurnEnd`, `cost: suspend` on `isSelfRef` | `suspends Eri and boosts the naturally linked host by exactly 3000 through the opponent's turn`; `does not fire for the opponent's own link on the opponent's turn`; `ignores a link whose subject is an opponent Digimon`; `cannot pay the suspend cost when Eri is already suspended, so nothing happens` |
| 2 target identity | `target.sourceRef: "triggerSubject"`, count 1 | `targets the linked Digimon itself and keeps the App Fuse tail behind the suspend cost` |
| 3 | `AppFuse` from `hand`, `optional: true`, sequenced after `ModifyDP` with `abortOnDecline: true` | `accepting the suspend then app fuses the boosted host into the hand Digimon and draws`; Q5354 decline test |
| 4 | `Security` effect `PlayWithoutCost` on `isSelfRef`, `payCost: false` | `plays itself from Security without paying its cost of 3` |

#### Behavioral endpoints proved

- Clause 1 uses the real turn loop (`startTurnLoop` + `waitForMainPhase`). Memory at Main open
  is exactly one higher than the identical fixture with an empty opposing battle area, and the
  clause stays silent while seat 1 holds the turn.
- Clause 2 uses the public `linkCard` intent. Musclemon (1000 DP) linked with Perorimon
  (2000 link DP) reads 6000 DP with Eri suspended; the vanilla bystander is unchanged; memory
  moves 5 -> 4 for the link declaration only. The boost still reads 6000 during the opponent's
  Main phase and falls to 3000 once that turn ends, pinning the exact `untilOpponentTurnEnd`
  boundary.
- Controller boundary: an opponent linking their own Digimon on their own turn leaves Eri
  unsuspended and the host at link DP only. A `whenLinked` subject controlled by the opponent
  does nothing.
- Cost failure: an already-suspended Eri produces no boost, no fusion and no `digivolved`
  event, while the link itself still succeeds.
- Q5354: with the suspend declined, DP stays at 3000, Eri stays unsuspended, the host top card
  is unchanged, the hand still holds the fusion result, hand size drops by exactly the linked
  card, and no `digivolved` event is emitted (so no App Fusion procedure draw either).
- Clause 3: accepting the suspend and the fuse offer, then declining Dosukomon's own When
  Digivolving Link, moves the host top to the exact Dosukomon hand instance, leaves
  `stack = [old top, consumed link]`, empties `linked`, emits
  `digivolved / mechanic: appFusion / cardId: BT23-021`, charges no extra memory (memory 4)
  and draws exactly one card (`BT1-010`).
- Clause 4: a real opponent attack drives the security check. Eri lands in the battle area, is
  not trashed, security empties, and the memory line matches a control run whose revealed
  security card (BT1-009) has no security effect. The control run is the free-of-charge
  baseline, so the cost-3 Tamer demonstrably paid nothing.

No test uses `advance().fireSubTrigger` for a case a public intent can reach; the two
remaining injected-timing uses are the opponent-controlled `whenLinked` subject and the
opponent-turn `OnStartMainPhase` refire, neither of which has a public route.

#### Defect found and fixed

`apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-079.ts` opened with `// @ts-nocheck`, which suppressed type
checking for the whole IR module (the risk called out in the brief for `duration:` strings and
`EffectDurationRef`). The directive was removed. `pnpm --filter @aegis/api typecheck` compiles
the module cleanly, so `duration: "untilOpponentTurnEnd"` is a real `EffectDurationRef` and not
a silent fallback to end-of-each-turn — which the behavioral expiry test independently confirms.

#### Verification

- `pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec vitest run src/cards/BT23/BT23-079.test.ts --maxWorkers=1
  --no-file-parallelism`: **1 file / 11 tests passed**.
- Peer/stack run:
  `pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec vitest run src/cards/BT23/BT23-079.test.ts
  src/cards/BT23/BT23-021.test.ts src/engine/appFusion.test.ts src/engine/linkState.test.ts
  --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism`: **4 files / 56 tests passed, 17.86s**
  (`logs/BT23-079-focused.log`). The App Fusion backend suites and BT23-021's Eri-driven
  App Fusion cases stay green.
- `pnpm --filter @aegis/api typecheck`: no error in `BT23-079.ts` or `BT23-079.test.ts`.
  Unrelated pre-existing errors in `BT23-078.test.ts` and `BT23-081.test.ts` belong to other
  lanes.
- `pnpm exec oxlint` on both files: clean. `pnpm exec oxfmt`: formatted.

#### Remaining gaps

- No test drives an App Fusion where two eligible linked materials force a choice from Eri's
  own route; BT23-021's suite already covers that selection prompt against the same primitive.
- The "those Digimon" plural wording is encoded as `count: 1` on the trigger subject. A single
  link event has one subject, so no fixture distinguishes the two encodings; a card that links
  several Digimon at once would be needed.
- Delivery gates (client transport, App Fusion overlay integration) are open per
  `APP-FUSION-MECHANISM.md` and are coordinator-owned.

#### Score

Catalog/rules 2/2; IR trace 2/2; behavior 2/2; peer/stack 2/2; delivery gates 0/2:
**8/10 worker-ready**.

### BT23-080 — Yu Nogi

#### Card

Catalog: `packages/shared/src/cards/data/cards.json` (`cardId: BT23-080`). Name Yu Nogi. Colors Blue/Yellow. Kind Tamer. Play cost 4. DP 0. Trait `[CS]`. Rarity U.

Printed clauses:

1. `[Start of Your Main Phase] If your opponent has a Digimon, gain 1 memory.`
2. `[All Turns] When any of your Digimon with the [CS] trait would be deleted, by returning this Tamer to the bottom of the deck, place 1 of those Digimon as the top security card.`
3. `[Security] Play this card without paying the cost.`

No catalog discrepancy. The catalog text uses non-breaking spaces, so the test compares on normalized whitespace.

#### Rulings covered

| Id | Summary | Coverage |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Q5355 | An opponent's `<Piercing>` attacker battles my `[CS]` Digimon. Clause 2 places it as top security instead of deleting it. Does `<Piercing>` still trigger? **No** — the deletion never happens. | Behavioral. A real seat-1 attack with BT1-026 Breakdramon (vanilla `<Piercing>`, 11000 DP) into a suspended BT23-006 Huckmon. Accept branch: no `securityChecked` event, defender's security grows by the Huckmon card. Decline branch (control): the same attack deletes Huckmon and `<Piercing>` checks exactly one security card. |

#### Clause -> test -> IR map

| Clause | IR | Tests |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | `StartOfYourMainPhase` -> `GainMemory{amount:1, condition: opponentHas{controllerDefault: opponent, kind:[Digimon], zone: battleArea}}` | `gains start-of-main memory only when the …` (both branches, real turn loop, memory 1 vs 0); `ignores an opponent Digimon that is only in the breeding area`; `does not gain memory at the start of the opponent's main phase` |
| 2 | `AllTurns` -> `Replacement{event: wouldBeDeleted, sourceFilter{controller: mine, kind:[Digimon], zone: battleArea, nameOrTrait CS}}` -> `SecurityManipulation{placeAsSecurity, toTop, source.sourceRef: triggerSubject, cost: return self to deckBottom, optional, abortOnDecline}` | `Q5355: replaces battle deletion …`; `declining the cost leaves Yu in play …`; `ignores a deleted Digimon without the [CS] trait` |
| 3 | `Security` -> `PlayWithoutCost{isSelfRef, payCost: false}` | `plays itself from security without paying its 4 cost` |
| all | shape | `carries the three printed clauses as IR` |

Endpoints asserted in the Q5355 test: Huckmon's card instance id is `security[0]` and the pre-attack security order follows it; Huckmon is not in trash; no `securityChecked` event; Yu's instance id is the **last** deck card and the deck grew by one; Breakdramon is still on the battle area and suspended; `pendingDecision` is undefined.

#### Defects found and fixed

Both fixes are local to `BT23-080.ts` and were proven red first.

1. **Clause 1 counted breeding-area Digimon.** The `opponentHas` filter had no `zone`, so a lone opponent Digimon in the breeding area granted the memory. Comprehensive rules 3-4-5-8 says information on breeding-area cards can't be referenced. Added `zone: "battleArea"`. Red before the fix (memory 1, expected 0), green after — test `ignores an opponent Digimon that is only in the breeding area`.
2. **Clause 2's replacement had no zone bound.** Same zone-less filter on `sourceFilter`, so a `[CS]` Digimon in the breeding area would have armed the replacement. Comprehensive rules 3-4-5-3 says breeding-area cards aren't affected by effects. Added `zone: "battleArea"`. Structural fix only — see gaps.
3. **Removed `// @ts-nocheck`** from the module. It typechecks clean without it, so future `duration`/filter type errors will surface.

The previous test file was replaced. It proved clause 2 only through `advance().verb.deletePermanent(...)` and clause 1 through an injected `fireTiming` cast, so it earned no behavioral credit and never exercised Q5355.

#### Commands and results

- `pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec vitest run src/cards/BT23/BT23-080.test.ts --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism` -> 1 file passed, **10 tests passed**, 7.92s. Log: `docs/audits/BT23-reaudit/logs/BT23-080-final.log`.
- `pnpm --filter @aegis/api typecheck` -> no errors in `BT23-080.ts` or `BT23-080.test.ts`. Pre-existing failures elsewhere (`src/engine/combat/controller.ts`, `BT23-078.test.ts`, `BT23-081.test.ts`, `immunityAffectation.test.ts`) belong to other lanes.
- `pnpm exec oxlint` -> clean. `pnpm exec oxfmt` -> formatted.

#### Remaining gaps

- The breeding-area bound on clause 2 has no behavioral test: no public intent deletes a breeding-area Digimon, and a verb-driven test would be structural only.
- Simultaneous deletion of two `[CS]` Digimon ("place **1** of those Digimon") is untested. The IR resolves per trigger subject, so the printed choice is not modelled as a single pick across the group. No ruling forces a decision here, so it is recorded rather than changed.
- Clause 2 is not once-per-turn, but Yu leaves play as its own cost, so a repeat is only reachable with a second copy. Not tested.
- Clause 3 does not assert Yu's own `[Start of Your Main Phase]` firing on the turn after it lands from security.

| Delivery gates | 0 | Coordinator-owned. |

### BT23-081 — Chitose Imai

Sources: committed catalog `BT23-081`; official BT23 card list (`logs/official-bt23.html`, entry BT23-081). KB: `node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT23-081` returns no entries, and `KB-INDEX.md` lists 0 Q&A ids for this card, so there is no card-specific ruling to cover.

#### Card

Yellow Tamer, play cost 4, traits Hudie/CS, SR. Catalog text matches the official list character for character.

Printed clauses:

1. `[Start of Your Main Phase] If you have a Digimon with the [CS] trait, gain 1 memory.`
2. `[On Play] You may play 1 play cost 5 or lower Digimon card with the [Hudie] trait from your hand without paying the cost.`
3. `[All Turns] When any of your [Hudie] trait Digimon suspend, by suspending this Tamer, 1 of your opponent's Digimon gets -3000 DP for the turn.`
4. `[Security] Play this card without paying the cost.`

#### Clause -> test -> IR plan

| Clause | IR | Test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | `StartOfYourMainPhase` -> `GainMemory 1` with `youHave` filter `kind:["Digimon"]`, trait `CS` | Real turn loop reaches own Main: memory rises by 1 with a CS Digimon on the battle area; no gain with no CS Digimon; **no gain when the only CS Digimon is in the breeding area** (comprehensive §3-4-5-8) |
| 2 | `OnPlay` -> `PlayWithoutCost` `playCostLte:5`, trait `Hudie`, `from:["hand"]`, `optional:true` | Public `playCard` of Chitose: pays exactly 4 memory, a cost-3 Hudie leaves hand for the battle area for free, a cost-8 Hudie and a non-Hudie stay in hand; separate decline branch leaves every hand card in place |
| 3 | `AllTurns` -> `SubTrigger whenSuspended` (source `mine` Digimon w/ `Hudie`) -> `ModifyDP -3000 forTheTurn` with self-suspend cost, `optional`, `abortOnDecline` | Public attack suspends an own Hudie: Chitose becomes suspended and the sole opposing Digimon drops 3000 DP; decline branch leaves both untouched; non-Hudie attacker fires nothing; an already-suspended Chitose cannot pay; duration expires at the real turn boundary |
| 4 | `Security` -> `PlayWithoutCost` self, `payCost:false`, `isSecurity:true` | Public attack into a security stack whose top card is Chitose: she enters the defender's battle area for free with memory unchanged by any play cost |

#### Suspected defect (probe first)

`youHave` in clause 1 carries no `zone`. `countMatching` (`apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/scaling.ts:95-101`) scans the battle area **and** the breeding area when `zone` is absent, so a CS Digimon sitting in breeding would wrongly satisfy the condition. Comprehensive §3-4-5-8 says breeding-area cards are not referenced unless the effect names the breeding area. Expected fix: add `zone: "battleArea"`. Prove red before, green after.

#### Defects found and fixed

**1. Start-of-Main condition counted the breeding area (fixed in `BT23-081.ts`).**
The `youHave` filter carried no `zone`. `countMatching` (`apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/scaling.ts:95-101`) falls through to a scan of the battle area *and* the breeding permanent when `zone` is absent, so a level-3 `[CS]` Digimon parked in breeding granted the memory. Comprehensive rules 3-4-5-8 say breeding-area cards are not referenced unless the effect names the breeding area. Fix: `zone: "battleArea"` on the condition filter.

Red-then-green proof, test `ignores a CS Digimon that is only in the breeding area`:
- Without the fix: `pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec vitest run src/cards/BT23/BT23-081.test.ts --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism -t 'breeding'` — 1 failed (breeding fixture reached Main with 1 memory, the empty control with 0).
- With the fix: same command — 1 passed; both fixtures reach Main with the same memory.

**2. Removed `// @ts-nocheck` from the module.** It typechecks clean without it, so `toDuration`-style silent duration mistakes now surface. `duration: "forTheTurn"` is a real `EffectDurationRef` member (`packages/shared/src/effects/ir/durations.ts:4`), so clause 3's duration is correct.

#### Behavioral evidence

All twelve tests drive public intents (`playCard`, `attack`, `endPhase`, `surrender`) or the production turn loop. No test uses `advance().fireTiming` or `advance().fireSubTrigger`; the previous suite drove clauses 1 and 3 entirely through injected timing, which earned no behavioral credit.

- **Clause 1.** Two turn-loop fixtures reach seat 0's Main through `startTurnLoop()`; the CS fixture leaves exactly one more memory than the identical non-CS fixture. A third pair proves the breeding-only case matches the empty board. The helper closes the Breeding phase with a public `endPhase` when a seeded breeding permanent holds it open.
- **Clause 2.** Chitose is played publicly from hand: memory falls 6 -> 2 (her printed 4), the cost-3 Hudie (BT23-037 Tentomon) reaches the battle area, and the cost-8 Hudie (BT23-032) plus the non-Hudie (BT1-028) stay in hand by exact instance id. The `playCostLte: 5` and trait bounds are therefore both exercised in one fixture. A decline branch under `autoDeclineOptional` leaves the eligible Hudie in hand and the battle area at length 1, with memory still 2.
- **Clause 3.** A public attack by an own Hudie (BT23-037) onto a suspended BT1-024 suspends Chitose and drops the target from 10000 to 7000. Three negatives: the decline branch leaves Chitose unsuspended at 10000; a non-Hudie attacker (BT1-028) fires nothing; an already-suspended Chitose cannot pay, so the target stays at 10000. A turn-loop test proves the reduction expires at the real turn boundary — 7000 during seat 0's Main, back to 10000 after a public `endPhase` reaches seat 1's Main, with the same physical permanent id.
- **Clause 4.** A public attack into a security stack topped by Chitose puts her exact security instance into the defender's battle area, not the trash, and removes it from security.
- **IR trace.** A final structural test pins all four compiled clauses, including the new `zone: "battleArea"`, the `playCostLte: 5` bound, the `whenSuspended` source filter, and the self-suspend cost with `abortOnDecline`.

Every endpoint assertion also checks `state.pendingDecision === undefined` and, where a battle ran, `observe(engine).isAttacking() === false`.

#### Commands and results

- `pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec vitest run src/cards/BT23/BT23-081.test.ts --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism` — 1 file / 12 tests passed, 38.16s. Log: `logs/BT23-081-focused.log`.
- `pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` — no `BT23-081` diagnostics. Pre-existing failures in `src/cards/BT23/BT23-078.test.ts`, `src/engine/effects/immunityAffectation.test.ts` and `src/engine/combat/controller.ts` belong to other lanes.
- `pnpm exec oxlint apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-081.ts apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-081.test.ts` — clean.
- `pnpm exec oxfmt --check` on the same two files — clean.
- `git diff --check` — clean.

#### Remaining gaps

- No KB Q&A exists for this card, so no ruling id is left uncovered.
- Clause 3 is not proven against a suspension caused by an **opponent** effect during the opponent's turn. `[All Turns]` should also fire there. No BT23 fixture in reach suspends an opposing Digimon through a public intent alone, so this path is untested rather than proven wrong. The IR's `sourceFilter.controller: "mine"` resolves to the source's owner seat (`interpreter/matching/permanent.ts:127-141`), independent of the turn player, so the code side looks right.
- The free-play target selection is exercised with exactly one eligible card. A multi-eligible choice is left to the shared `PlayWithoutCost` selection primitive.
- No retained red or skip. No engine change was needed.

#### Catalog check

Catalog `BT23-081` matches the official BT23 list entry field for field (colors, kinds, cost 4, DP -, types Hudie/CS, both text blocks). The runtime `effectText` differs from the raw JSON only by typographic whitespace, so the test compares the printed clauses on collapsed whitespace. No catalog edit is needed.

#### Card summary

Printed clauses:

1. `[Start of Your Main Phase] If your opponent has a Digimon, gain 1 memory.`
2. `[Your Turn] When any of your Digimon digivolve into a Digimon with the [Beastkin], [Holy Beast], [Cherub] or [CS] trait, by returning this Tamer to the hand, you may play 1 [Lopmon] or level 3 Digimon card with the [CS] trait from your hand without paying the cost.`
3. `[Security] Play this card without paying the cost.`

#### Rulings

`node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT23-082` returns `(no knowledge-base entries)`. There is no Q&A, errata or restriction to cover. The audit therefore rests on the printed text plus the comprehensive-rules behaviour of bracketed card names (exact-name matching, the rule the module violated).

#### Clause -> test -> IR mapping

| Clause | IR | Test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Catalog identity and full text | `coverage: "full"`, `residual: []` | `matches every catalog field and printed text` |
| Bracketed `[Lopmon]` is an exact name | `nameOrTrait: [{ tokens: ["Lopmon"], match: "nameExact" }]`, `orFilters[0].levels: [3]` | `compiles the bracketed [Lopmon] reference as an exact-name match` |
| 1 — gain 1 memory, opponent has a Digimon | `trigger: "StartOfYourMainPhase"` + `GainMemory` gated by `condition.kind: "opponentHas"` | `gains 1 memory at the start of its controller's main phase while the opponent has a Digimon` |
| 1 — condition is real | same | `gains no memory when the opponent controls no Digimon` |
| 1 — "Your" main phase only | `StartOfYourMainPhase` owner gate | `stays silent at the opponent's own start of main phase` |
| 2 — trigger + return cost + free `[Lopmon]` play | `SubTrigger whenOneOfYoursDigivolves` -> `PlayWithoutCost` with `cost: { kind: "return", to: "hand", isSelf: true }` | `returns itself to the hand to play a [Lopmon] for free after a CS digivolution` |
| 2 — "you may" refusal branch | `optional: true`, `abortOnDecline: true` | `keeps the Tamer on the board and the card in hand when the option is declined` |
| 2 — level 3 `[CS]` alternative | `orFilters[0]: { levels: [3], nameOrTrait: [{ tokens: ["CS"], match: "trait" }] }` | `plays a level 3 Digimon with the [CS] trait and leaves an unrelated level 3 in hand` |
| 2 — level bound on the trait branch | same | `refuses a level 4 [CS] Digimon: only level 3 qualifies through the trait branch` |
| 2 — exact-name bound on the name branch | `match: "nameExact"` | `refuses [Lopmon (X Antibody)]: the bracketed name is exact, not a substring` |
| 2 — trait gate on the digivolution | `sourceFilter.nameOrTrait` with `match: "trait"` | `does not fire for a digivolution into a Digimon without a listed trait` |
| 3 — free security play | `trigger: "Security"`, `isSecurity: true`, `PlayWithoutCost` self | `plays itself for free from security` |
| Peer/stack | — | `fires off BT23-026 Lopmon's Makiko-gated route into [Antylamon]` |

Every behavioural test drives public intents (`digivolve`, `attack`, `endPhase`, `surrender`) or the real turn loop (`startTurnLoop()` + `advance(...).waitForMainPhase(seat)`). The previous suite used the injected `advance(...).fireSubTrigger` and `engine.fireTiming` seams; those calls are gone.

Endpoint assertions pin physical instance ids in the destination zone, memory after the cost, hand contents, the digivolution stack, the drawn card, the empty security stack, `pendingDecision === undefined`, and `observe(engine).isAttacking() === false` where a battle was involved.

#### Defects found and fixed (local, in `BT23-082.ts`)

1. **`[Lopmon]` matched as a substring.** The target filter compiled the bracketed name with `match: "name"`, which `matchNameOrTrait` (`apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/matching/definition.ts:352`) implements as `name.includes(token)`. `BT16-067 Lopmon (X Antibody)` therefore qualified. Fixed to `match: "nameExact"`. Proof: `refuses [Lopmon (X Antibody)]` was red before the fix (the X Antibody card was played from hand onto the battle area) and green after.
2. **`// @ts-nocheck` and an `as any` cast removed.** The module now typechecks unmodified; `tsc --noEmit` reports no error for either BT23-082 file. Removing the suppression is what the brief asks so that bad `duration:` strings and similar errors surface. This module has no `duration:` field.

No change was needed for the trait gate: `sourceFilter` on a `whenOneOfYoursDigivolves` watcher is evaluated against the event subject, which is the resulting permanent, so the printed "digivolve **into** a Digimon with the ... trait" is enforced correctly. `match: "trait"` is exact trait equality, so `CS` cannot leak onto a different trait.

#### Commands and results

- `pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec vitest run src/cards/BT23/BT23-082.test.ts --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism` — 1 file, 13 tests passed, 4.2 s. Log: `logs/BT23-082-focused.log`.
- `pnpm exec tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` (from `apps/api`) — no diagnostic mentions BT23-082. The pre-existing engine-lane failure in `src/engine/combat/controller.ts` is unrelated and untouched.
- `pnpm exec oxlint apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-082.ts apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-082.test.ts` — clean.
- `pnpm exec oxfmt` — both files formatted; `git diff --check` clean.

#### Remaining gaps

- No KB entries exist for this card, so no ruling-specific behaviour could be cross-checked.
- The `[Your Turn]` window is proven for clause 1 (the opponent's start of main is silent) but not for clause 2: making one of your Digimon digivolve during the opponent's turn needs a third card's effect, which is outside this lane's editable files. The IR carries no explicit `turnScope` on the watcher; a coordinator-owned check of whether `trigger: "YourTurn"` installs the turn gate on the sub-trigger would close this.
- The free play is not proven to bypass a play-cost restriction or colour requirement; the printed clause only says "without paying the cost", and the memory endpoint of 0 after a 3-cost digivolution already proves no cost was charged.
- The peer test leans on BT23-026's granted route, which another lane owns. If that grant changes, the memory endpoint of 0 in that one test moves with it.

### BT23-083 — Fei

#### Card summary

Green/Black Tamer, play cost 4. Traits: Zaxon, CS.

Printed clauses:

1. `[Start of Your Main Phase] If you have a Digimon with the [Royal Base] or [CS] trait, gain 1 memory.`
2. `[All Turns] When cards are placed face up in your security stack, if any of them have the [Zaxon] or [Royal Base] trait, by suspending this Tamer, gain 1 memory. Then, if you have 7 or fewer cards in your hand, <Draw 1>.`
3. `[Security] Play this card without paying the cost.`

Catalog text matches the compiled IR. No catalog discrepancy. BT23-042's report uses Fei as its
"[Royal Base] in text, not in traits" fixture; that remains true after this audit — Fei's traits
are still `Zaxon`/`CS` and only its effect text names `[Royal Base]`.

#### Rulings covered

- **Q5356** — the part after "then" cannot be processed without the "by" condition. Proved by
  the decline test: refusing the suspend yields no memory and no `<Draw 1>`, and Fei stays
  unsuspended. The already-suspended test proves the same gate from the other side: an
  unpayable cost resolves nothing.

#### Clause -> test -> IR mapping

| Clause | Test | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Catalog identity, all three clauses | "matches every catalog field and complete compiled clause" | whole `compiled` |
| 1, positive | "gains 1 memory at the start of its controller's main phase with a [CS] Digimon out" | `StartOfYourMainPhase` / `GainMemory` |
| 1, trait gate | "gains no start-of-main memory without a [Royal Base]/[CS] Digimon" | `condition.youHave.nameOrTrait` |
| 1, zone gate | "does not count a [CS] Digimon in the breeding area (CR 3-4-5-3)" | `condition.youHave.filter.zone` |
| 1, controller/turn gate | same positive test asserts no gain during the opponent's Main phase | trigger side |
| 2, full resolution | "suspends Fei, gains 1 memory and draws when a [Zaxon] card is placed face up in your security" | `SubTrigger whenAddSecurity` -> `Suspend` -> `GainMemory` -> `Draw` |
| 2, Q5356 refusal | "gains no memory and no draw when the suspend is declined" | `optional: true`, `abortOnDecline: true` |
| 2, unpayable cost | "gains nothing when Fei is already suspended" | `Suspend` cost |
| 2, hand boundary | "gains memory but does not draw with 8 cards in hand" | `zoneCount hand lte 7` |
| 2, trait gate | "ignores a face-up add whose card has neither [Zaxon] nor [Royal Base]" | `triggerAddedSecurityHasTrait` |
| 2, controller gate | "ignores a face-up [Zaxon] add to the OPPONENT's security stack" | `triggerSecurityIsYours` |
| 3 | "plays itself for free when checked from security by an opponent's attack" | `Security` / `PlayWithoutCost` |

Every case runs on public intents: `playCard` (BT23-086 Yuugo places a [Zaxon] Digimon face up
as the bottom security card; BT22-100 Cyberspace EDEN places itself, [CS] only), `attack` for
the security check, `endPhase`/`surrender` and `startTurnLoop` + `advance().waitForMainPhase`
for the phase trigger. Endpoints asserted: exact memory for the acting seat after every effect
settles, security stack contents with instance ids and face-up flags, Fei's `isSuspended`, hand
membership by instance id for the draw and the paid security card, and the battle-area identity
of the security-played Fei.

#### Defects found and fixed

- **`youHave` counted the breeding area.** `BT23-083.ts`'s start-of-main condition had a
  zone-less filter, so a `[CS]` Digimon in the breeding area satisfied "if you have a Digimon
  with the [Royal Base] or [CS] trait". Comprehensive Rules 3-4-5-3 and 3-4-5-8 say breeding-area
  cards can't be referenced by an effect that does not name the breeding area. Fixed locally by
  adding `zone: "battleArea"`. Proved red-then-green: without the fix the breeding test reads 4
  memory instead of 3.
- **`// @ts-nocheck` removed** from `BT23-083.ts`. The module typechecks without it, so type
  errors in this card now surface (per the set-wide `EffectDurationRef` lesson).
- **The previous test file was structural.** It reached past the seam into
  `engine.primitives.addSecurity(...)` and `engine.fireTiming(...)`, so it never proved the
  clauses through any route a player can take, and never touched the `[Security]` clause. It is
  replaced.

#### Remaining gaps

- The `[All Turns]` label is proved only on the controller's own turn: no fixture grows the
  controller's security face up during the opponent's turn. The available public route for that
  is BT23-015's `[On Deletion]` self-place after an opponent's attack, which needs an 11000-DP
  board to set up.
- Multi-card face-up adds (several cards placed at once, only some matching) are untested — no
  implemented card places more than one card face up in one operation.
- The `[Security]` clause is proved with an unopposed player attack; it is not proved after a
  security battle or with a full stack beneath it.


1 file, 11 tests, all passing, 6.13s (log: `logs/BT23-083-vitest.log`).

(`EffectDuration.ForTheTurn`), which belongs to the engine lane.

```
pnpm exec oxfmt --check apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-083.ts apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-083.test.ts
```

existing style across the set.

### BT23-084 — Erika Mishima

Green Tamer, play cost 4, traits [Hudie] and [CS].

#### Printed clauses

| # | Clause |
| --- | --- |
| C1 | [Security] Play this card without paying the cost. |
| C2 | [Start of Your Main Phase] If you have a Digimon with the [CS] trait, gain 1 memory. |
| C3 | [End of Your Turn] By suspending this Tamer and returning 1 of your Digimon with the [Hudie] trait to the hand, you may play 1 level 3 Digimon card with the [CS] trait from your hand to your empty breeding area without paying the cost. |
| C4 | Inherited [Your Turn] While this Digimon is [Hudiemon], [Eater Legion] or [Eater EDEN], it gains ＜Alliance＞. |

Source: `packages/shared/src/cards/data/cards.json`.

#### Rulings

`node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT23-084` returns three entries.

| Q&A | Subject | Coverage |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Q5357 | A Digimon played into the breeding area by C3 does not trigger its [On Play]. | Covered behaviorally. |
| Q5257 | Hudiemon carrying Erika: two ＜Alliance＞ instances plus [When Attacking] resolve together; a DNA digivolve into [Shakkoumon] arrives unsuspended, so the 2nd ＜Alliance＞ can suspend it; the 1st instance's DP bonus survives the DNA digivolve. | Partly covered: Erika's own contribution (the inherited ＜Alliance＞ on a [Hudiemon] carrier) is proven. The multi-instance attack sequencing belongs to BT23-101 and BT23-032; see gaps. |
| Q5319 | Duplicate of Q5257 (identical text and answer). | Same as Q5257. |

#### Clause to test to IR mapping

| Clause | Test | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| C1 | `plays itself from the security stack without paying its cost` — seat 0 attacks the player, seat 1's security check plays Erika into seat 1's battle area; asserts the exact instance id is on the board, not in trash or security, security stack drops to 1, memory unchanged. | `trigger: "Security"`, `isSecurity: true`, `PlayWithoutCost` on `isSelfRef` with `payCost: false`. |
| C2 | `gains 1 memory when the Main phase opens with a [CS] Digimon in the battle area` (memory 1), `does not gain memory without a [CS] Digimon` (memory 0), `does not count a [CS] Digimon in the breeding area, per comprehensive rules 3-4-5-8` (memory 0). All three run the real turn loop and read memory when seat 0's Main phase opens. | `trigger: "StartOfYourMainPhase"`, `GainMemory` amount 1 gated by `youHave` on kind Digimon, trait [CS], `zone: "battleArea"`. |
| C3 | `pays both costs at the end of the turn and plays a level 3 [CS] Digimon into the empty breeding area`, `declining the optional pays neither cost and plays nothing`, `pays nothing when the breeding area is occupied`, `pays nothing without a [Hudie] Digimon to return`, `does not play a level 3 Digimon without the [CS] trait`. Each drives a public `endPhase` and asserts suspension, the returned instance id in hand, the played instance id in breeding (never in the battle area), and no pending decision. | `trigger: "EndOfYourTurn"`, `PlayWithoutCost` from hand, `breeding: true`, `requiresEmpty: "breedingArea"`, `optional` + `abortOnDecline`, compound cost of `suspend` (self) and `return` (trait [Hudie]) to hand. |
| C3 / Q5357 | `does not trigger the [On Play] effect of the Digimon played into breeding, per Q5357` — plays BT23-006 Huckmon (whose [On Play] reveals the top 3 cards) and asserts the deck instance order is untouched and hand size is flat. | Same action; the breeding destination suppresses the entry trigger. |
| C4 | `projects inherited ＜Alliance＞ onto BT26-041 / BT23-075`, `grants nothing to the same carrier without Erika in its digivolution cards`, `does not project ＜Alliance＞ onto BT23-073 / BT23-006`, `grants the inherited ＜Alliance＞ only on its controller's turn`. | `trigger: "YourTurn"`, `isInherited: true`, `Aura` granting keyword Alliance on `isSelfRef`, gated by `anyOf [selfHasName ["Hudiemon","Eater Legion","Eater EDEN"]]`. |
| All | `matches every catalog field and the complete compiled clause set` and `compiles the bracketed carrier names as exact-name checks and the costs as one compound payment`. | Structural pins over the whole IR. |

Fixture notes. BT23-101 Hudiemon and BT23-074 Eater Legion print ＜Alliance＞ themselves, so they cannot prove the inherited grant; the positives use BT26-041 Hudiemon (a different card with the same printed name, no printed Alliance) and BT23-075 Eater EDEN. The near-name negative is BT23-073 Eater Bit. Name matching is `selfHasName`, which compares effective names for equality (`interpreter/conditions.ts:506`), matching the printed [Bracketed] wording; `selfHasNameContaining` would be substring matching and is not used.

#### Defect found and fixed

`StartOfYourMainPhase` counted the breeding area. The `youHave` filter carried no `zone`, and `countMatching` (`apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/scaling.ts:95-101`) adds the breeding permanent when the filter is zone-less. A [CS] Digimon sitting in breeding therefore granted the memory. Comprehensive rules 3-4-5-8 says information on cards in breeding areas cannot be referenced, so this was wrong. Fix: `zone: "battleArea"` on the condition filter, in `apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-084.ts`.

Proof: with the fix removed, `does not count a [CS] Digimon in the breeding area, per comprehensive rules 3-4-5-8` and the structural pin both fail (2 failed / 16 passed); with the fix they pass (18 passed).

Also removed `// @ts-nocheck` from the module. It typechecks clean without it, so future `duration` or filter typos now surface.

#### Catalog discrepancy (coordinator-owned, not edited)

BT23-084's `[Security]` clause sits inside `effectText` and the record has no `securityEffectText`. Every other Tamer with the same clause (BT21-081, BT23-078, BT23-082, BT23-083, BT23-085, BT23-086) puts it in `securityEffectText`. Engine behavior is unaffected — the security window is driven by the compiled `trigger: "Security"` effect — but `activeEffectText` for the security resolution reads `securityEffectText` and will be undefined here, and any consumer keying off the field sees BT23-084 as having no security effect. The same record uses a non-breaking space in "…[CS] trait, gain 1 memory." The test asserts over `effectText` plus `securityEffectText` with whitespace normalized, so it stays green after the catalog fix.

#### Commands and results

- `pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec vitest run src/cards/BT23/BT23-084.test.ts --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism` — 1 file, 18 tests passed, 5.26 s. Log: `docs/audits/BT23-reaudit/logs/BT23-084-focused.log`.
- `pnpm --filter @aegis/api typecheck` — no errors in BT23-084 files. Pre-existing failures in `src/cards/BT23/BT23-087.test.ts` and `src/engine/effects/immunityAffectation.test.ts` belong to other lanes.
- `pnpm exec oxlint` on both files — only `no-explicit-any` warnings on the three structural IR casts, matching the exemplar suites (BT23-030).
- `pnpm exec oxfmt --check` on both files — correctly formatted. `git diff --check` clean.

#### Remaining gaps

- Q5257 / Q5319 sequencing is not reproduced end to end: it needs BT23-101's two ＜Alliance＞ instances and its [When Attacking] replay, plus the DNA digivolve into BT23-032 [Shakkoumon] arriving unsuspended and keeping the first Alliance DP bonus. Erika's own contribution to that chain (the inherited ＜Alliance＞ on a [Hudiemon] carrier and being the played Tamer) is proven here; the ordering belongs to the BT23-101 lane, whose report already covers Alliance projection from Erika.
- No test proves the security play resolves under a security check that ALSO trashes other cards, or the interaction with a suppressed security effect.
- No retained reds or skips.

### BT23-085 — Ryuji Mishima

#### Card summary

Catalog record (`packages/shared/src/cards/data/cards.json`):

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Name | Ryuji Mishima |
| Kind | Tamer |
| Color | Black |
| Types | Hudie, CS |
| Play cost | 4 |
| Rarity | SR |

Printed clauses:

1. `[Start of Your Main Phase] If you have a [CS] trait Digimon, gain 1 memory.`
2. `[On Play] Until your opponent's turn ends, their effects can't reduce the DP of 1 of your [Hudie] trait Digimon, and it gains ＜Reboot＞ and ＜Blocker＞.`
3. `[All Turns] When any of your [Hudie] trait Digimon suspend, by suspending this Tamer, you may use 1 single-color [CS] trait Option card from your hand without paying the cost.`
4. `[Security] Play this card without paying the cost.`

The catalog text matches the official card list entry (`docs/audits/BT23-reaudit/logs/official-bt23.html`, line 6789). **No catalog discrepancy.**

#### Rulings covered

`node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT23-085` returns **no knowledge-base entries**, and `KB-INDEX.md` line 91 agrees (0 Q&A ids). Coverage therefore rests on printed text plus comprehensive-rules prose:

- §3-4-5-8 (breeding-area information can't be referenced) — drives the breeding negative on clause 1.
- §15-1-3 (DP-reduction protection) — drives the clause 2 immunity tests.

#### Clause -> test -> IR map

| Clause | IR | Tests |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 Start of Your Main Phase | `StartOfYourMainPhase` -> `GainMemory 1`, gated by `youHave` `{controllerDefault:"mine", kind:["Digimon"], zone:"battleArea", trait CS}` | `gains start-of-main memory only with a battle-area [CS] Digimon` / `... with no [CS] Digimon at all`; `ignores a [CS] Digimon that is only in the breeding area`; `stays silent at the start of the opponent's main phase` |
| 2 On Play | `OnPlay` -> `Restrict dpImmune` (`byOpponentEffectsOnly`, `untilOpponentTurnEnd`) + two `GainKeyword` actions (Reboot, Blocker) bound with `sameTarget` | `grants DP-reduction immunity, ＜Reboot＞ and ＜Blocker＞ to exactly one chosen [Hudie] Digimon`; `blocks the opponent's -3000 DP effect on the protected Digimon but not on its neighbour`; `does not stop the controller's own effect from reducing the protected Digimon's DP`; `lets the granted ＜Blocker＞ block a real attack`; `expires the ＜Reboot＞/＜Blocker＞ grants once the opponent's turn ends` |
| 3 All Turns suspend watcher | `AllTurns` -> `SubTrigger whenSuspended`, `sourceFilter` = own battle-area [Hudie] Digimon, `cost: suspend self`, body `UseOptionWithoutCost` (`from:["hand"]`, `singleColor`, [CS], `payCost:false`, optional) | `suspends Ryuji and uses a single-color [CS] Option for free when a [Hudie] Digimon attacks`; `declining leaves Ryuji unsuspended and the Option in hand`; `ignores the suspension of a Digimon without the [Hudie] trait`; `cannot activate while Ryuji is already suspended`; `does not use an Option without the [CS] trait`; `[All Turns]: fires when the [Hudie] Digimon suspends to block on the opponent's turn` |
| 4 Security | `Security` `isSecurity: true` -> `PlayWithoutCost` self | `plays itself from security without paying its 4 cost` |

Plus two conformance tests: full catalog field match with the normalized printed text, and the IR shape of all four clauses.

Every behavioral test runs the real turn loop (`startTurnLoop` + `advance().waitForMainPhase`) and drives public intents (`playCard`, `attack`, `declareBlock`, `endPhase`, `surrender`). No `fireTiming` or `fireSubTrigger` injection remains.

#### Defects found and fixed (local, in `BT23-085.ts`)

1. **Breeding-area leak on clause 1.** The `youHave` condition filter carried no `zone`, and
   `countMatching` (`apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/scaling.ts:95-101`) scans the breeding
   area as well as the battle area when `zone` is unset. A lone [CS] Digimon in breeding therefore
   granted a memory. Added `zone: "battleArea"`.
   *Red-then-green proof:* with the fix reverted, `ignores a [CS] Digimon that is only in the
   breeding area` fails with `expected 1 to be +0`; with the fix, it passes.
2. **Missing zone scope on the clause 2 targets and the clause 3 source filter.** The three
   `[Hudie]` filters had no `zone`, so a breeding Digimon could match a watcher whose printed
   subject is a battle-area Digimon. Target enumeration already defaults to `battleArea`
   (`targeting/permanents.ts:110-117`), so this is a hardening change, not a behavior change — but
   it removes reliance on that default and matches the sibling card BT23-080.
3. **`// @ts-nocheck` removed.** The module typechecks without it, so duration/filter typos now
   surface (the brief's `toDuration` pitfall). `duration: "untilOpponentTurnEnd"` is a real
   `EffectDurationRef` value (`packages/shared/src/effects/ir/durations.ts`).

The prior test file was replaced. It was structural in the places that mattered: three of its five
cases asserted only IR shape, and the two "behavioral" ones injected `fireTiming(OnPlay, …)` and
`advance().fireSubTrigger("whenSuspended", …)` directly, which proves nothing about how the card
behaves under real play. Nothing in it pinned the memory paid, the security stack, the duration
boundary, or any refusal branch.

#### Findings that are NOT defects

- `byOpponentEffectsOnly` is honored end to end: the opponent's -3000 DP effect is blocked while
  the controller's own `modifyDP` still lands. Both directions are pinned by tests.
- `UseOptionWithoutCost` has no printed cost cap on this card, and `optionUseCandidates`
  (`interpreter/actions/borrowed.ts:423-428`) applies no cap when the filter names none, despite
  the "historical default of 5" mentioned in the IR doc comment. A cost-5 Option (Comet Hammer) is
  usable, which the behavioral test proves.
- `sameTarget` on the two `GainKeyword` actions correctly reuses the Digimon the `Restrict` action
  chose (CAP-A9). The "exactly one of two [Hudie] copies" test pins it.

#### Remaining gaps

- **Cost paid with no legal Option.** When the watcher fires and the hand holds no eligible
  Option, the engine still asks and, on accept, suspends Ryuji for nothing. No Q&A covers this and
  no ruling contradicts it, so the test asserts only that the ineligible Option stays in hand and
  never reaches the battle area, not whether Ryuji suspends. Worth a coordinator ruling check.
- **＜Reboot＞ is proved as a grant, not as an unsuspend.** The test asserts the keyword is live
  through the opponent's turn and gone afterwards; it does not walk the opponent's Unsuspend phase
  with the Digimon suspended. The grant expiry boundary makes that window narrow, and the keyword
  itself is engine-shared behavior, not card behavior.
- **The `[All Turns]` proof on the opponent's turn uses BT23-051 Golemon's printed ＜Blocker＞**
  rather than the ＜Blocker＞ Ryuji grants, so the two clauses stay independently proved.

#### Commands and results

```
pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec vitest run src/cards/BT23/BT23-085.test.ts --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism
  Test Files  1 passed (1)
  Tests       18 passed (18)
  Duration    7.78s
```
