### BT23-086 — Yuugo

#### Card

- Catalog: `packages/shared/src/cards/data/cards.json` → `BT23-086`, Tamer, Black/Red, play cost 5, traits `[Zaxon]` `[CS]`.
- Printed text:
  1. `[Start of Your Turn] If you have 2 or less memory, set it to 3.`
  2. `[On Play] By adding your top security card to the hand, you may place 1 Digimon card with the [Zaxon] trait from your hand or trash face up as the bottom security card.`
  3. `[End of Your Turn] By suspending this Tamer, 1 of your level 6 Digimon with the [Machine] or [Zaxon] trait may attack a player.`
  4. `[Security] Play this card without paying the cost.`

#### Rulings covered

`node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT23-086` returns Q5358–Q5361, all about face-up security cards (the zone clause 2 writes into).

| Q&A | Meaning | Coverage |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Q5358 | A card placed face up stays revealed and is otherwise a normal security card | test "keeps the placed card revealed…" |
| Q5359 | A security check on a face-up card runs normally, card stays revealed | test "checks the face-up bottom security card…" |
| Q5360 | A face-up security card's `[Security]` effect still triggers | no Zaxon Digimon in the catalog prints a `[Security]` effect, so clause 2 cannot place one. Covered indirectly by clause 4's own `[Security]` test; documented as unreachable through this card. |
| Q5361 | Shuffling security turns face-up cards face down | BT23-086 never shuffles security. Not reachable through this card. |

#### Clause → test → IR plan

| Clause | Behavioral test (public intents) | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | Real turn loop, memory armed to 2 before the turn starts; with Yuugo → 3, without Yuugo → 2 (control); memory 4 → 4 | `StartOfYourTurn` + `SetMemory 3` gated by `memoryAtMost 2 / mine` |
| 2 | `playCard` Yuugo for 5: top security card → hand, chosen `[Zaxon]` Digimon from hand → bottom of security face up; non-Zaxon hand card is not a candidate; trash variant; decline branch leaves both zones untouched; empty security = unpayable cost | `OnPlay` `SecurityManipulation placeAsSecurity`, `from: ["hand","trash"]`, `toTop:false`, `faceUp:true`, `cost.securityToHand`, `optional`, `abortOnDecline` |
| 3 | Real turn loop end of turn: Machine branch and Zaxon branch each attack the player (opponent security −1) with Yuugo suspended; level-5 Machine and level-6 non-trait negatives; already-suspended Yuugo cannot pay | `EndOfYourTurn` `Attack` with `attackPlayer`, `cost.suspend isSelfRef`, target `levels:[6]`, trait tokens `["Machine","Zaxon"]` |
| 4 | Opponent-side attack checks our security holding Yuugo → Yuugo enters our battle area, memory unchanged | `Security` `PlayWithoutCost isSelfRef payCost:false` |

#### Tests written

All behavioral tests drive public intents (`playCard`, `attack`, `endPhase`, `surrender`) or the real
turn loop (`runOneTurn` / `startTurnLoop` + `advance().waitForMainPhase`). No injected timing API is
used; the previous suite drove every clause through `engine.fireTiming`, which proved nothing about
the production windows, and it has been replaced.

| Test | What it pins |
| --- | --- |
| matches every catalog field and complete compiled clause | catalog identity, printed texts, trigger list |
| raises the start-of-turn memory to 3 only while Yuugo is in play | memory 2 → 3 at the real Main open, plus a Yuugo-less control that stays at 2 |
| leaves start-of-turn memory above 2 untouched | memory 4 → 4 |
| pays the top security card and places a Zaxon Digimon from hand face up at the bottom | memory 6 → 1, top security instance in hand, non-Zaxon hand card untouched, stack order `[kept, placed]`, only the placed card `faceUp` |
| places a Zaxon Digimon taken from the trash | trash source branch, exact trash residue |
| declining the On Play leaves the security stack and hand untouched | `abortOnDecline`: no cost paid |
| cannot activate the On Play with an empty security stack | unpayable cost, Zaxon stays in hand |
| keeps the placed card revealed and checks it face up when the opponent attacks | Q5358 across the turn boundary, Q5359 through a real opposing attack |
| suspends Yuugo at end of turn so a level 6 Machine Digimon attacks the player | Machine branch, cost paid, opponent security −1 |
| also grants the end-of-turn attack to a level 6 Zaxon Digimon | Zaxon branch |
| does not attack when only an ineligible level or trait is on the board | level-5 Machine and level-6 non-trait negatives |
| declining the end-of-turn effect leaves Yuugo unsuspended and the attack unmade | refusal branch |
| cannot pay the end-of-turn cost while Yuugo is already suspended | cost gate (suspension armed after the Active phase, per the fixture note in the brief) |
| plays itself from security without paying its cost | `[Security]` clause through an opposing attack, no memory paid |
| compiles each printed clause into the expected IR | IR trace for all four clauses |
| (retained red) restricts the end-of-turn attack to the player | see defect 1 |

#### Commands and results

- `pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec vitest run src/cards/BT23/BT23-086.test.ts --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism`
  → 1 file passed, 15 passed | 1 expected fail (16), 13.49s. Log: `docs/audits/BT23-reaudit/logs/BT23-086-vitest.log`.
- `pnpm --filter @aegis/api typecheck` → no error mentions BT23-086. The remaining errors belong to
  other lanes (`BT23-087.ts/.test.ts`, `engine/effects/immunityAffectation.test.ts`, `engine/combat/controller.ts`).
- `pnpm exec oxlint apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-086.ts apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-086.test.ts` → clean.
- `pnpm exec oxfmt --check` on both files → clean. `git diff --check` → clean.

#### Defects

1. **Retained red (engine/IR seam): the end-of-turn attack is not restricted to the player.**
   Printed: "1 of your level 6 Digimon … may attack a player." `AttackAction`
   (`packages/shared/src/effects/ir/actions/combat.ts`) exposes only `attackPlayer`, and
   `forceAttack` (`apps/api/src/engine/effects/primitives.ts` ~5557-5578) treats it as a WIDENING
   flag: `attackPlayer !== false` adds the player to the candidate list, and the opponent's legal
   (suspended) Digimon are added unless `attackPlayerOnly` is set. `attackPlayerOnly` exists on the
   primitive but has no IR field, so no card can request it. Observed candidate list with one
   suspended opposing Digimon: `["player", "seed-perm-113"]`; expected `["player"]`. Auto-selection
   happens to take the first candidate, so the only visible symptom is the illegal choice offered to
   the controller. Not fixable inside the allowed files (needs a `attackPlayerOnly` field in
   `packages/shared` plus a line in `interpreter/actions/combat.ts`). Retained as `it.fails`.
   The same shape affects every "may attack a player" card in the repo (BT17-081, BT18-088, EX8-054,
   BT20-080, BT22-067), so the fix is one IR field, not a per-card patch.

2. **Fixed locally: `// @ts-nocheck` removed from `BT23-086.ts`.** The module typechecks without it,
   so future duration/field errors will now surface instead of being silently swallowed. No IR change
   was needed — every clause already matches the printed text (`nameOrTrait` trait matching for
   `[Zaxon]`/`[Machine]`, `levels: [6]`, `toTop: false`, `faceUp: true`, `abortOnDecline`).

#### Rules note

Paying the end-of-turn suspend cost with no eligible level 6 Digimon is legal: comprehensive rules
15-7-5 lets the controller execute an optional processing condition even when the processing after it
cannot be executed. The negative test asserts Yuugo suspends and no attack happens, rather than
treating the suspension as a bug.

#### Remaining gaps

- Q5360 (a face-up security card's `[Security]` effect still triggers) cannot be reached through this
  card: no `[Zaxon]` trait Digimon in the catalog prints a `[Security]` effect, so clause 2 can never
  place one. Clause 4's own `[Security]` test proves the engine fires `[Security]` effects, but not
  from a face-up card.
- Q5361 (shuffling security flips face-up cards face down) is out of this card's text. Worth a
  cross-card check by the coordinator: `SecurityManipulation` `op: "shuffle"` calls
  `ctx.fx.shuffleSecurity(seat)` with no face-down pass in `interpreter/actions/security.ts`.
- The report file is under `docs/audits/*`, which `.gitignore` line 41 excludes; the coordinator has
  to force-add it like the other audit reports.

### BT23-087 — Violet Inboots

Sources: committed catalog `BT23-087`; KB Q5362, Q5569; comprehensive rules 3-4-5-3 and 3-4-5-8 (breeding area).

#### Card summary

Violet Inboots is a Purple Tamer, play cost 3, trait LIBERATOR. Printed clauses:

1. `[Start of Your Main Phase] By returning this Tamer to the bottom of the deck, you may play 1 [Violet Inboots] from your hand without paying the cost.`
2. `Then, if you don't have a Digimon, you may play 1 [Ghostmon] from your trash without paying the cost.`
3. `[Your Turn] When any of your Digimon digivolve into a Digimon with the [Ghost] trait, by suspending this Tamer, that Digimon gains <Rush> for the turn.`
4. `[Security] Play this card without paying the cost.`

#### Rulings covered

| Id | Ruling | Coverage |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Q5362 | The "then" tail only resolves if the return cost was paid. | Decline test: nothing moves, Ghostmon stays in trash. |
| Q5569 | The Violet Inboots played by this effect cannot activate its own `[Start of Your Main Phase]` effect in the same window. | Turn-loop test: exactly one Violet returns; the played copy stays on the field. |

#### Clause -> test -> IR plan

| Clause | IR | Test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 return cost + play from hand | `PlayWithoutCost` with `cost.kind: "return"`, `to: "deckBottom"`, `optional`, `abortOnDecline` | Real turn loop reaches Main; field copy at deck bottom, hand copy on field, memory unchanged. |
| 1 name condition | `nameOrTrait` `match: "nameExact"` on `Violet Inboots` | `matchNameOrTrait` unit assertion (exact vs near-name). |
| 2 conditional tail | second `PlayWithoutCost` with `condition.kind: "youHaveNone"` | Played when the battle area has no Digimon; skipped when it has one. |
| 2 breeding boundary | condition filter needs `zone: "battleArea"` | A Digimon in breeding only must not block the tail (rules 3-4-5-8). |
| 2 name condition | `nameOrTrait` `match: "nameExact"` on `Ghostmon` | `matchNameOrTrait` unit assertion. |
| 2 Q5362 | `abortOnDecline: true` on action 1 | Decline branch. |
| 1+2 Q5569 | phase-boundary candidate snapshot | Turn-loop test with two Violets. |
| 3 subtrigger | `SubTrigger whenOneOfYoursDigivolves` + `GainKeyword Rush` `forTheTurn` | Public `digivolve` intent into a Ghost Lv4; Violet suspends, the evolved Digimon attacks the same turn despite summoning sickness. |
| 3 refusal | `optional: true` | Decline: not suspended, attack rejected. |
| 3 non-Ghost | `sourceFilter.nameOrTrait` trait `Ghost` | Digivolve into a non-Ghost Lv4: no suspend, no Rush. |
| 3 controller | `sourceFilter.controller: "mine"` + `[Your Turn]` | Opponent digivolve on the opponent turn: no suspend. |
| 4 security | `PlayWithoutCost` `isSelfRef` `isSecurity` | Security check plays it to the field free. |

Findings, commands and score follow.

#### Defects found and fixed (all local to `BT23-087.ts`)

1. **Breeding area leaked into "you don't have a Digimon".** The tail condition used a
   zone-less `youHaveNone` filter, and `countMatching` counts breeding-area permanents by
   default (`engine/effects/interpreter/scaling.ts:22`). Comprehensive rules 3-4-5-8 forbid
   referencing breeding-area information, so a Digimon in breeding wrongly blocked the
   Ghostmon play. Fixed with `zone: "battleArea"`. Red proof: with the fix reverted,
   `-t 'breeding area'` fails (`expected true to be false` — Ghostmon stayed in trash).
2. **Bracketed names matched by substring.** Both `[Violet Inboots]` and `[Ghostmon]` used
   `match: "name"`, which is substring matching
   (`engine/effects/interpreter/matching/definition.ts:352`). Changed to `nameExact`. No
   printed card currently has either name as a strict superset, so this is proved through
   `matchNameOrTrait` directly (`Violet Inboots ACE`, `SoulGhostmon`,
   `Ghostmon: X Antibody` all now fail to match).
3. **Invalid duration string.** The Rush grant used `duration: "untilTurnEnd"`, which is not
   an `EffectDurationRef`; `toDuration` silently maps unknown strings to end-of-each-turn.
   The printed wording is "for the turn", so the value is now `forTheTurn`. This surfaced
   only after removing the module's `// @ts-nocheck`.
4. **`// @ts-nocheck` removed.** The module typechecks without it, so future duration and
   filter errors will surface at build time.

Also added `zone: "battleArea"` to the sub-trigger `sourceFilter`. This one is
documentation, not a bug fix: the engine already does not raise
`whenOneOfYoursDigivolves` for a breeding-area digivolution, so the new
"inside the breeding area" test passes with and without the field. It is kept because
rules 3-4-5-3 make the battle-area scope the correct printed meaning.

#### Test evidence

17 tests, all behavioral tests driven through public intents (`startTurnLoop`,
`waitForMainPhase`, `playCard`, `digivolve`, `attack`, `endPhase`, `surrender`). No
`advance.fireSubTrigger` and no `fireTiming` cast remain; the previous suite drove both
`[Start of Your Main Phase]` and the digivolve watcher through injected timing.

| Test | Proves |
| --- | --- |
| catalog/IR clause tests (4) | Every catalog field including the exact `effectText` (with its U+00A0), plus the full compiled shape for all four clauses. |
| bracketed names | `nameExact` semantics against three near-names. |
| real Main window | Field copy is the deck's last card, hand copy and trash Ghostmon are on the battle area by instance id, hand and trash no longer hold them, deck grew by exactly one, memory stayed at 0 for two cost-3 plays, no pending decision. |
| Q5569 | Two Violets in hand; exactly one copy in the deck, exactly one on the field, the second hand copy untouched. |
| Q5362 | Declined: the field copy stays on the board and out of the deck, the hand copy stays in hand, Ghostmon stays in trash. |
| Digimon present | Hand copy played, Ghostmon stays in trash. |
| breeding boundary | Breeding Digimon does not block the tail; the breeding permanent stays in breeding. |
| Rush grant | Ghostmon played this turn is refused an attack, digivolves into BT4-080 Bakemon, Violet suspends, the stack holds the exact Ghostmon instance, the attack is then accepted and takes one security card. |
| Rush refusal | Declined: Tamer unsuspended, no Rush, attack rejected, security intact at 3. |
| non-Ghost | Digivolve into BT4-082 Dobermon: no suspend, no Rush. |
| breeding digivolve | Ghost digivolution inside breeding does not suspend the Tamer or grant Rush. |
| opponent turn | Opponent's Ghost digivolution on the opponent's turn does not suspend the Tamer. |
| Rush expiry | Rush is live during its own Main (memory 3 after the cost-2 digivolve) and gone once the opponent's Main opens; the Tamer stays suspended through the opponent's turn. |
| Security | Attacked player's security Violet Inboots reaches the battle area, never the trash, security empties, memory unchanged. |

#### Commands and results

- `pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec vitest run src/cards/BT23/BT23-087.test.ts --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism` — 1 file / 17 tests passed, 2.4-11.4 s across runs. Log: `logs/BT23-087-focused.log`.
- `pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec vitest run src/engine/cards --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism` — 17 files / 170 tests passed (compiler and catalog-sync guards, run because the module's IR shape changed).
- `pnpm --filter @aegis/api typecheck` — clean except the pre-existing unrelated
  `src/engine/effects/immunityAffectation.test.ts(29,80)` failure (`EffectDuration.ForTheTurn`), which belongs to another lane.
- `pnpm exec oxlint` and `pnpm exec oxfmt --check` on both files — clean. `git diff --check` clean.

#### Remaining gaps

- No test distinguishes `forTheTurn` from the old silent fallback, because both clear at the
  same boundary for a grant made on the controller's own turn. The fix is a type-correctness
  fix, proved by `tsc`, not by behavior.
- The `nameExact` fix has no behavioral proof because the catalog contains no card whose
  name strictly contains "Ghostmon" or "Violet Inboots". Proof is at the matcher level.
- The sub-trigger `zone: "battleArea"` addition is unproven (green before and after).
- Both plays are "without paying the cost"; no test covers a cost-reduction or play-blocking
  interaction from another card.

### BT23-088 — K

#### Card summary

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Name | K |
| Kind | Tamer |
| Color | Purple |
| Play cost | 3 |
| Traits | [CS] |
| Rarity / max in deck | U / 4 |

Printed clauses (official card list, `docs/audits/BT23-reaudit/logs/official-bt23.html` line 7015+):

1. `[Start of Your Main Phase] By trashing 1 card with the [Undead], [Dark Animal] or [CS] trait from your hand, gain 1 memory.`
2. `[End of Your Turn] By deleting this Tamer, 1 of your Digimon may digivolve into a level 5 or lower Digimon card with the [Undead] or [Dark Animal] trait in the trash without paying the cost.`
3. `[Security] Play this card without paying the cost.`

#### Rulings

`node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT23-088` returns **no knowledge-base entries**, matching
`KB-INDEX.md` (`| BT23-088 | 0 | None |`). No Q&A id to cover. The clauses are governed by
comprehensive rules instead:

- §3-4-5-3 — cards in the breeding area cannot be affected by effects that do not name the
  breeding area. Clause 2 says "1 of your Digimon", so the breeding resident is out of reach.
- "Without paying the cost" waives the memory cost only; the digivolution requirement still
  applies (`engine/effects/interpreter/actions/digivolve.ts::legalIntoCandidates`, and the
  matching comment there).

#### Clause -> IR -> test map

| Printed clause | IR | Test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Trigger 1 timing | `trigger: "StartOfYourMainPhase"` | "trashes exactly 1 eligible hand card and gains exactly 1 memory when the real main phase opens" (real `startTurnLoop`) |
| Trigger 1 cost — 1 hand card with [Undead]/[Dark Animal]/[CS] | `cost.kind: "trash"`, `filter.zone: "hand"`, `nameOrTrait tokens ["Undead","Dark Animal","CS"] match "trait"` | same test asserts the eligible card is the only card trashed and the ineligible BT1-014 stays in hand |
| Trigger 1 effect — gain 1 memory | `GainMemory amount 1` | same test: memory 0 -> 1 exactly |
| Trigger 1 optional / abort on decline | `optional: true`, `abortOnDecline: true` | "declining the trash cost gains no memory and keeps the hand intact" (memory 0, hand and trash untouched) |
| Trigger 1 with no eligible card | cost cannot be paid | "gains no memory when the hand holds no [Undead]/[Dark Animal]/[CS] card" |
| Trigger 1 controller boundary and reset | `StartOfYourMainPhase` | "stays silent on the opponent's main phase and fires again on the next own turn" — 3 real main phases, exactly one trash per own turn |
| Trigger 2 timing | `trigger: "EndOfYourTurn"` | "deletes K at end of turn and digivolves ..." (fires on the seat-0 `endPhase`) |
| Trigger 2 cost — delete this Tamer | `cost.kind: "deleteOwn"`, `filter.isSelfRef: true`, `isSelf: true` | same test: K leaves the battle area and is found in the trash by instance id |
| Trigger 2 — 1 of your Digimon digivolves | `target { controller: "mine", kind: ["Digimon"], count: 1 }` | same test: the base permanent's `topCard` is the trash card and its `stack` is exactly the old base instance |
| Trigger 2 — into a trash card | `from: ["trash"]` | same test: the into-card is no longer in trash |
| Trigger 2 — level 5 or lower | `into.levelComparison { lte, 5 }` | negative case "a level 6 trash card exceeds the level 5 ceiling" (BT23-068) |
| Trigger 2 — [Undead]/[Dark Animal] trait | `into.nameOrTrait` | negative case "a requirement-legal trash card without the [Undead]/[Dark Animal] trait" (BT10-074 Quetzalmon, Lv.4 Purple, EvoCost Purple Lv.3 — legal route, wrong trait) |
| Trigger 2 — "without paying the cost" waives memory only | `payCost: false`, no `ignoreReqs` | negative case "a level 5 trash card whose EvoCost the Lv.3 base cannot meet" (BT23-066 needs a Lv.4 base) plus the IR test asserting no ignore-requirement flag |
| Trigger 2 optional | `optional: true`, `abortOnDecline: true` | "keeps K on the board when the end-of-turn cost is declined" |
| Trigger 2 controller boundary | `EndOfYourTurn` | "does not fire the end-of-turn clause on the opponent's turn" |
| Trigger 2 zone boundary (rules §3-4-5-3) | target filter carries no `zone`; engine defaults zone-less permanent filters to the battle area | "does not reach a breeding-area Digimon, so K survives with no battle-area Digimon" |
| Clause 3 | `trigger: "Security"`, `isSecurity: true`, `PlayWithoutCost payCost: false` | "[Security] plays K into the battle area without paying its 3 cost" — real `attack` intent into security, memory unchanged at 3 |

Every digivolve test pins the endpoints the rubric asks for: physical instance ids in the
expected zone, the resulting `topCard`/`stack` split, `currentDP` from the into-card's printed
DP, the digivolve draw (hand +1, deck -1), the memory value, and `pendingDecision === undefined`
plus `assertNoLoudGap` on the two positive paths.

#### Defects found

1. **Fixed (local): the module carried `// @ts-nocheck`.** Removed. The module typechecks
   without it, so a future bad `duration:` or action shape now surfaces at compile time
   (the BT23-047 failure mode named in the brief).
2. **Fixed (local): the old test proved almost nothing behaviorally.** It drove both triggers
   through the injected `engine.fireTiming` cast rather than the real turn loop, asserted
   `s.state.memory === before` after an end-of-turn digivolve, never checked the digivolution
   requirement, the trait gate, the decline branches, the controller boundaries, the digivolve
   draw, or the `[Security]` clause. Replaced with 16 tests, 11 of them behavioral through
   public intents (`startTurnLoop`, `endPhase`, `attack`, `surrender`).
3. **Catalog discrepancy (NOT fixed — coordinator owns cards.json).**
   `cards.json` stores BT23-088's `effectText` with two **non-breaking spaces** (U+00A0) at
   offsets 86 and 270: `"trait from your hand"` and `"trait in the trash"`. The
   official card list prints ordinary spaces at both positions (verified byte-for-byte against
   `logs/official-bt23.html`). Cosmetic only — no parser in the effect pipeline reads
   `effectText` for these clauses — but it will break any exact-text assertion or diff against
   the official list. The test normalizes U+00A0 and records why.

No engine defect found. No retained red, no skip.

#### Remaining gaps

- The "without paying the cost" proof is indirect at the memory level: the turn handover
  overwrites memory before the test can read it, so the accepted branch is compared against the
  declined branch reaching the same handover value (3) from the same board, plus the IR's
  `payCost: false`. A direct "memory did not drop by 2 at the instant of the digivolve" reading
  would need an `onEvent` probe inside the resolution.
- The `[Security]` test proves the free play; it does not prove what happens when the security
  play would be the fourth Tamer or otherwise blocked, which is generic play-path behavior, not
  this card's text.
- Clause 2 is not tested with several eligible bases at once (the choice among two of your
  Digimon). The engine's `chooseTargets` path is shared and covered elsewhere; this card adds
  no selection rule of its own.

### BT23-089 — Takumi Aiba

#### Card

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Name | Takumi Aiba |
| Kind | Tamer |
| Color | White |
| Play cost | 4 |
| Trait | CS |

Printed text (official card list, BT-23):

1. `[Start of Your Main Phase]` If your opponent has a Digimon, gain 1 memory.
2. `[All Turns]` When any of your Digimon with the `[CS]` trait would leave the battle area, by
   suspending this Tamer and trashing 2 same-level cards from 1 of your `[CS]` trait Digimon's
   digivolution cards, they don't leave.
3. `[Security]` Play this card without paying the cost.

Catalog check: `packages/shared/src/cards/data/cards.json` matches the official list on every
field (name, color, kind, cost, trait, both effect texts). The catalog writes "trash 2 same-level
cards" where the official page writes "trashing"; same clause, no rules difference. No catalog
edit needed, so none was made.

#### Rulings

`node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT23-089` returns no knowledge-base entries, and
the Knowledge base index section of this document lists BT23-089 with 0 Q&A ids. There is nothing to cover
from the Q&A set. Mechanics therefore come from the comprehensive rules:

- §3-4-5-3 / §3-4-5-8: cards in breeding areas can't be affected by effects, and their
  information can't be referenced, unless the effect names the breeding area. This settles both
  board clauses: "your opponent has a Digimon" and "leave the battle area" are battle area only.

#### Clause -> test -> IR

| Clause | IR | Test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 gain 1 memory | `StartOfYourMainPhase` + `GainMemory` gated by `opponentHas` | "gains 1 memory at the start of its controller's main phase..." |
| 1 condition false | same | "gains no memory when the opponent controls no Digimon" |
| 1 battle area only | `opponentHas.filter.zone = "battleArea"` | "ignores an opponent Digimon that is only in the breeding area" |
| 1 controller boundary | `StartOfYourMainPhase` | "stays silent at the opponent's own start of main phase" |
| 2 prevention + compound cost | `Replacement wouldLeavePlay` `mode: "prevent"`, `cost.kind: "compound"` | "pays the compound cost to keep a [CS] Digimon that would be deleted in battle" |
| 2 optional | `optional: true` | "lets the controller decline..." |
| 2 same-level pair | `sameLevelPair` + `sameHost` | "cannot pay from a stack without a same-level pair" |
| 2 suspend half of the cost | `cost.costs[0].kind: "suspend"` on self | "cannot pay while this Tamer is already suspended" |
| 2 `[CS]` trait gate | `target.filter.nameOrTrait` trait CS | "does not protect a Digimon without the [CS] trait even when the cost is payable" |
| 2 "your" Digimon | `target.filter.controller: "mine"` | "does not protect the opponent's [CS] Digimon" |
| 2 "they don't leave" | `affectsAll: true`, `target.count: "all"` | "saves every [CS] Digimon leaving together for a single payment" |
| 2 host must be `[CS]`, trashed cards need not be | `hostFilter` on the trash cost | the paid-cost test trashes a non-`[CS]` Numemon plus a `[CS]` GoldNumemon |
| 3 free play from security | `Security` + `PlayWithoutCost` `payCost: false` | "plays itself from security without paying its cost" |
| structural | whole replacement shape | "compiles the prevention as an all-affecting battle-area replacement with a hosted pair cost" |

Every behavioral test drives the engine through public intents (`startTurnLoop` +
`waitForMainPhase`, `attack`, `playCard`) — no injected timing API.

##### Fixture legality

`SAME_LEVEL_STACK` is `[BT2-056 Numemon (Lv.4), BT22-031 GoldNumemon (Lv.4)]` under
`BT23-031 Angewomon (Lv.5, [CS])`. That stack has a real public route: Numemon digivolves into
GoldNumemon ("[Digivolve] Lv.4 w/[Numemon] in name: Cost 2" — a rare same-level route), which
digivolves into Angewomon ("[Digivolve] Lv.4 w/[CS] trait: Cost 3"). The fixture seeds the
resulting stack rather than replaying the three plays, because the clause under test does not
depend on how the stack formed. It also gives a non-`[CS]` card in the pair, which is what makes
the `hostFilter` fix observable.

Security fixtures use main-deck Digimon (BT1-009/010/011). No Digi-Egg is placed in a deck or
security stack.

#### Defects found and fixed (all local, in `apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-089.ts`)

1. **The trash cost demanded that the trashed cards be `[CS]` Digimon.** The cost filter carried
   `kind: ["Digimon"]` and the `[CS]` trait alongside `zone: "digivolutionCards"`. For a hosted
   zone, `candidateLooseInstances`
   (`apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/targeting/loose.ts` ~300) applies the plain filter to
   each **stack card** and only `filter.hostFilter` to the host permanent. The printed clause puts
   the `[CS]` requirement on the Digimon whose digivolution cards are trashed, not on the trashed
   cards. Moved to `hostFilter`. Red proof: with the old shape, "pays the compound cost..." fails
   (the pair Numemon + GoldNumemon has only one `[CS]` card, so the cost is unpayable).
2. **`affectsAll` was missing.** The printed text says "they don't leave" — one payment saves
   every `[CS]` Digimon leaving in the same event, exactly as on peer BT19-048
   (`apps/api/src/cards/BT19/BT19-048.ts`). Without it, `consultLeavePrevention` charges once per
   saved permanent. Added `affectsAll: true` and `target.count: "all"`. Red proof: removing it
   alone fails "saves every [CS] Digimon leaving together for a single payment".
3. **Both board clauses reached the breeding area.** `opponentHas` with no `zone` counts breeding
   permanents (`interpreter/scaling.ts` `countMatching`, default branch), and the replacement's
   `sourceFilter`/`target.filter` likewise did not restrict the leave to the battle area. Added
   `zone: "battleArea"` to all three, per comprehensive rules §3-4-5-8. Red proof: removing it
   from `opponentHas` fails "ignores an opponent Digimon that is only in the breeding area".
4. **`// @ts-nocheck` removed.** The module typechecks without it, so the suppression only hid
   future IR type errors (the `EffectDurationRef` class of defect found elsewhere in this set).
   `pnpm --filter @aegis/api typecheck` reports no error in either BT23-089 file.

  effects.json. BT23-089 is one of those 33 — see "For the coordinator" below.

#### Remaining gaps

- No retained reds or skips. No engine seam was hit.
- A non-deletion leave (return to hand or to the deck by an opponent effect) is not covered.
  `wouldLeavePlay` is the right event and `leaveCause` is left at `any`, so the shape is correct,
  but the behavior is untested for lack of a clean single-clause bounce card in the fixture set.
- The `SAME_LEVEL_STACK` route is argued from the printed digivolution requirements rather than
  replayed through three public digivolve intents.
- Simultaneous interaction with another leave replacement on the same permanent (the
  `orderReplacements` path) is not covered; the fixture deliberately uses a host with no
  competing replacement.

`BT23-catalog-sync.test.ts` reports BT23-089 as out of sync. That file is coordinator-owned and

### BT23-090 — Keisuke Amasawa

Sources: committed catalog `BT23-090`; KB Q5363; comprehensive rules 3-4-5-3 / 3-4-5-8 (breeding area).

#### Card summary

White Tamer, play cost 4, traits [Hudie] / [CS]. Printed clauses:

1. `[Start of Your Turn] If you have 2 or less memory, set it to 3.`
2. `[End of Your Turn] By suspending this Tamer and returning 1 of your Digimon with the [Hudie] trait to the hand, you may play 1 Tamer card with the [CS] trait from your hand without paying the cost.`
3. `[All Turns] All of your [Hudie] Digimon get +1000 DP.`
4. `[Security] Play this card without paying the cost.`

Q&A covered: **Q5363** — the `[End of Your Turn]` "by" cost is all-or-nothing. You cannot suspend this Tamer and skip the [Hudie] return; if either part cannot be paid, nothing happens.

#### Clause -> test -> IR mapping

| Printed clause | IR | Test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Start of Your Turn set memory 3 | `StartOfYourTurn` / `SetMemory value 3` gated by `memoryAtMost 2` | "sets memory to 3 through the real turn start when it is 2 or less" (memory 1 -> 3); "leaves memory above the gate untouched" (memory 5 -> 5) |
| End of Your Turn compound cost + free CS Tamer play | `EndOfYourTurn` / `PlayWithoutCost` from hand, `optional` + `abortOnDecline`, `cost.kind: "compound"` = `suspend` self + `return` [Hudie] Digimon to hand | "pays both costs ... and plays a [CS] Tamer from hand for free"; "declining the optional pays neither cost"; "suspends nothing and plays nothing without a [Hudie] Digimon to return (Q5363)"; "cannot pay the return cost with the opponent's [Hudie] Digimon"; "does not activate while this Tamer is already suspended"; "does not play a Tamer without the [CS] trait" |
| All Turns +1000 DP to your [Hudie] Digimon | `AllTurns` / `ModifyDP +1000`, `count: "all"`, `duration: "permanent"`, filter `controller: mine`, `kind: ["Digimon"]`, trait `Hudie` (exact) | "gives +1000 DP to your [Hudie] Digimon only, on both turns" |
| Security self play without cost | `Security` / `PlayWithoutCost` on `isSelfRef` with `payCost: false`, `isSecurity: true` | "plays itself from the security stack without paying its cost" |

#### Behavioral endpoints asserted

- The `[End of Your Turn]` window is reached through the real turn loop (`startTurnLoop()` + public `endPhase`), never an injected timing call. The prior test file drove `fireTiming(OnEndTurn)` directly; that was replaced.
- Free play proved by memory, not by `ok: true`: an `onEvent` observer snapshots `state.memory` at the instant the Tamer's `cardsMoved` lands in the battle area. It reads `-3` (the turn-pass rule reframes the gauge to the incoming turn player's sign before the End of Your Turn window opens), i.e. seat 0's 3 memory untouched. Paying BT23-080's printed cost of 4 would read `-7`.
- Exact instance ids asserted in the destination zone for the returned [Hudie] (hand, and absent from battle area) and the played [CS] Tamer (battle area, absent from hand and trash), plus `pendingDecision === undefined` after the window settles.
- Refusal branches assert an intermediate state, never a bare await: the Tamer stays unsuspended, the [Hudie] stays on board, the Tamer card stays in hand.
- DP aura: friendly [Hudie] 7000 -> 8000; friendly non-[Hudie] [CS] Digimon (BT23-006) unchanged at 1000; opponent [Hudie] (BT23-101) unchanged at 7000; a [Hudie] arriving mid-test (BT23-020) picks the buff up at the next recompute (5000 -> 6000); the buff still reads 8000 during the opponent's Main, proving `[All Turns]` rather than `[Your Turn]`.
- Security: attacked into a security stack holding BT23-090; the card leaves security, is not in trash, sits in the opponent's battle area, and memory is unchanged.

#### Defects found and fixed

- **`// @ts-nocheck` removed from `BT23-090.ts`.** The module typechecks without it, so the suppression only hid future errors (the brief's `EffectDurationRef` pitfall). `pnpm --filter @aegis/api typecheck` reports no error in this file; the only errors in the package are pre-existing and belong to other lanes (`src/engine/effects/immunityAffectation.test.ts:29`).
- **Test file rewritten.** The previous suite proved the End of Your Turn clause only through `engine.fireTiming(EffectTiming.OnEndTurn)` (an injected timing, structural credit only) and had no Security test, no controller boundary, no already-suspended boundary, and no CS-trait negative.

No IR change was needed. Two checks that looked like defects are not:

- The `ModifyDP` and `return` filters carry no `zone`. Permanent targeting defaults to the battle area (`interpreter/targeting/permanents.ts` `allowsZone`, and `matching/permanent.ts`), so breeding-area Digimon are already excluded per CR 3-4-5-3. The `zone: "battleArea"` pitfall applies to `youHave`-style conditions, which this card has none of.
- `duration: "permanent"` is a valid `EffectDurationRef` member, not a silent fallback.

#### Mutation probes (evidence the tests bite)

Each probe was applied to `BT23-090.ts`, run, then reverted:

| Probe | Result |
| --- | --- |
| return-cost filter `controller: "mine"` -> `"any"` | "cannot pay the return cost with the opponent's [Hudie] Digimon" fails |
| drop the `[CS]` trait filter on the play target | "does not play a Tamer without the [CS] trait" fails |
| break the `suspend` half of the compound cost | "pays both costs ... for free" fails |

#### Commands and results

- `pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec vitest run src/cards/BT23/BT23-090.test.ts --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism` — 1 file, 11 tests, all passed, 4.95s. Log: `logs/BT23-090-vitest.log`.
- `pnpm --filter @aegis/api typecheck` — no error in `BT23-090.ts` / `BT23-090.test.ts`; one pre-existing failure elsewhere (`immunityAffectation.test.ts:29`, other lane).
- `pnpm exec oxlint` and `pnpm exec oxfmt --check` on both files — clean. `git diff --check` — clean.

#### Remaining gaps

- No test removes Keisuke from the board to prove the +1000 DP aura lapses; no public intent deletes a Tamer on your own turn without adding another card's effect to the fixture.
- The [Start of Your Turn] clause is proven with `state.memory` armed before the loop starts (the same lever `turnEndHarness.test.ts` uses). The turn loop itself does not offer a public way to enter your turn holding a chosen memory value.
- Q5363 is proven from the "cannot pay part of the cost" side (no [Hudie] to return, opponent-only [Hudie], already-suspended Tamer). There is no engine surface that would let a player suspend and then decline the return, so the "partial payment" case cannot be requested at all — that is the correct shape, not an untested branch.

### BT23-091 — Wolkenapalm

Worker lane: card-only. Branch `audit-bt23-astra-luna`.

#### Card summary

Catalog record (`packages/shared/src/cards/data/cards.json`): Red Option, play cost 5, trait `[CS]`,
rarity C. The catalog text matches the official card list; no discrepancy found.

Printed clauses:

| # | Clause |
|---|---|
| C1 | While you have a Digimon or Tamer with the [CS] trait on the field, you can ignore this card's color requirements. |
| C2 | [Main] Delete 1 of your opponent's Digimon with the lowest DP. Then, place this card in the battle area. |
| C3 | [Your Turn] When one of your [CS] trait Digimon attacks, ＜Delay＞ ・Delete 1 of your opponent's Digimon with the lowest DP. |
| C4 | [Security] Delete 1 of your opponent's Digimon with the lowest DP. Then, place this card in the battle area. |

#### Rulings covered

`node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT23-091` lists one Q&A.

| Id | Question | How it is covered |
|---|---|---|
| Q5364 | What does "while you have (the specified card) on the field" mean? Answer: battle area or breeding area. | Covered, with an override. Comprehensive Rules §3-4-5-8 says information on breeding-area cards cannot be referenced unless the effect names the breeding area, and its worked example is this exact waiver shape ("even if you have a Digimon with the [Armor Form] trait in the breeding area, you can't ignore the color requirements"). So for C1 the [CS] check reads the battle area only. Test: "does not waive the color requirement for a [CS] Digimon in the breeding area (CR 3-4-5-8)", paired with the battle-area positive. |

Rules consulted: CR §4-21 (color requirements), §16-17 (＜Delay＞), the glossary line "You can't
activate this effect the turn this card enters play", §3-4-5-8 (breeding-area information),
§17-1-3-2-2 (an Option in the battle area is trashed unless an effect placed it).

#### Clause → test → IR mapping

| Clause | Test | IR |
|---|---|---|
| C1 gate closed | rejects the play when no [CS] card and no Red source is on the board | `Static` / `WaiveColorRequirement` condition unmet → `playCard` returns `color-requirement-unmet` |
| C1 Digimon branch | waives the color requirement for a battle-area [CS] Digimon | `condition.filter.kind: ["Digimon","Tamer"]`, `nameOrTrait match: "trait"` |
| C1 Tamer branch | waives the color requirement for a battle-area [CS] Tamer | same filter, Tamer arm |
| C1 zone scope | does not waive the color requirement for a [CS] Digimon in the breeding area | `condition.filter.zone: "battleArea"` |
| C1 controller scope | does not waive the color requirement for an opponent's [CS] Digimon | `controllerDefault: "mine"` |
| C2 | deletes only the lowest-DP opponent Digimon and then places itself in the battle area | `Main` → `Delete {controller:"opponent", kind:["Digimon"], superlative:"lowestDP"}`, then `PlaceInBattleAreaSelf` |
| C2 empty board | still places itself when the opponent has no Digimon to delete | same, delete resolves to nothing |
| C3 timing gate + spend | holds the Delay on the placement turn, then spends it on the next own turn | `YourTurn` → `SubTrigger whenAttacking`, `keywords: [Delay]` |
| C3 decline | leaves the Delay unspent when its optional prompt is declined | ＜Delay＞ is optional processing (CR §16-17-2) |
| C3 source filter | does not offer the Delay when a non-[CS] Digimon attacks | `sourceFilter.nameOrTrait [CS] match:"trait"` |
| C3 controller/turn | does not offer the Delay on the opponent's turn for the opponent's [CS] attacker | `trigger: "YourTurn"`, `sourceFilter.controller: "mine"` |
| C4 | deletes the attacker side's lowest-DP Digimon from security and then places itself | `Security` / `isSecurity: true`, same two actions |

Endpoints asserted: memory paid (5 → 0) and memory untouched on a refusal; exact surviving
`permanentId` lists per seat; card ids in each trash; `placedByEffect` on the placed Option (the
only reason it survives the §17-1-3-2-2 sweep); hand size; security length; and `assertNoLoudGap`
for the unsupported-effect detector. The ＜Delay＞ tests also count the engine's optional prompts
for `sourceCardId === "BT23-091"` — exactly 1 in the accept and decline branches, exactly 0 in the
same-turn, non-[CS] and opponent-turn branches — so the negatives prove no window opened rather
than merely observing an unchanged board.

The ＜Delay＞ tests drive the real turn loop (`startTurnLoop` + `advance().waitForMainPhase`) across
two of the controller's own turns and use public `playCard` / `attack` intents only. No injected
timing API is used anywhere in this file.

#### Defects found and fixed

1. **Illegal fixtures (the coordinator's flag).** The old test put `BT1-001` Yokomon and `BT1-002`
   Bebydomon — both Digi-Eggs — into security. Replaced. The whole file now uses main-deck cards
   only: `BT22-053` Keramon (the [CS] carrier), `BT1-049` Labramon, `BT1-034` Ikkakumon,
   `BT1-028` Elecmon, `BT1-030` Gomamon, `BT23-082` Makiko Date (the [CS] Tamer).

2. **Invalid `zone` literal in the module.** `BT23-091.ts` used `zone: "field"` on the C1 condition.
   `"field"` is not a member of `ZoneRef` (`packages/shared/src/effects/ir/filters/zones.ts`); it
   only compiled because the file carried `// @ts-nocheck`. At runtime `countMatching`
   (`apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/scaling.ts`) treats an unrecognised zone as the default
   scan, so the observable result happened to be correct, but nothing held it there. Changed to
   `zone: "battleArea"`, which is both type-legal and the CR §3-4-5-8 semantics. Discriminator
   proof: setting the same field to `"breeding"` makes the breeding-area test fail (the waiver then
   fires from breeding); `"battleArea"` makes it pass.

3. **`// @ts-nocheck` removed** from `BT23-091.ts`. The module now typechecks with no errors, so a
   future bad `duration:` or `zone:` literal will surface instead of being silently accepted.

4. **Structural-only assertions replaced.** Three of the six old tests only read the compiled object
   (`effects.find(...).actions[0]` shape checks). Those earned no behavioral credit and are gone; the
   only remaining structural check is the catalog/coverage test.

5. **`placedByEffect` no longer hand-set.** The old tests seeded the Option as a battle-area
   permanent and wrote `permanent.placedByEffect = true` by hand. Every test now places it through
   its own printed [Main] or [Security] effect, so the placement path itself is under test.

No engine change was needed. No retained red, no skip.

### BT23-092 — Ice Archery

#### Card

Blue Option, play cost 5, trait [CS]. Catalog record: `packages/shared/src/cards/data/cards.json` (cardId `BT23-092`). No discrepancy against the printed card; the catalog text is asserted verbatim by the first test (including the two U+00A0 after `[CS]`).

Printed clauses:

1. **Colour waiver (static).** "While you have a Digimon or Tamer with the [CS] trait on the field, you can ignore this card's color requirements."
2. **[Main].** "Until your opponent's turn ends, 1 of their Digimon and 1 of their Tamers can't suspend. Then, place this card in the battle area."
3. **[Your Turn] ＜Delay＞.** "When one of your [CS] trait Digimon attacks, ＜Delay＞ ・Until your opponent's turn ends, 1 of their Digimon and 1 of their Tamers can't suspend."
4. **[Security].** "Until your opponent's turn ends, 1 of their Digimon and 1 of their Tamers can't suspend. Then, place this card in the battle area."

#### Rulings covered

| Q&A | Ruling | Where it is proven |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Q5365 | "While you have (the specified card) on the field" means the battle area **or** the breeding area | `Q5365: a [CS] Digimon in the BREEDING area alone satisfies "on the field"` — the Blue Option is played with nothing in the battle area and a Red [CS] Agumon in breeding |

Comprehensive Rules §16-17 (＜Delay＞) is also covered: trashing the card is the activation cost, the effect is optional, and it cannot be activated the turn the card enters play.

#### Clause → test → IR

| Clause | IR | Test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 colour waiver | `Static` → `WaiveColorRequirement`, condition `youHave`, `kind: ["Digimon","Tamer"]`, `nameOrTrait: [{tokens:["CS"], match:"trait"}]` (exact trait, CR 2-3-2-3), zone battle area + breeding | plays a **Blue** Option off a **Red** [CS] Agumon; negative with a Red non-CS Digimon rejects `color-requirement-unmet`; Q5365 breeding-only variant |
| 2 [Main] | `Main` → `Restrict suspend` (opponent Digimon, count 1) → `Restrict suspend` (opponent Tamer, count 1) → `PlaceInBattleAreaSelf` | play from hand: memory 5 → 0, hand empty, the Option is a battle-area permanent with `placedByEffect` (not trash, not the delay zone), exactly one of the two opposing Digimon plus the Tamer restricted, no decision left open |
| 2 the restriction is real | `restriction: "suspend"` is recorded as `beSuspended` and read back through the suspend/beSuspended equivalence (`engine/effects/continuous.ts:612`) | on the opponent's turn the restricted Digimon's `attack` intent returns `{ok:false, reason:"illegal-target"}` (`engine/combat/legality.ts:206`) and stays unsuspended, while an unrestricted board-mate attacks normally |
| 2 duration | `duration: "untilOpponentTurnEnd"` | restriction survives the opponent's Active phase unsuspend and their whole Main phase, and is gone at my next Main phase |
| 3 ＜Delay＞ | `YourTurn` → `SubTrigger whenAttacking` with `sourceFilter` `controller: mine`, `kind: ["Digimon"]`, exact `[CS]` trait; `keywords: [Delay]` | same-turn attack leaves the Option on the board (§16-17-3); on my next turn the same [CS] attack trashes the Option and applies both restrictions; declining leaves the Option and applies nothing; a non-[CS] attacker never arms it |
| 4 [Security] | `Security` (`isSecurity: true`) → the same two Restricts → `PlaceInBattleAreaSelf` | the opponent attacks into a security stack holding this card: the **attacker's** Digimon and Tamer are restricted, the Option leaves security and becomes the defender's battle-area permanent |

#### Defects found and fixed

1. **`zone: "field"` is not a `ZoneRef`** (`apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-092.ts`). The waiver condition used a zone string the IR type does not define. It only reached the correct behaviour through `countMatching`'s unknown-zone fallback (`apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/scaling.ts:95-101` scans the battle area **and** breeding for any zone it does not recognise) — one tightening of that fallback would have silently changed the card. Replaced with the type-valid `zone: ["battleArea", "breeding"]`, which routes to the same fallback branch deliberately. Behaviour is unchanged and pinned by both waiver tests (battle-area source and breeding-only source) plus the negative.
2. **`// @ts-nocheck` removed** from the module. It was masking defect 1. The module now typechecks clean.
3. **Illegal fixtures replaced.** The previous test seeded `BT1-001` / `BT1-002` (Digi-Eggs) in security. Replaced with main-deck Lv3 Digimon `BT1-009` / `BT1-010` / `BT1-011`.
4. **Structural-only assertions replaced.** Three of the four previous tests asserted the compiled IR shape only, and the two that ran the engine hand-seeded the Option into the battle area and set `placedByEffect` by hand — which skips the [Main] body, the colour gate and the placement itself. Every clause now runs through public intents (`playCard`, `attack`, `endPhase`) on a real turn loop.

#### Notes on what is not proven

- **A Tamer's "can't suspend" is asserted at the ledger, not through a public action.** No public intent suspends an opponent's Tamer; Tamer suspension is an activation cost of that Tamer's own controller-chosen ability. The restriction on the Tamer is asserted with `observe(...).isRestricted(perm, "beSuspended")`. The Digimon side of the same restriction is proven publicly (the attack refusal), and both come from the same `Restrict` action, so the ledger read is the narrow part.
- **The choice of which opposing Digimon/Tamer is restricted** is steered with `preferInstanceIds` where the fixture has more than one candidate. The card does not say "of your opponent's choice", so the resolving player choosing is correct.

A positive receipt — an event or decision showing the gate declined — would be stronger, but the engine emits none at `apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/actions/subTrigger.ts:1109`, which returns before asking.

### BT23-093 — Big Bang Punch!

Sources: committed catalog `BT23-093`; KB Q5366, Q5367; comprehensive rules on ＜Delay＞ (§16-17).

#### Card summary

Blue Option, play cost 2, type [Appmon], rarity C. Printed clauses:

- **C1** While you have a Digimon or Tamer with the [Appmon] trait on the field, you can ignore this card's color requirements.
- **C2** [Main] ＜Draw 1＞. Then, place this card in the battle area.
- **C3** [All Turns] When any of your [Appmon] trait Digimon suspend, ＜Delay＞ ・You may link 1 [Appmon] trait Digimon card from your hand to 1 of those Digimon without paying the cost.
- **C4** [Security] Place this card in the battle area.

#### Rulings covered

| Q&A | Ruling | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Q5366 | "on the field" = battle area **or** breeding area | Two positive C1 tests (Appmon Digimon in breeding; Appmon Tamer in battle area) plus a no-Appmon negative. The IR condition filter pins `zone: ["battleArea", "breeding"]`. |
| Q5367 | The ＜Delay＞ cannot link a card that has no ＜Link＞ | Two tests: mixed hand links only the ＜Link＞-capable Appmon and leaves the other in hand; hand holding only a no-＜Link＞ Appmon leaves the Delay unpaid and the Option in the battle area. |

#### Clause -> test -> IR mapping

| Clause | Tests | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| C1 | waives from breeding Appmon Digimon; waives from battle-area Appmon Tamer; refuses with no Appmon; refuses when only the **opponent** has Appmon cards | `Static` -> `WaiveColorRequirement` with `youHave` condition (`controllerDefault: "mine"`, `zone: ["battleArea","breeding"]`, `kind: ["Digimon","Tamer"]`, `nameOrTrait` trait `Appmon`) |
| C2 | draws exactly 1 then places itself for memory 2 (exact drawn instance in hand, exact second card still on top of deck, `placedByEffect`, not in trash, memory 2 -> 0) | `Main` -> `Draw 1` then `PlaceInBattleAreaSelf` |
| C3 | pays intrinsic Delay and links only the ＜Link＞-capable Appmon; links to the suspending Appmon, not a bystander; links a cost-1 Appmon with memory unchanged; does not fire for a non-Appmon suspension; does not fire for an opponent-controlled Appmon suspension; does not link from the trash; declined Delay keeps card and hand; cannot pay the turn it is placed and can on the next own turn | `AllTurns` + `Delay` keyword -> `SubTrigger` on `whenSuspended` with `sourceFilter` (`controller: "mine"`, `kind: ["Digimon"]`, trait `Appmon`) -> `Link` (`from: ["hand"]`, `payCost: false`, `optional: true`, recipient `isTriggerSource`) |
| C4 | places itself in the opponent battle area from a security check (`placedByEffect`, security emptied, not in trash) | `Security` -> `PlaceInBattleAreaSelf`, `isSecurity: true` |

Two structural tests pin the catalog fields, `coverage: "full"` / empty `residual`, and the exact ＜Delay＞ IR shape.

#### Behavioral notes

- **Delay timing.** The once-per-card timing rule ("can't activate the turn this card enters play") is proven on the real turn loop, not by injection: the Option is publicly played during turn 1 Main, the same-turn attack suspension leaves it unpaid and the link card in hand, then after a full opponent turn the same attacker's suspension pays the Delay, trashes the Option and links the exact card instance.
- **No-cost link.** `linkRequirement` on BT21-009 is asserted as "Cost 1", and memory is asserted unchanged at 3 across the link, so `payCost: false` is proven rather than assumed.
- **Recipient identity.** With two suspendable Appmon on board, only the attacker receives the link; the bystander stays unlinked and unsuspended. This proves `isTriggerSource` rather than "any own Appmon".
- **Fixture vocabulary.** [Appmon] is a *form* on BT21-009 / BT22-016, and the engine's trait set is forms ∪ attributes ∪ types, so `match: "trait"` (exact) matches. BT21-009 carries ＜Link＞, BT22-016 does not — that pair is the Q5367 control. BT21-084 Haru Shinkai supplies the Tamer half of Q5366. Security fixtures use main-deck BT1-011.

#### Defects found

None in the IR. Comparing against `HEAD`, the compiled module already modelled all four clauses correctly. The only module change is the removal of `// @ts-nocheck`; the file typechecks without it, so future `EffectDurationRef` / `Action` type errors will surface.

One local test defect was fixed: `getCardDefinition(APPMON_WITH_LINK).linkRequirement` was a strict-mode type error (`TS2532`); it is now optional-chained.

#### Commands and results

- `pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec vitest run src/cards/BT23/BT23-093.test.ts --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism` — 1 file / **17 tests passed**, 8.59s. Log: `logs/BT23-093-vitest.log`.
- `pnpm --filter @aegis/api typecheck` — no errors in `BT23-093.ts` or `BT23-093.test.ts`. Pre-existing failures in other lanes' files (`BT23-095.test.ts`, `BT23-098.ts`, `engine/combat/controller.ts`) are untouched.
- `pnpm exec oxlint apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-093.*` — no errors; only `no-explicit-any` warnings on the IR-shape assertions, matching the accepted style in the exemplar tests.

#### Retained reds / skips

None. No engine seam was hit on this card.

#### Remaining gaps

- The Delay is only exercised through attack-driven suspension. A non-attack suspension source (an effect that suspends an own Appmon) is not covered; the SubTrigger event is `whenSuspended`, so the path should be identical, but it is untested.
- No test proves the Option cannot be played for its [Main] effect while it is already in the battle area, nor the interaction of two copies of the Option in play at once.
- Catalog: no discrepancy found. `effectText` and `securityEffectText` match the official wording.

### BT23-095 — Crescent Leaf

#### Card

Sources: committed catalog `packages/shared/src/cards/data/cards.json` (`BT23-095`); KB Q5383.

Green Option, cost 5, trait `CS`, rarity C, no DP, no evolution cost.

Printed clauses:

1. Static: "While you have a Digimon or Tamer with the [CS] trait on the field, you can ignore this card's color requirements."
2. `[Main]` "Return 1 of your opponent's suspended Digimon to the bottom of the deck. Then, place this card in the battle area."
3. `[Your Turn]` "When one of your [CS] trait Digimon attacks, ＜Delay＞ ・Return 1 of your opponent's suspended Digimon to the bottom of the deck."
4. `[Security]` "Return 1 of your opponent's suspended Digimon to the bottom of the deck. Then, place this card in the battle area."

Catalog text matches the printed card. No discrepancy to report. The catalog stores a
non-breaking space inside "your [CS] trait"; the test normalizes it before comparing.

#### Rulings covered

| Q&A | Content | Coverage |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Q5383 | "on the field" means the battle area **or** the breeding area | Two positive waiver tests (CS Digimon in breeding only; off-color CS Tamer in the battle area only) plus a negative with non-CS Digimon in both zones. |

#### Clause -> test -> IR mapping

| Clause | IR | Test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 static waiver | `Static` / `WaiveColorRequirement`, `youHave` condition with `controllerDefault: "mine"`, `zone: ["battleArea","breeding"]`, `kind: ["Digimon","Tamer"]`, `nameOrTrait: [{tokens:["CS"], match:"trait"}]` | waiver from a Red CS Digimon in breeding; waiver from a Black CS Tamer in the battle area; negative with no CS card -> `color-requirement-unmet`, card stays in hand, memory untouched; near-trait negative (see Findings) |
| 2 `[Main]` | `Main` / `Return{to:"deckBottom"}` then `PlaceInBattleAreaSelf` | public `playCard`: named target instance is the **last** deck entry, deck length grows by 1, its digivolution card is in the owner's trash, the opponent's unsuspended Digimon and the player's own suspended Digimon are untouched, the Option is a permanent with `placedByEffect` (not in trash), hand empty, memory 5 -> 0, no pending decision |
| 2 "Then" | same | no suspended opposing Digimon -> deck identical, Option still placed |
| 3 `[Your Turn]` ＜Delay＞ | `YourTurn` / `SubTrigger{event:"whenAttacking"}` with CS `sourceFilter`, `keywords: [Delay]` | end-to-end through `startTurnLoop()`: play the Option on turn 1, survive the opponent's turn, re-suspend a target, attack with a CS Digimon on the next own turn -> target at deck bottom, Option trashed |
| 3 negatives | same | non-CS attacker -> no return, Option stays untrashed; near-trait attacker -> same; opponent's CS Digimon attacking on the opponent's turn -> `[Your Turn]` + `controller: "mine"` both block it; `autoDeclineOptional` -> deck unchanged, Option stays, no pending decision |
| 4 `[Security]` | `Security` / `isSecurity` + the same two actions | opponent attacks into a BT23-095 security card: the suspended attacker goes to the bottom of **its owner's** deck, the attacker's battle area empties, the Option is placed in the **defender's** battle area with `placedByEffect`, security empty, attack resolved |

```

  Test Files 1 passed (1)   Tests 14 passed (14)   Duration ~1.3s

```

#### Findings

**Defect fixed (module):** the module carried `// @ts-nocheck`, so type errors in the IR were
invisible. It is removed; the module now typechecks clean.

**Exactness proof for `[CS]`.** The printed wording is "with the [CS] trait", which is exact trait
matching (CR 2-3-2-3), not `traitContains`. Trait substring matching in
`apps/api/src/engine/cards/cardData.ts` is case-insensitive (`toLowerCase().includes`), so the
trait "Abadin Electronics" contains "cs". Two behavioral negatives pin this:

- `BT17-090 Tomonori Ryusenji` (Purple Tamer, only trait "Abadin Electronics") does **not** waive
  the green colour requirement.
- `BT16-039 Pulsemon` (trait "Abadin Electronics") attacking does **not** pay the Delay.

Red-then-green proof: flipping both `match: "trait"` entries to `"traitContains"` in
`BT23-095.ts` fails 4 tests (the two structural ones plus both near-trait negatives); the
committed `match: "trait"` passes all 14. The module was restored afterwards.

No IR defect found beyond the `@ts-nocheck`. The compiled clauses match the printed text:
zone-aware `youHave` for Q5383, `to: "deckBottom"` (not `deckTop`), `suspended: true` and
`controller: "opponent"` on every target, `PlaceInBattleAreaSelf` after each "Then" clause, and
`isSecurity: true` on the security effect.

#### Remaining gaps

- No test forces a **choice** between two legal suspended opposing Digimon; the targeting tests
  each present exactly one legal target, so target legality is proven but player choice is not.
- The Delay clause is not tested with **no** legal suspended target (does the Option still get
  trashed?). The printed text gives no explicit answer and the KB has no ruling on it, so this is
  left open rather than blessed by a test.
- No test covers the Option being returned to the battle area interacting with an opposing
  removal effect; out of scope for this card's printed text.
- No retained reds. No engine seam suspected for this card.

### BT23-096 — Comet Hammer

#### Card

Black Option, play cost 5, trait [CS], rarity C.

Printed clauses (catalog `effectText` / `securityEffectText`):

1. **Static color waiver** — "While you have a Digimon or Tamer with the [CS] trait on the field, you can ignore this card's color requirements."
2. **[Main]** — "＜De-Digivolve 4＞ 1 of your opponent's Digimon. Then, place this card in the battle area."
3. **[Your Turn] ＜Delay＞** — "When one of your [CS] trait Digimon attacks, ＜Delay＞ ・＜De-Digivolve 4＞ 1 of your opponent's Digimon."
4. **[Security]** — "＜De-Digivolve 4＞ 1 of your opponent's Digimon. Then, place this card in the battle area."

#### Rules used

- Q5384 (the card's only KB Q&A): "on the field" = battle area **or** breeding area.
- CR §16-12 ＜De-Digivolve＞: trash cards from the top of the chosen stack; mandatory once activated (§16-12-3); **can't trash from level 3 or lower** (§16-12-4).
- CR §16-17 ＜Delay＞: while the card is in the battle area, trashing it activates the effect; optional (§16-17-2); **can't be activated the turn the card entered the battle area** (§16-17-3).
- CR §2-3-2-3: "with the [CS] trait" is exact trait matching (`match: "trait"`), not `traitContains`.

#### Clause -> test -> IR mapping

| # | Clause / rule | Test | IR touched |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Catalog identity + full coverage + Q5384 zones + exact trait match + opponent-only target | "matches every catalog field and complete compiled clause" | whole `compiled` |
| 2 | Static waiver, CS Digimon in breeding (Q5384 breeding half) | "waives the black color requirement from an off-color CS Digimon in breeding" | `Static` / `WaiveColorRequirement` |
| 3 | Static waiver, CS **Tamer** in the battle area (Q5384 battle-area half, "Digimon or Tamer") | "waives the color requirement from a CS Tamer in the battle area" | same |
| 4 | Static waiver negative: no CS card on the field | "refuses the play when the only card on the field is off-color and lacks the [CS] trait" | same |
| 5 | [Main] end to end: cost 5 paid, De-Digivolve 4 on the opponent, self placed in the battle area | "plays for 5, de-digivolves the opposing stack to the level-3 floor and places itself in the battle area" | `Main` / `DeDigivolve` + `PlaceInBattleAreaSelf` |
| 6 | §16-12-4 level floor: peeling stops once a level-3 card is the top | same test (stack + trash assertions) | `DeDigivolve amount 4` |
| 7 | §16-17-3 same-turn refusal **and** next-own-turn reset, through the real turn loop | "cannot pay ＜Delay＞ the turn it is placed and can on the next own turn" | `YourTurn` SubTrigger + intrinsic delay gate |
| 8 | Delay arming filter: a non-CS attacker does not arm it | "does not pay Delay when a non-CS Digimon attacks" | SubTrigger `sourceFilter` |
| 9 | Delay controller boundary: only an opponent's Digimon is de-digivolved | "de-digivolves only the opponent's Digimon, never your own" | SubTrigger `DeDigivolve target.controller: opponent` |
| 10 | [Security] end to end: revealed in security, de-digivolves the attacker's stack, places itself in the security player's battle area | "resolves from security, de-digivolves the attacker and places itself in the battle area" | `Security` clause |

#### Findings

- **No IR defect found.** The compiled IR already matches the printed text: `match: "trait"` (exact, §2-3-2-3), `zone: ["battleArea", "breeding"]` (Q5384), `kind: ["Digimon", "Tamer"]`, `controller: "opponent"` on every De-Digivolve target, and ＜Delay＞ on the `[Your Turn]` window so `withIntrinsicDelayGate` installs the §16-17 trash cost and the §16-17-3 entry-turn gate. The only module change is removing `// @ts-nocheck`.
- **Prior test coverage was thin.** Four of the six existing tests were structural (`compiled.effects` shape) or leaned on hand-set `placedByEffect`. The [Main] and [Security] clauses had no behavioral proof at all, and the §16-17-3 entry-turn restriction — the only rule that governs when this card's Delay may be used — was untested.

##### Engine behaviour relied on

- `subTrigger.ts` ~1105: `delaySource.enterFieldTurnCount === subCtx.game.state.turnCount` implements §16-17-3.
- `primitives.ts` `deDigivolve` (~1795): peels from the top, `levelFloor = stopAtLevel ?? 3` implements §16-12-4.

##### Remaining gaps

- **Declared number not modelled (§16-12-1/§16-12-3).** The rules let the player declare any number from 1 to 4; `runAction` always passes the printed maximum to `fx.deDigivolve`. No prompt is opened. This is an engine-wide De-Digivolve simplification, not a BT23-096 defect, so it is reported, not patched here. It only matters when a player wants to stop a peel early (for example to leave a Digimon at level 4).

- **Fixed (local):** removed `// @ts-nocheck` from `apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-096.ts`. The module typechecks without it, and the suppression would have hidden a bad `duration:` string or a mistyped action if one were ever introduced (the BT23-047 failure mode).

|---|---|
| `pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec vitest run src/cards/BT23/BT23-096.test.ts --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism` | 1 file passed, **11 tests passed**, 8.51s (log: `logs/BT23-096-vitest.log`) |
| `pnpm --filter @aegis/api typecheck` | No `BT23-096` errors. Pre-existing failures in `src/cards/BT23/BT23-101.test.ts` and `src/engine/effects/immunityAffectation.test.ts` belong to other lanes. |
| `pnpm exec oxfmt --check` on both files | clean |
| `pnpm exec oxlint` on both files | 6 `no-explicit-any` warnings on IR-shape assertions, matching the accepted style in `BT23-030.test.ts` / `BT23-094.test.ts` |
| `git diff --check` | clean |

Test count went from 6 (4 of them structural) to 11, of which 8 are behavioral.

No retained reds, no skips, no catalog discrepancy: the catalog `effectText` and `securityEffectText` are asserted verbatim, non-breaking spaces included.

### BT23-097 — Seventh Penetration

#### Card summary

Purple Option, play cost 7, type [Seven Great Demon Lords]. Catalog record matches the official
text; no discrepancy found.

Printed clauses:

- **C1** `[Trash] [Your Turn] When any of your Digimon digivolve into [Belphemon (X Antibody)], by
  returning this card to the bottom of the deck, activate this card's [Main] effects.`
- **C2** `[Main] Delete 1 of your opponent's Digimon with a level as high or higher as the number of
  cards in your hand.`
- **C3** `[Security] Activate this card's [Main] effects.`

#### Q&A covered

| Id | Subject | Covered by |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Q5385 | A {Trash} effect can only trigger or activate while the card is in the trash | "stays silent while the copy is in the hand, not the trash (Q5385)" |
| Q5570 | C1 and the digivolution triggers are simultaneous; the player chooses the order | "offers the player the activation order against Belphemon's own trigger (Q5570)" |

#### Clause -> test -> IR mapping

| Clause | Behavioral tests | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| C1 trigger + cost | "returns itself under the deck and deletes a level 3 …", "leaves the level 3 alive and the card in the trash when the return is declined", "ignores an opponent's digivolution into Belphemon (X Antibody)", "does not trigger on a digivolution into Belphemon: Rage Mode", Q5385 test, Q5570 test | `YourTurn` effect, `isFromTrash: true`, `SubTrigger whenOneOfYoursDigivolves` with `sourceFilter.controllerDefault: "mine"` and `nameOrTrait [{ tokens: ["Belphemon (X Antibody)"], match: "nameExact" }]`, then `ActivateMain` with `optional`, `abortOnDecline` and `cost { kind: "return", to: "deckBottom", target.isSelf }` |
| C2 deletion + level floor | "deletes only an opponent Digimon whose level reaches the post-play hand size", "deletes nothing when every opponent Digimon is below the hand size …", "never reaches the controller's own Digimon" | `Main` effect, `Delete` count 1, filter `controller: "opponent"`, `kind: ["Digimon"]`, `levelComparison { op: "gte", value: 0, scaling { per: 1, unit: "cards", filter { controllerDefault: "mine", zone: "hand" } } }` |
| C3 security routing | "deletes an attacking-side Digimon that reaches the security player's hand size" | `Security` effect, `isSecurity: true`, `[{ kind: "ActivateMain" }]` |

Three IR-shape tests pin the compiled clauses so a silent recompile cannot drift the exact-name
gate, the opponent-only level floor, or the security routing.

##### Endpoints proven

- Cost: the card leaves the trash and lands at the **last** deck index (a three-card deck, not an
  empty one, so "bottom" is distinguishable from "top").
- Hand-size boundary: the Option is paid out of hand before the floor is read (5 in hand -> floor 4:
  the level 4 dies, the level 3 does not). The digivolution draw replaces the Belphemon card, so the
  C1 path resolves at the pre-intent hand size (3 -> the level 3 dies).
- Isolation from BT23-070's own `[When Digivolving]` "delete all with the highest level": every C1
  fixture gives the opponent a highest-level Digimon **plus** a lower one. Belphemon can only reach
  the former, so the latter's fate is attributable to BT23-097 alone. The declined run is the
  A/B control: the level 5 still dies, the level 3 lives.
- Controller boundaries: an opponent's digivolution does not trigger C1; C2 never offers the
  controller's own Digimon; from security, "your opponent" resolves to the attacking side and the
  floor is read from the **security player's** hand.
- Memory: 7 -> 0 on every play-from-hand run.
- No `actionRejected` / unsupported-effect event on any behavioral test (`assertNoLoudGap`).

#### Defect found and fixed

`apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-097.ts` used `match: "name"` for the bracketed card name
`[Belphemon (X Antibody)]`. `matchNameOrTrait` (`engine/effects/interpreter/matching/definition.ts`
~350) treats `"name"` as **substring** and `"nameExact"` as equality; a bracketed printed name is an
exact reference. Changed to `nameExact`.

Honest caveat: no released card's normalized name contains "belphemon x antibody" as a proper
substring (the catalog holds only `Belphemon: Sleep Mode`, `Belphemon: Rage Mode` and
`Belphemon (X Antibody)`), so this fix cannot be proven by a failing-then-passing test today. The
near-name control ("does not trigger on a digivolution into Belphemon: Rage Mode") passes under both
spellings; it is a regression guard, not proof. The green C1 tests do prove `nameExact` still fires
on the real card.

Also removed the module's `// @ts-nocheck`: the file typechecks without it, so future IR errors now
surface (per the brief's BT23-047 lesson).

#### Commands and results

```
pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec vitest run src/cards/BT23/BT23-097.test.ts --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism
  Test Files  1 passed (1)      Tests  14 passed (14)      Duration ~0.8s
pnpm --filter @aegis/api typecheck        # no BT23-097 errors (pre-existing failures in BT23-093/095/098 and engine/combat/controller.ts belong to other lanes)
pnpm exec oxlint  apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-097.ts apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-097.test.ts   # clean
pnpm exec oxfmt   apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-097.ts apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-097.test.ts   # formatted
```

Log: `docs/audits/BT23-reaudit/logs/BT23-097-vitest.log`.

#### Retained reds / skips

None. No engine seam was hit.

#### Remaining gaps

- The exact-name fix is unprovable behaviorally until a card named with `Belphemon (X Antibody)` as
  a prefix exists (see above).
- C1 is not once-per-turn and has no duration, so there is no reset or expiry boundary to prove.
- Not exercised: two copies of BT23-097 in the trash on one digivolution (the second copy's own
  ordering entry). The Q5570 test proves the ordering decision exists and is answerable; the
  multi-copy case is a resolver concern, not a card-module one.
- The prior test file asserted `deck[0]` for a deck-bottom return against an empty deck, and read
  both opponent deletions as this card's work when one of them was BT23-070's. Both were replaced.

### BT23-098 — Unique Emblem: Soul Banquet

#### Card

Purple Option, play cost 3, type LIBERATOR, rarity C. Catalog text (`packages/shared/src/cards/data/cards.json`) matches the official card list; no discrepancy found.

Printed clauses:

1. `[Main] You may play 1 [Ghostmon] or [Violet Inboots] from your hand or trash without paying the cost.`
2. `Then, place this card in the battle area.`
3. `[Your Turn] When any of your [Violet Inboots] suspend, ＜Delay＞ ・1 of your Digimon with the [Ghost] trait may digivolve into a Digimon card with the [Ghost] trait and [LIBERATOR] in the hand with the digivolution cost reduced by 3.`
4. `[Security] Activate this card's [Main] effects.`

Rulings: Q5386 (2025-10-03) — the ＜Delay＞ target may only digivolve into a card that has **both** the [Ghost] and [LIBERATOR] traits, not either one.

#### Clause -> test -> IR map

| Clause | Test | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 (hand) | plays a [Ghostmon] from hand for free and then places itself in the battle area | `PlayWithoutCost` `from: ["hand","trash"]`, `payCost: false`, `nameExact` tokens |
| 1 (trash) | plays a [Violet Inboots] out of the trash for free | same action, trash branch |
| 1 (optional) | still places itself when the controller declines the free play | `optional: true`, no `abortOnDecline` |
| 1 (name gate) | does not offer a non-[Ghostmon]/[Violet Inboots] purple card for the free play | `match: "nameExact"` |
| 2 | every [Main] test above asserts the Option permanent with `placedByEffect` | `PlaceInBattleAreaSelf` |
| 3 (＜Delay＞ gate) | cannot pay its ＜Delay＞ on the turn it was placed | keyword `Delay`, `enterFieldTurnCount` gate |
| 3 (gate reset) | pays its ＜Delay＞ on the next own turn through the real turn loop | same, via `startTurnLoop()` |
| 3 (trigger + cost) | trashes itself for ＜Delay＞ and digivolves a Ghost with the cost reduced by exactly 3 | `SubTrigger whenSuspended`, `Digivolve payCost: true, reduceCost: 3` |
| 3 (Q5386) | does not pay ＜Delay＞ when the hand only holds Ghost-without-LIBERATOR or LIBERATOR-without-Ghost cards | `into.and: [Ghost trait, LIBERATOR trait]` |
| 3 (refusal) | keeps itself in the battle area when the controller declines ＜Delay＞ | `optional: true` on the Digivolve |
| 3 (controller) | ignores an opponent-controlled Violet Inboots suspension | `sourceFilter.controller: "mine"` |
| 4 | runs the full [Main] effect from a real security check | `isSecurity`, `ActivateMain` |
| all | matches every catalog field / compiles the printed clauses into the expected IR | structural |

#### Defects found and fixed (both local, proven red then green)

1. **The ＜Delay＞ digivolution was free.** The compiled `Digivolve` action carried `reduceCost: 3` but no `payCost`. `apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/actions/digivolve.ts:247` reads `const pays = action.payCost === true`, so the omission made the whole digivolution cost 0 instead of the printed cost minus 3. Red: the cost test read memory 3 where 2 was due. Fixed by adding `payCost: true`; the same test is now green and pins the exact remainder (Necromon cost 4 - 3 = 1).
2. **Substring name matching.** The previous author had already switched both `[Ghostmon]` / `[Violet Inboots]` filters from `match: "name"` to `match: "nameExact"` and removed `// @ts-nocheck`; that change is kept and pinned by the IR test. It is not behaviorally observable — the catalog holds no card whose name contains "Ghostmon" or "Violet Inboots" as a proper substring — so the proof is structural only.

#### Fixture corrections to the partial work

- Four `[Main]` tests played the Option with no colour source and died on `color-requirement-unmet` (CR 4-21-2, `GameEngine.printedColorRequirementMet`). A purple Digi-Egg (BT11-006) in breeding is now the legal colour source.
- The "declines ＜Delay＞" test asserted that Violet Inboots suspends under `autoDeclineOptional`. It cannot: Violet Inboots suspends itself through its own optional `[Your Turn]` cost, which that flag refuses. The test now answers each prompt by hand (accept the suspension, refuse the ＜Delay＞) and asserts the prompt text of each.

#### Commands and results

- `cd apps/api && ./node_modules/.bin/vitest run src/cards/BT23/BT23-098.test.ts --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism` — 1 file, 13 tests passed, 16.97s. Log: `logs/BT23-098-focused.log`. (Run the binary directly: the `rtk` proxy on `pnpm exec vitest` swallows the reporter output and its JSON report lags one run.)
- `cd apps/api && ./node_modules/.bin/tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` — no errors.
- `pnpm exec oxlint` and `pnpm exec oxfmt --check` on both files — clean.
- `git diff --check` — clean.

#### Remaining gaps

- **`[Your Turn]` boundary is untested.** No public route suspends your own Violet Inboots during the opponent's turn — its own suspension cost is `[Your Turn]` too — so "own Violet suspends on the opponent's turn does not fire the ＜Delay＞" has no legal fixture. The opponent-controller case is covered instead.
- **Same-permanent ＜Delay＞.** When the only Ghost is the permanent that is mid-digivolution (the digivolve that suspended Violet Inboots), the effect-driven digivolve resolves to nothing. Not reproduced as a red test: no ruling was found either way, so it is reported rather than blessed. Suspected seam: the target permanent is still inside `digivolveFromInstance`'s own resolution window when the nested ＜Delay＞ runs.
- The free-play clause is not proven against a *deck*-zone candidate or an illegal zone; the printed zones are hand and trash and both are covered.
